The name set is the game-designer's (`TL_STRAINED`, `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_MAX`); the gain and relax names above are added so the Tuning Knobs table has one set, and the game-designer's per-band rate knobs are retired.)* *Rejected.* A continuous temperature-to-speed penalty (the Controller: "no continuous speed penalty"); a visible temperature number. *Criterion.* AC-SE-22: a twenty-minute soak in a FROST fixture ends at STRAINED with cap FULL; a fixture past the shifted onset reaches CRITICAL no sooner than `HOLD_12` runs and FAILED no sooner than `HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(warmth)` runs. *Pillar.* P1, P2.

**SE-21. Insulation.** *Rule.* One Suit-owned `INSUL_MC` **5000 mC** `[PH]` (range 0-10000). The effective cold onset is `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC − insul_eff_mc`, with `insul_eff_mc = INSUL_MC × min(integrity_bp, SUIT_LEAK_HP) / SUIT_LEAK_HP` (a *leaking* suit insulates less: the coupling is anchored at the existing Pillar 1 witness, the seam jet, so insulation is full at and above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, integrity STRAINED has no thermal effect, and the lethal cold onset never moves while the only witness is a lie-capable reading; CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26, option (a); linear below the knee, int32-safe as `INSUL_MC` ≤ 10000 times `SUIT_LEAK_HP` ≤ 6000). A patched suit (`SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` is above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`) insulates fully again and stays permanently STRAINED: the scar is a mark, not a penalty (SE-22). **No heat shift** in the MVP (heat is derelict B's hazard, not a loadout puzzle). There is no per-item insulation (no progression). The Suit source contains none of the four band literals. *Exported to Derelict Authoring:* with the shift, an unpowered home-ship never reaches the shifted onset inside a 30-minute session (the Graph's SHIP-COLD fixture needs about 40 minutes to reach the shifted value), so cold damage lands only in a room whose equilibrium temperature is below the shifted onset — any cold-hazard derelict room must be authored that way, or `INSUL_MC` lowered. *Why.* One suit, no progression (the Emitter State's "no light progression" generalised). *Rejected.* Per-item insulation; none; the previous knee at `SUIT_HP_OK`, which moved the lethal cold onset with no body witness (the hidden stat the Pillar 1 test forbids). *Criterion.* AC-SE-1 (literal lint) and AC-SE-22. *Pillar.* P1.

**SE-22. Suit integrity.** *Rule.* `integrity_bp` runs on the Graph's basis-point **scale** (0 to `SUIT_HP_FULL` 10000) with Suit's own symbols **`SUIT_HP_OK`, `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, `SUIT_HP_FAIL`, `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP`** `[PH]` — never the Graph symbols `HP_OK` and `HP_FAIL` as Suit thresholds. Build relations: `SUIT_HP_FAIL < SUIT_LEAK_HP < SUIT_HP_OK`; `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP < SUIT_HP_OK` and above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`. Damage sources: (1) SCORCH-band burn, `SCORCH_BURN_BP_PER_RUN` per run (endogenous); (2) arc: when `failures(filter)` returns a mode 2 SHORT descriptor at its top severity in the occupant's compartment and the seat is within `ARC_RADIUS_PX` of the descriptor's anchor (LS-14), `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN` per run (endogenous; its 10-30 s tell is the Graph's overload pre-stage); (3) **`apply_suit_damage(player_id, amount_bp, source_ref)`** (Class B, clamp) from Interaction's `begin_harm` and from Horror — each exogenous source carries its own tell. Below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` the suit **leaks**: the reserve drains an extra `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` every run, whatever the source, and a seam vapour jet is drawn on the sprite — the Pillar 1 "visibly leaking pipe" applied to a body (a leak that only cost air in vacuum would be a hidden stat until the worst moment; the stated side effect is that a leaking suit can reach reserve 0 in a safe room, and the reserve reading and the jet are the witnesses). **Leak witness timing (HUD & Suit Layer OQ-HS-60):** every seam leak carries a body cue — the seam jet (drawn) and the seam hiss (audio) — from the run `integrity_bp` first reads below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, which is the run of, or the run before, the first leak debit (SE-39 step 5); no leak debit is ever unwitnessed, so no margin constant (`RESERVE_LEAK_ERR_MARGIN_S`) is minted. Whether the reserve reading should model the leak is the HUD's open question (OQ-HS-58); the jet is an honest check: the Graph's consequence-channel list carries it (OQ-9 answered, registry `truth_channel_union`). Integrity cues are in the States rung table. **Integrity STRAINED is inert (CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26):** between `SUIT_LEAK_HP` and `SUIT_HP_OK` the cap is FULL, nothing drains and insulation is full (SE-21), so the rung has no body cue by design and is carried by the integrity reading's zone band alone; every integrity consequence (the leak drain, the insulation loss, WALK_ONLY) begins at or below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, on the run the seam jet is drawn. *The floor also holds measured from the jet:* the endogenous integrity paths take at least `HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(integrity)` runs from the first jet run to FAILED (A7 gives the arc `(SUIT_LEAK_HP − SUIT_HP_FAIL) / ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN ≥ HOLD_12` runs to cross `SUIT_HP_FAIL`, then `HOLD_23(integrity)`; A3 bounds the sum by `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`): 225 runs (22.5 s) for the arc at the defaults, exactly 200 runs (20 s) at the top of the `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN` range, 724 runs (72.4 s) for the SCORCH burn. **Repair:** the self-patch calls `patch_integrity(player_id, bp)`, capped at `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` — a patched suit never returns to OK, mirroring the Graph's patch cap; patches have no decay in the MVP. Interaction hosts the self-patch as a `begin_patch` declaration on object class ACTOR_SELF (IA-17), calling `patch_integrity`; the no-repair fallback is retired; a passive mend is rejected (it erases attrition and is a hidden regeneration). Integrity may be **data-dormant** at the MVP (no guaranteed SCORCH room, Horror harm undefined, friendly harm Vertical Slice B): specify the model and the mutators, do not over-tune. *Why.* A legible physical landing for harm, SCORCH and decompression, and a repair that is the same welder as everything else. *Rejected.* No integrity at all (contradicts the art bible's HR-3 and leaves harm nowhere to land); a passive mend. *Criterion.* AC-SE-23 (D.7 examples; the leak flag and the jet cue at the same run). *Pillar.* P1, anti-pillar not a combat game (a landing, not a stat).

**SE-23. The limiter applies to endogenous sources; an exogenous hit sets the rung from `integrity_bp` with no hold.** *Rule.* Endogenous damage (SCORCH burn, arc) runs through the SE-17 ladder. An `apply_suit_damage` whose `source_ref` is classed EXOGENOUS (Horror, Interaction `begin_harm`) sets `exo_pending`; on the next run, while `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` is 1 (the default), the integrity rung is set to the threshold band of the new `integrity_bp` with no hold — STRAINED below `SUIT_HP_OK`, CRITICAL (the Controller's stagger on the falling edge, then walk-only) below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, FAILED (cap NONE) below `SUIT_HP_FAIL` — then `exo_pending` clears (diagnostic `suit_exo_bypass`). The flag is consumed on the next run whatever the knob: at knob 0 it clears with no effect, so a later data flip never fires a stale bypass. Interaction's harm lands per step, so the victim's rung tracks the damage inside the channel. Grounds: R19; LS-13 (exogenous causes bypass the gate; every non-player exogenous source carries its own 10-30 s tell, exported to Horror Entities); the user's decision that death is lethal including friendly fire. With the knob at 0 the uniform limiter applies to every source (the previous default; a data flip). *(checkpoint ruling CK-2/CK-3; a stagger at the first band was declined: the cap is FULL at STRAINED and a cap-free stagger would need the `SYSTEM` stagger command, SE-35, which Suit submits only on the D.12 two-band pressure drop; Interaction's designed IA-31 withdraws that request.)* *(Interaction's designed IA-31, D.8, Edge Cases E and Open Question 3 state default 1; the former CONFLICT is closed, 2026-10-27. The creative-director may reverse by data.)* *Why.* P2 for endogenous paths, the user's lethality decision for exogenous ones; the Graph splits endogenous and exogenous causes on M18 the same way. *Rejected.* A bypass for endogenous sources (it would erase the tell floor on SCORCH and arcs); a uniform limiter as the default (a completed friendly-harm act would leave the victim STRAINED with a FULL cap for at least `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`, contradicting the user's lethality decision). *Criterion.* AC-SE-24: with the knob 1, after each exogenous apply the rung equals the threshold band of `integrity_bp` on the next run; with the knob 0, a `HARM_TOTAL_BP` sequence reaches FAILED no sooner than the SE-17 floor. *Pillar.* P2 (and the user's binding lethality decision).

**SE-24. Death is Suit's rule — what it decides.** *Rule.* Suit decides (i) what drives the cap to NONE — any axis FAILED; (ii) recovery while DOWNED — the cap rises on the very run an axis leaves FAILED (SE-17 descent: the room regains air or warmth, a crewmate tops up in Vertical Slice B), with **no timer extension and no self-revive**; and (iii) its own tell floor (SE-18). Suit does **not** own `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` 1800 or `STAGGER_TICKS` 30 or locomotion (the Controller's), nor the restore (Save/Load SL-7 and SL-8: restart from the newest verified generation; WORLD blocks including `suit.state` roll back; "no beat, no lights-out, no death screen"). A DOWNED seat keeps breathing and keeps draining (DOWNED is not a Suit pause); **a DOWNED seat's beam stays lit (draining; `force_off` only at zero or at DEAD, where the Emitter State forces it off) — this answers Lighting's LO-28 locator ask, and Lighting may repeat it**; Suit never writes the beam's aim (the aim is the Controller's `set_aim`, its placement the Emitter State's), so whether the beam holds its last committed aim while the seat cannot act is the Controller's answer (Lighting Open Question 14); a DEAD seat **freezes**: no draw, no drain, no rung evaluation, and the beam is forced off by the Emitter State. `revive(player_id)` is declared and dormant (Vertical Slice B, Interaction's `begin_revive`). Suit recomputes the cap every run while DOWNED (no divisor change; the Controller's `PC_DOWNED_RECOVERED` histogram clustering at 0-5 ticks is the trigger to revisit a divisor-1 DOWNED mode). Because Suit runs before `controller.step`, a cap raised on the `death_tick` run is honoured: the Controller checks the cap first, then death. *Why.* The Graph hands the body to Suit and the Controller hands the window to the Controller; this is the seam between them. *Rejected.* A Suit-owned death timer; a mercy extension. *Criterion.* AC-SE-25 (recovery on the exact `death_tick` is honoured; a DEAD seat's block is unchanged by further runs). *Pillar.* P2, P5.

**SE-25. Body cues are a pure function of true state.** *Rule.* Every body cue is a function of the occupant's compartment band enums and `suit.state` only. Suit code never reads Sanity, Attention or any `PERCEPTION_OUT` view (CR-12); the read views return true values only; the block holds no believed or displayed field (the Item DB's forbidden-key ban applied here). The Perception Filter may neither remove nor add to a body cue, move the chest point, or edit overlay visibility. *Why.* The consequence channel is closed. *Rejected.* A cue driven by anything the player's sanity could bend. *Criterion.* AC-SE-26: a cue-purity lint (no import of a Sanity, Attention or `PERCEPTION_OUT` symbol) and a replay with a mutated sanity block that yields identical cue outputs. *Pillar.* P1, P4.

**SE-26. Closed-list classification of every cue.** *Rule.* The Graph's closed consequence-channel list names Suit body cues "breath, visor fog, vignette, shiver". Per the Item DB's rule, a cue that is neither on the list nor an anchor is module-body, corruptible, and cannot serve as an honest check; Suit therefore states the channel of each:

| Cue | Channel class | Driving true value |
|---|---|---|
| Breath cadence (thin, closed-reserve, load steps) and the exhale vapour puff | closed list: breath | `band_o2`, `band_p`, breath source, rung; `band_t` for the puff; `cue_load` |
| **Reserve valve click** (engage and disengage) and the **pack-creak load layer** | the breath family (breath-source and breath-load sounds) | change of breath source; `cue_load` |
| Visor fog (two levels) | closed list: visor fog | breath rung |
| Shiver (idle) | closed list: shiver | warmth rung, cold side |
| **Rime** on shell plates (never at a fitting), **seam vapour jet**, **HEAVY back-frame**, **stagger / walk-only / DOWNED** | on-sprite anchors (art bible §5.3: "anything drawn on it is true on every client"); **routed amendment** to the Graph's closed list | warmth rung (cold); `integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`; the stowed class; `locomotion_cap` |
| Cell whine and the warning marks | **readings** — the audio and visual signature of `true_value(suit.charge)` and of the reading a mark sits beside; lie-capable by architecture, uncorrupted in the MVP | `cell_ticks`; the reading's own value |
| The beam going dark | physical result (Emitter State) | `force_off` at zero cell |

*(CONFLICT C1, closed 2026-10-27: the Graph's LS-14 consequence-channel list now carries Suit SE-27's set — rime, stagger / walk-only / DOWNED, the seam vapour jet and the HEAVY back-frame (registry `truth_channel_union`) — so these four are consequence-channel cues, not corruptible module-body; the creative-director ratified the amendment's content — every on-sprite anchor in VA-1 and VA-5 to VA-9 belongs on the consequence channel. Heat-side sweat/shimmer is **not** a cue — the art-director ruled no heat body cue, so the heat side is carried by the reading, the room's scorch trail, the audio and SCORCH's integrity damage.)* *Why.* Without the classification, an off-list cue would silently be corruptible. *Criterion.* AC-SE-27 (inspection: every player-facing value in Visual/Audio and UI Requirements is tagged cue or reading). *Pillar.* P1, P4.

**SE-27. Vignette, tunnel and "confusion" are not implemented.** *Rule.* The Graph's list and band descriptions name a vignette, "mild vision tunnel" (THIN) and "confusion cues" (HYPOXIC); the art bible forbids a vignette or any mask between a lit pixel and the screen (Principle 1) and budgets no screen-space vital overlay, and the art-director rejects a HUD-edge tint as a mask by other means. The standing authority is the art bible, and a closed list is a maximum, so using fewer is legal. **The MVP cue set is: breath (audio and vapour puff), visor fog, rime, shiver, the reserve valve click, the load layer, the seam jet, the HEAVY back-frame, stagger and walk-only.** No screen effect, no blur, no false state from hypoxia (a lie from the wrong source: Pillar 4 reserves lies for Sanity). LOW's "ear pressure" gets no new cue type: its witnesses are the draught (Failure & Trail's AIRFLOW row) and the Graph's hiss at the opening; DECOMP's witness is the reserve valve click plus breath. *(CONFLICT, licensed by the Graph's column header "Felt as (Suit owns the exact effect)"; **the vignette drop is ratified by the creative-director, CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26**: a screen-space mask between a lit pixel and the player is indistinguishable in kind from a Sanity presentation effect; sweeps owed in Cross-References.)* *Criterion.* AC-SE-28 (screenshot and review: no screen-space body effect in any state). *Pillar.* P1 (the design test "mask versus true occlusion → occlusion").

**SE-28. The mass cue is breath with a load signature.** *Rule.* `cue_load = max(carried_class, stowed_class)`, with the classes from the registry's `weight_class` formula (LIGHT 0, MEDIUM 1, HEAVY 2) — **tools excluded**: the in-hand tools are heavy in the start kit and would pin the baseline at MEDIUM and make LIGHT unreachable (`held_mass_g` remains a read view for diagnostics and the Vertical Slice A field check only). `carried_class` is the class of the carried instance (LIGHT when none); `stowed_class` is the class of `stowed_mass_g`, counted only if it reaches the knob **`STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS`** (default MEDIUM, so every class counts; set to HEAVY for the reading in which only a stowed mass at or above `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` — the 30 kg `part_hull_plate` — sounds). The binding trigger from the Item DB is met either way: a stowed mass at or above `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` always produces the HEAVY step. *(orchestrator ruling: the stage ruling's prose says the stowed term "fires at `stowed_mass_g ≥ WEIGHT_HEAVY_G`" while its expression is `max(class(carried), class(stowed_mass_g))`; they differ for parts totalling a MEDIUM class — 15 kg of parts is silent under the prose and a MEDIUM step under the expression. The expression is adopted because it resolves all three classes, as the Item DB requires, and the knob makes the prose reading a data-only change. The cost is stated: a stowed HEAVY masks every carried class — the field check is blind to a carried module while the plate is on the back — and the Item DB's junk-versus-lure gap must fall in different classes.)* The cue is **breath**: load = a deeper breath at the air class's own rate plus a pack-creak layer and a footfall-linked exhale; thin air = shallower, faster, no creak; the two are separable by cadence *direction*, and breath RATE is driven only by the air class — the load signature is two additional layers that never change the rate, so a HEAVY load in THIN air is rate-up plus creak and the two cannot cancel at baseline. Three steps: LIGHT baseline, MEDIUM +1, HEAVY +2. Mass never changes speed or noise. *(Boarding & Salvage and Sanity must repeat: the VS-A field check on a carried item is valid only while the stowed class is below the carried class under test — drop the plate first; the knob `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` and this precondition are OQ-8, ruled at checkpoint CK-8.)* *Rejected carriers.* Shiver (cold), visor fog (bad air), a vignette (SE-27), pack-breathing amplitude (not on the closed list). *Risk RK-2 stands:* a blind two-clip audio test (load versus thin air, at least 90 % identification) is a precondition for the Vertical Slice A phantom-salvage check; the MVP exposure is the plate and a carried module. *Criterion.* AC-SE-29 (the D.6 table; the manual blind audio test, a spike). *Pillar.* P1, P4.

**SE-29. Readings versus cues; lie-capable architecture, honest MVP.** *Rule.* Every HR-3 reading reaches the HUD only through the Perception Filter's **`perceived`**, which wraps **`true_value(key, player_id)`**, with keys **`suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`**. The architecture is lie-capable (the reading is drawn in the reading register with the art bible's HR-5 tri-modal tell), but **no reading is corrupted in the MVP** — the MVP's single class (b) hallucination lives on ship instruments. Body cues are never corruptible regardless. A lying numeral is disproved by a crewmate's visor or by breath cadence, the costly check; Pillar 2 protects a first-time player's only death clock until the cue ladder is playtested. Suppressant and glowstick counts beside the rail glyphs are **anchors**, not readings: honest at MVP and Vertical Slice A, with no `true_value` key (HUD HS-34, Sanity SN-19; OQ-6 closed). **The warmth reading's value.** `true_value(suit.warmth, player_id)` is a cold-to-hot permille of the thermal load, `warmth_pm = (500 × (thermal_load + TL_MAX)) / TL_MAX` (integer division of a non-negative numerator; 0..1000; COMFORT, `thermal_load` 0, at the centre 500; cold below), carried by the Filter as the `Perceived` displayed permille; it is a load, not a body temperature (no temperature number exists, SE-20). Its six band edges are the images of −`TL_MAX`, −`TL_CRITICAL`, −`TL_STRAINED`, +`TL_STRAINED`, +`TL_CRITICAL`, +`TL_MAX`: **0, 333, 466, 533, 666, 1000** at the defaults `[PH]`, so the zone band matches the load thresholds of the warmth rung (SE-20); a band-driven STRAINED (FROST with no load yet) leaves the reading in its centre zone — the body is ahead of the number (Player Fantasy). The HUD's equal spacing is replaced by these edges (HUD & Suit Layer OQ-HS-9). Must be repeated by the HUD & Suit Layer, Sanity & Hallucination Rules and the Perception Filter. *Rejected.* Corrupted-in-MVP (a lying O2 numeral on an untested death clock); never lie-capable (loses the "my numeral says warm, my visor is fogged" teaching moment). *Criterion.* AC-SE-30: a stub Filter wrapping `true_value` changes the HUD value and no body cue. *Pillar.* P4, P2.

**SE-30. The O2 numeral, the reserve reading and the hand rail.** *Rule.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: when the reserve engages it becomes the death clock, but it sat on the corner panel while the strip kept showing a room `p_o2` of 0 in cyan with the player breathing fine, and the breath warning mark changed position with the breath source.)* The two O2 readings are named **ambient O2** (`suit.o2`: the **suit-sensed `p_o2` (dkPa) of the occupant's compartment** — "the suit's reading of compartment atmosphere via Suit, not a Graph overlay") and **suit O2** (`suit.reserve`, seconds or permille; the HUD picks the format; it has its own glyph on the suit panel). **The critical strip's breath dial (arc and numeral) shows the suit O2 while the breath source is RESERVE, and the ambient O2 in every other source**; while it shows the suit O2 the ambient numeral is demoted to the suit panel (the HUD places it, OQ-41). **Each axis has one mark anchor:** the breath axis's warning mark always sits on the strip's breath dial when the breath axis is CRITICAL, whichever reading the dial is showing and whichever the breath source is (GASP, NONE or RESERVE); it no longer moves between the reserve glyph and the O2 reading. This adds a reading to HR-3's set — an art-bible amendment routed to the art-director (accepted by the art-director; amendment text owed). **The mark is rung-based, not condition-based**: it fires only at the CRITICAL rung of an axis (after the hold, so a transient never shows it) and at `CELL_WARN_S` on the charge reading, never at STRAINED; this keeps it rare. The mark inherits the class of the reading it sits beside — it lies when the reading lies and is **not** an independent honest backup; the honest second opinion is always the body and the room. **Hand rail** (the Item DB's OQ 6 confirmed): beside each tool, the suppressant charges 0-3, the glowstick count 0-4, the carried-module glyph, and the equipped tool in the Tungsten fixture colour; **no weight readout, no inventory screen, no per-part counts** (a part count is read by Interaction's repair prompt). *Criterion.* AC-SE-31 (retained screenshots; UI BLOCKING). *Pillar.* P1, P4.

#### Group F — Seams

**SE-31. No stimuli pushed.** *Rule.* Suit pushes no noise or power stimuli to Attention in the MVP. Per-verb noise class lives in **Interaction's verb declarations**; Suit keeps no noise field of its own (the earlier idea of a Suit-side noise table is withdrawn so there is one table of record). The kernel's Interactions row 4 says Suit "pushes noise/power stimuli to Attention": that wording is a routed refresh (Cross-References). The top-up is a Graph stock withdrawal; whether Attention sees it is not relied on here. *Criterion.* AC-SE-32 (no call from Suit's code to an Attention intake). *Pillar.* P3.

**SE-32. Pause.** *Rule.* Nothing Suit registers carries `allowed_when_paused`; `equip_tool` and every mutator are rejected while paused. The Chart Table's beam-shape pick is a boot or new-game call, not a runtime command. *Rejected.* Paused equip (lets a solo player re-arm with time stopped mid-tell, against Pillar 2). *Criterion.* AC-SE-33. *Pillar.* P2.

**SE-33. Autosave seam (routed to Save/Load).** *Rule.* Save/Load's hard gate stops only at DOWNED or DEAD, so an autosave can capture a seat seconds from FAILED; the death restore then restores that doomed generation, and the step-back fires only for RISKY generations. Proposal: a soft gate input "any bound seat `vitals_state ≥ CRITICAL` defers an autosave", capped at `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (registry 7200) and then written RISKY; invisible to the player (no glyph). Suit provides `view(player_id).vitals_state`. An optional refinement — a derived `time_to_failure_runs` read — would also catch a STRAINED-yet-doomed seat (31 s of reserve and a 40 s exit) that the predicate misses; it is not among the SE-40 read names and is carried as an open question, not minted here. A Save/Load amendment, not Suit's edit, and a **USER DECISION** (Save/Load Open Question 40, Suit OQ-13) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: under the binding rule "MVP death = restart from the last autosave" an autosave that captures a seat at CRITICAL can produce a repeat-death loop. The creative-director recommends adding "any bound seat `vitals_state ≥ CRITICAL`" to the soft gate, capped by `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS`; Save/Load's recommended package (its OQ-40 (a)-(c)) already reads Suit's `view(player_id).vitals_state` for it. A second route into the same state, a data retune making every saved restore fail, is closed on Suit's side by `data_rev` (SE-34, AC-SE-49). *Criterion.* AC-SE-34a and 34b. *Pillar.* P5.

**SE-34. Save, restore, derived values.** *Rule.* SL-15 items 1-12 apply: `supported_versions`; a plain-data `snapshot()` (no `Vector2i`, no `StringName` keys, no typed containers); a two-phase `restore` returning `(ok, reason, detail)`; **hard failure, never clamp** on an out-of-range field *(except the declared `data_rev` migration below)*; `default_state()`; `post_restore` sets every `prev_*` field to its current value (no spurious edge cue after a restore); explicit dirty keys (4.7 packed-array rule); restore-idempotent (`snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` byte-equal); PER_PLAYER keyed by PlayerId. The closed restore reasons are in SE-38. **Data revisions** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the block persists a block-level `data_rev` (the revision of `assets/data/suit/*.json`, bumped by hand for any retune of `R_BREATH`, `RESERVE_S`, `CELL_S`, `TL_MAX`, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS`, `HYPOX_FAIL_S`, `INTEG_FAIL_S` or `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS`), and `assets/data/suit/rev_history.json` keeps, for each past revision, the saved values of `R_BREATH`, `RESERVE_S`, `CELL_S`, `TL_MAX` and the derived hold maxima. A saved `data_rev` equal to the current one is restored strictly (an out-of-range field is `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`, a defect or tampering). A saved `data_rev` **older** than the current and present in the history is a legitimate retune and is **rescaled, not failed**: `reserve_mgu` and `cell_ticks` keep their felt fraction (`new = floor(saved × RESERVE_MAX_now / RESERVE_MAX_saved)`, likewise `CELL_MAX_TICKS`), `thermal_load` and every `hold`, `rf_warmth` and `p_age` are reduced to the new maxima (the safe direction: a shorter counter only lengthens the tell), the diagnostic `suit_restore_rescaled` is emitted, and the restore succeeds. A saved `data_rev` newer than the build, or absent from the history, fails `SUIT_RESTORE_DATA_REV_UNKNOWN`. Without this path a data retune made every saved restore fail and left the player with no loadable save at the moment of death (the doomed-save risk of OQ-13's second route). The rescale is a declared migration, not a clamp; Save/Load confirms it is within SL-15 (OQ-45). Derived values — `locomotion_cap`, `vitals_state`, cue levels, `held_mass_g`, `stowed_mass_g`, `cue_load`, the breath source — are computed on read and never saved. MVP death is restart from the last autosave: `suit.state` rolls back with the world and there is no PROFILE block in the MVP. *Criterion.* AC-SE-35 (SL-15 round trip, restore idempotence). *Pillar.* P5.

**SE-35. External stagger, DECOMP pull, pod.** *Stagger.* The Controller registers the `SYSTEM`-origin command type `stagger {actor}` (registry `stagger`; Player Controller PC-1 b: `coalesce NONE`, `max_age_ticks` 120, no player binding, rejected `NOT_STAGGERABLE` in DOWNED, DEAD or IN_POD, Applied without extending the lock in STAGGERED); the former CONFLICT is closed, 2026-10-27. Suit submits one on a two-band pressure drop inside `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` `[PH]` (live at MVP), evaluated by **D.12** (the one normative statement of the trigger): the occupant's `band_p` index (NORMAL 0, LOW 1, DECOMP 2, VACUUM 3) must rise by 2 or more between a sample taken at most `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` earlier and the current run, **in the same compartment**. A compartment change (a door crossing, the SE-3 NONE-to-EXPOSED switch, leaving IN_POD, the first run after a bind) re-anchors the window and never counts, so a player who walks into a vented room by choice is not input-locked for it, while a pressure fall inside the room they stand in is; one stagger per event; the two window ages are persisted (SE-38) so a restore mid-window replays the same trigger; the DECOMP cue plays as well, and a cap falling edge still staggers through the Controller's own rule. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *Pull.* Cue only (a lean, draught audio, dust); no displacement ("a stagger is animation plus input lock, never an impulse"). *Pod.* The pod's Graph section is dormant and is never breathed (Web Map & Transit WM-17): while the Controller's `loco_state` is IN_POD, Suit holds the reserve and the cell constant for the hop, makes **no `draw_air` call**, and the thermal load and the hold counters do not advance because no band is read (the IN_POD branch of SE-3, HOLD_ROOM); `breath_starved` stays 0 and no GASP cue fires across a leg. A NONE with `loco_state` not IN_POD is not a hold: it is EXPOSED to the EXTERIOR (SE-3, Ship Body BDY-29). *(Web Map & Transit WM-17 and ASK-S1 answer the former Open Question 32.)* *Criterion.* AC-SE-36a to 36f. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *Pillar.* P1.

**SE-36. Co-op seams (record, do not build).** *Rule.* `repl_hint` is `SHARED_SUMMARY`, defined as exactly **`{locomotion_cap, vitals_state, cue_cold, cue_fog, cue_load, beam_shape, charge_band, carried_class}`** — no reserve value, no tool charges, no integrity number. A reading is private truth; a cue is public truth. The crew strip carries no vitals by default (the honest check is lighting a crewmate's visor). `revive` is dormant. Shape uniqueness is Co-op Session's; I-SUPPLY at four seats is SE-10's note. *Requested amendment (routed, not adopted on this GDD's authority):* the art-director finds the eight fields cannot drive all crewmate overlays — the summary lacks the seam jet (`cue_leak`) and the HEAVY back-frame (`stowed_heavy`), `cue_cold` must carry four values (none, vapour, rime level 1, rime level 2), and the systems-designer asks for a signed thermal cue so the heat side is not dropped; VS-B only (OQ-5). *Criterion.* AC-SE-37. *Pillar.* P1 (human channel).

**SE-37. No randomness, no scene tree.** *Rule.* Suit uses no RNG, no wall clock and no `delta` (H9): drain, ladder and cue thresholds are deterministic. Suit's hook, mutators and block contain no `Node` (H10). All values are int32-safe. *Criterion.* AC-SE-38. *Pillar.* P1 (replay determinism).

#### Group G — The interface Suit publishes

**SE-38. The block schema (`suit.state`, per seat).** *Rule.* All fields are integers or plain-data dictionaries; no floats. One dictionary per seat, keyed by PlayerId.

| Field | Type | Range | Persisted | Dirty key (bucket) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reserve_mgu` | int | 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | yes | `S/<PlayerId>` on a 5 % bucket of the maximum, on the engage edge and the warn edge |
| `breath_src` | int | 0 ROOM / 1 RESERVE / 2 GASP / 3 NONE | yes | on change |
| `breath_src_change_seq` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: D.10)* | int | 0..65535 | yes (so a restore is never an edge; presenters adopt the value silently) | on change (with `breath_src`) |
| `pending_draw_mgu` | int | 0..`R_BREATH` (the breath request only; the total asked of the Graph is this plus `pending_extra_mgu`, 0..`R_BREATH` + `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | yes | none (internal) |
| `breath_starved` | int | 0..1 | yes | on change |
| `pending_extra_mgu` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: PASSIVE credit term)* | int | 0..`TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN`; 0 in VERB mode | yes | none (internal) |
| `cell_ticks` | int | 0..`CELL_MAX_TICKS` | yes | 5 % bucket, the warn edge and the empty edge |
| `thermal_load` | int | `-TL_MAX`..`+TL_MAX` | yes | on a rung change or a cue-level change |
| `integrity_bp` | int | 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL` | yes | 250 bp bucket (the Graph's quantum) |
| `rung_breath`, `rung_warmth`, `rung_integrity` | int | 0..3 | yes | on change |
| `hold_breath`, `hold_integrity` | int (runs) | 0..`HOLD_23` max | yes (a restore must not reset the floor mid-hold) | none (internal) |
| `exo_pending` | int | 0..1 | yes | none (persisted; a restore mid-channel must not drop a landed hit) |
| `rf_warmth` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: D.4 refractory)* | int (runs) | 0..`HOLD_12` max | yes (a restore must not shorten the refractory) | none (internal) |
| `p_age0`, `p_age1` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: D.12)* | int (runs) | 0..`STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS` + 1 | yes (a restore mid-window must replay the same trigger) | none (internal) |
| `data_rev` *(block-level, not per seat; Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | int | 1..the build's current revision | yes | none |
| `beam_shape` | int | 1..4 | yes | never after bind |
| `equipped_slot` | int | 1..4 (0 = none) | yes | on change |
| `tools` | dict `def_id -> instance` | one per `tool_*` | yes | on a charge change |
| `stowed` | dict `def_id -> instance` | count within the def's `stack_max` (stowed plus reserved) | yes | on a count change |
| `reserved` | dict `def_id -> count` | 0..`stack_max` (SE-41) | yes | on a count change |
| `carried_id`, `carried` | int, instance | id or NONE | yes | on change |
| `last_section_id`, `last_room_ref` | int | valid or NONE | yes | none |
| `prev_*` (edge-detection copies, including `prev_cid`, which is NONE while the seat is in HOLD_ROOM) | int | — | **no**, rebuilt in `post_restore` (`prev_cid` = the current compartment, or NONE if IN_POD, so a restore is never a compartment crossing) | none |

One `S/<PlayerId>` dirty family per seat per change burst — never one key per field — and a restore yields one `ALL` notification then silence; the budget is well under `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256. Size is on the order of 330 bytes per seat after the 2026-10-27 additions (`p_age0`, `p_age1`, `rf_warmth`, `pending_extra_mgu`, `breath_src_change_seq` and one block-level `data_rev`), under 1.5 KB at four seats *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The **closed restore reasons** are `SUIT_RESTORE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`, `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE` (any field outside the table, including a `rung` not in 0..3, a `hold` or `rf_warmth` above its maximum or a `p_age` above `STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS` + 1), `SUIT_RESTORE_STOWED_OVERFLOW` (a count above `stack_max`), `SUIT_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_DEF`, `SUIT_RESTORE_BEAM_SHAPE`, `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`, `SUIT_RESTORE_SEAT_UNBOUND` and `SUIT_RESTORE_DATA_REV_UNKNOWN` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. *Criterion.* AC-SE-35 and AC-SE-49a-c. *Pillar.* P5.

**SE-39. Run order inside `suit.vitals`.** *Rule.* Per run, for each bound seat in ascending PlayerId: (1) skip a seat that is unbound or DEAD (frozen); (2) resolve the room (SE-3) and step the pressure-drop window (D.12; may submit one `stagger`); (3) reconcile the respiration ledger (SE-5); (4) choose the breath source and draw or debit (SE-4); (5) apply the leak debit when `integrity_bp` is below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` (SE-22) — integrity as committed before this run, so a burn or arc crossing the threshold in step 8 of run n shows the seam jet on run n (derived on read) and first debits on run n+1, while a hit landed in P2 before run n debits in run n; (6) drain the cell from `emitter.state.lit` as of T-1 and call `force_off` at zero (SE-8); (7) step the thermal accumulator (SE-20); (8) apply the SCORCH burn and the arc damage (SE-22); (9) evaluate the three ladders (SE-17) on the post-step state; (10) derive the outputs (`locomotion_cap`, cue levels) and mark the dirty keys. Damage and credit mutators that land earlier in the tick (P2 apply, or another system's P3 hook) have already been applied to the block when the hook reads it. *Cost target (a model parameter, no budget exists in any GDD for Suit):* at most 0.05 ms per run in the Release export, O(bound seats × constant).

**SE-40. The published surface.** *Rule.* Every item below is Suit's, and **only these ids appear in any GDD example** (the kernel's `graph.propagate` example was copied by siblings; examples are read as contracts). *Commands:* exactly `equip_tool {slot: 1..6}`. *Reason codes Suit registers in its own namespace (closed; the kernel namespaces reasons per registering system, so Suit's `BUSY` and `NOT_ACTIONABLE` on `equip_tool` are distinct from Interaction's and the Controller's codes of the same name):* `EMPTY_SLOT`, `NOT_ACTIONABLE`, `BUSY`, `ALREADY_FULL` (read by Interaction's `top_up` validate), `STACK_FULL` (read by Interaction's pickup). `CHARGE_EMPTY` is the Emitter State's reason, read from Suit's charge; `OUT_OF_CHARGES` is Interaction's, read from Suit's counts. *Read views (pure; true values only):*

| Read | Returns | Primary reader |
|---|---|---|
| `tool_in_hand(player_id)` | `def_id` or NONE | Interaction (`select_verb`) |
| `stowed_count(player_id, def_id)` | int | Interaction (`throw_glowstick` validate, repairs) |
| `carried_instance(player_id)` | instance or NONE | Interaction, Controller overlays |
| `locomotion_cap(player_id)` | FULL, WALK_ONLY or NONE | Controller |
| `charge(player_id)` | `cell_ticks` | Emitter State (`toggle_beam.validate`) |
| `tool_charge(player_id, def_id)` | int, 0..`charges_max` (0 for an unheld def) | the suppressant effect verb's registrant (Attention or Horror, per Interaction's command-table note and Suit row — no MVP row) for its `OUT_OF_CHARGES`; the HUD rail |
| `held_mass_g(player_id)`, `stowed_mass_g(player_id)` | int | diagnostics, Vertical Slice A field check |
| `can_stow(player_id, def_id, n)` | bool | Interaction (pickup validate) |
| `reserve_deficit_mgu(player_id)`, `cell_deficit_ticks(player_id)` | int | Interaction (`top_up` validate) |
| `true_value(key, player_id)` | the five reading keys (SE-29) | Perception Filter (`perceived(SUIT_KEY(k), viewer)` wraps it; the HUD calls `perceived`, Sanity calls nothing) and Debug Overlay (True Values) (DIAGNOSTIC, dev builds only) |
| `view(player_id)` | the HUD bundle: `locomotion_cap`, `vitals_state`, the three rungs, `o2_pp_dkpa`, `reserve_pm`, `cell_pm`, `integrity_pm`, the ambient band enums, `equipped_slot`, **the tool charges** (also carried here for the HUD; validates read `tool_charge` — CK-5, OQ-7 ruled), `glowstick_count`, `carried_def_id`, the cue levels (including `cue_breath`, `breath_src_change_seq` and `charge_advise` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*), `beam_shape`; the four reading fields (`o2_pp_dkpa`, `reserve_pm`, `cell_pm`, `integrity_pm`) are pre-wrap values for cue derivation and diagnostics only — the HUD draws the five reading numbers only through `perceived`, which wraps `true_value` (SE-29) | HUD, Adaptive Audio, Presenter |

*Class B mutators* (clamp, never reject; each changes only `suit.state`; the callers are provisional until their GDDs land):

| Mutator | Signature | Clamp or refusal | Callers |
|---|---|---|---|
| `add_stowed` | `(player_id, instance) -> accepted_count` | merge per IDB-15; refuses over `stack_max` | Interaction `pickup` |
| `spend_stowed` | `(player_id, def_id, n) -> spent` | clamp to stock | Interaction `throw_glowstick` |
| `reserve_stowed`, `release_stowed`, `consume_reserved` | `(player_id, def_id, n) -> bool`, `-> released`, `-> consumed` (SE-41) | clamp to the stowed (respectively reserved) count | Interaction FULL repair jobs |
| `set_carried` | `(player_id, instance_id, instance)` | refuses if already carrying | Interaction `pickup` |
| `release_carried` | `(player_id) -> (instance_id, instance)` | returns NONE if empty | Interaction `drop` and the NONE-cap conversion; Assembly `install_module` apply (MSA-27), after Graph M5 in the same `WriteScope` |
| `consume_charge` | `(player_id, def_id) -> ok` | clamp at 0 | Interaction / Attention-owned effect verbs |
| `credit_reserve` | `(player_id, mgu) -> accepted` | clamp to the deficit | Interaction V7 apply |
| `credit_cell` | `(player_id, ticks) -> accepted` | clamp to the deficit | Interaction V7 apply |
| `apply_suit_damage` | `(player_id, amount_bp, source_ref)` | clamp at 0; `source_ref` classed ENDOGENOUS or EXOGENOUS (SE-23); an unclassed ref is a programmer error (debug assert and a registration lint), never defaulted | Interaction `begin_harm`, Horror (HE-15: the 3-argument call; its `source_ref` is classed EXOGENOUS once at record creation and persisted across restore) |
| `patch_integrity` | `(player_id, bp)` | capped at `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP`, never lowers | Interaction `begin_patch` on ACTOR_SELF (IA-17) |
| `replace_tool` | `(player_id, instance) -> discarded` | only when the held tool's charge is 0 | Interaction `pickup` |
| `revive` | `(player_id)` | dormant (Vertical Slice B) | Interaction `begin_revive` |

*Calls Suit makes:* Graph `draw_air` (M15); Graph `withdraw_store` (M16) only inside V7's apply; Emitter `bind_beam` (E4, new game) and `force_off` (E5). *Retired names — do not use:* `consume_glowstick`, `stow`, `unstow`, `has_glowstick`, `equipped_tool`, `apply_harm`. *Diagnostics (names frozen 2026-10-27; read by the Debug Overlay as DIAGNOSTIC, dev builds only):* `suit_reserve_shortfall`, `suit_rung_limited{axis}`, `suit_cue_state{player}`, `suit_exo_bypass`; and, added 2026-10-27, `suit_restore_rescaled{from_rev, to_rev}` (not part of the frozen four; the Debug Overlay may subscribe). *Criterion.* AC-SE-39 (the guard-rail test: every published mutator changes only `suit.state`; the only registered command is `equip_tool`). *Pillar.* P1.

**SE-41. Reserved parts — Interaction's reservation API, adopted.** *Rule.* A FULL repair job holds its part in a reserved state from the begin tick, so that the stock count and the weight cue cannot reveal whether a subject is truly damaged (a hallucinated-broken module would otherwise "spend no part" and a truly damaged one would, an oracle readable from the actor channel in about 100 ms at zero cost). Suit supplies the holder side with three Class B mutators, in addition to the SE-40 table: **`reserve_stowed(player_id, def_id, n) -> bool`** (moves `n` from the stowed count to a per-seat `reserved` bucket; returns false and changes nothing if the stowed count is below `n`), **`release_stowed(player_id, def_id, n)`** (returns reserved units to the stowed count) and **`consume_reserved(player_id, def_id, n)`** (destroys reserved units — the part is spent). A part in the reserved bucket is treated as having **left the pack from the begin tick, in both truth branches**: it is excluded from `stowed_count`, from `stowed_mass_g` and therefore from `cue_load`. Physically, the reserved part is at the work site, out of the pack, from the begin tick in both truth branches — so `cue_load` and the HEAVY back-frame remain true readings of what is on the back, never a suppressed cue (P1). The ledger of RESERVED and PAID records, their absolute `until` and `grace_until` ticks and the resume allowance are **Interaction's** (persisted in its own block); Suit persists only the per-seat reserved counts (SE-38), so a restore mid-job keeps the reservation and reintroduces no oracle. `spend_stowed` remains for the glowstick throw. A pickup into a stack that has units reserved is bounded by `stack_max` over stowed plus reserved. *(Interaction's designed IA-18 carries the three names, which Suit adopts on the precedent that the second GDD adopts the first's text. The stage ruling's R20 list had only `spend_stowed` for "a part on the first productive FULL step"; the reservation supersedes that caller. Interaction's stated fallback if Suit refuses — keep the spend at the first productive step and accept a one-run oracle — is not taken.)* *Why.* P1 and P4: an oracle that answers "is this module truly damaged" for free defeats the cost rule that makes a hallucinated broken state falsifiable only by time or risk. *Rejected.* Spending at the first productive step only (the oracle); refunding on abort (a free probe). *Criterion.* AC-SE-40: reserve → read `stowed_count` and `cue_load` → both fall at the begin tick whether or not the subject is damaged; `release_stowed` and `consume_reserved` conserve the total. *Pillar.* P1, P4.

### States and Transitions

Suit has no single state machine. It has a **seat lifecycle**, a **breath-source machine**, **three rung ladders** whose worst rung becomes the cap, a **cell ladder** and a small set of **holder states**; each is a function of the true state, and each row below says *who may cause the transition*. "The run" means one `suit.vitals` execution (every `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, 0.1 s). Controller states (IDLE, STAGGERED, DOWNED, DEAD, IN_POD, CHANNELLING) are the Controller's; Suit reads `loco_state` and never writes it.

**Seat lifecycle (Suit's view).**

| State | Entry | Suit behaviour | Exit | Who may cause it |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UNBOUND | no seat for the PlayerId | no record; no run cost | `new_game` or a seat bind creates the record (start kit, then `create_seat_beam` (E9), then `bind_beam` (E4)) | the new-game flow (Save/Load SL-18 `new_game`; the Chart Table, CT-24) / Co-op Session (host-local boot) |
| ACTIVE | seat bound, Controller `loco_state` not DEAD | the full run (SE-39) | seat DEAD or unbound | the Controller (death), the host (unbind) |
| HOLD_ROOM | `loco_state == IN_POD`, whatever `room_at` answers (Web Map's ASK-S1 "NONE or IN_POD", narrowed by Ship Body's BDY-29: a bare NONE is EXPOSED, next row) (SE-3, SE-35, WM-17) | while IN_POD, reserve and cell are **held constant**, no `draw_air` is called, and the thermal load and the hold counters do not advance (`last_room_ref` kept) | `loco_state` leaves IN_POD | the Controller (`loco_state`), Web Map & Transit (the hop) |
| EXPOSED | `room_at` answers NONE and `loco_state` is not IN_POD — a defect-only state on a Layout body (BDY-29) (SE-3) | as ACTIVE, with the EXTERIOR's values by rule (VACUUM, ANOXIC, `T_AMB_MC`), never the last room's bands; no `draw_air`; everything keeps running | `room_at` answers a room | Ship Body / Layout (position) |
| DEAD | Controller `loco_state` DEAD | block **frozen**: no draw, no drain, no rung evaluation | restore from the autosave (WORLD rollback) | the Controller, then Save/Load's `request_restore` |

DOWNED is a Controller state; Suit treats it exactly as ACTIVE (SE-24). IN_POD is a Controller state; Suit enters HOLD_ROOM for it (the pod's section is dormant and never breathed, WM-17).

**Breath-source machine (SE-4).** Four states; transitions are evaluated every run from the enums and the reserve and never from a grant.

| State | Entry condition | Exit condition | Behaviour | Cue on entry |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ROOM | air class AIR_OK | air becomes unbreathable | `draw_air(cid, R_BREATH)` each run; pending request recorded | valve click on **disengage** when returning from RESERVE |
| RESERVE | unbreathable air and `reserve_mgu > 0` | air becomes AIR_OK, or the reserve reaches 0 | debits `min(R_BREATH, reserve)`; calls nothing | **valve click on engage**; closed-cadence breath; visor fog |
| GASP | unbreathable, `band_o2` HYPOXIC, `band_p` NORMAL or LOW, reserve 0 | air becomes AIR_OK, or air worsens to ANOXIC / DECOMP / VACUUM | `draw_air(cid, R_BREATH)` — whatever the room holds | laboured breath; fog |
| NONE | unbreathable (ANOXIC, DECOMP or VACUUM) and reserve 0 | air improves or a top-up credits the reserve | nothing is breathed | gasp loop |

The causes are the Graph's bands (the room) and the reserve (Suit, Interaction's `credit_reserve`).

**Breath axis — rung, condition, cue, cap** (`d` = demand, `t` = terminal; names only).

| Rung | Condition (true state) | Cue (see the cue table) | Cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| OK 0 | source ROOM and `band_o2` NORMAL and `band_p` NORMAL | baseline breath | FULL |
| STRAINED 1 | `band_o2` THIN, or `band_p` LOW, or source RESERVE with the reserve above `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` — **immediate on entry** | shorter, quicker breath (thin signature); visor fog level 1; valve click once on engage | FULL |
| CRITICAL 2 | source RESERVE with the reserve at or below `RESERVE_WARN_MGU`, or source GASP, or source NONE, or `breath_starved` — held `HOLD_12` | ragged breath; fog level 2; **warning mark** on the strip's breath dial (the one anchor for this axis, whichever reading the dial is showing *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*); stagger on the falling edge | WALK_ONLY |
| FAILED 3 | `t`: source NONE — held `HOLD_23(breath)` after CRITICAL | gasp loop; slump (the Controller's DOWNED) | NONE |

**Warmth axis** (the accumulator `thermal_load` is the hold; SE-20).

| Rung | Condition | Cue | Cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| OK 0 | `band_t` in {COMFORT, COOL} and `abs(thermal_load) < TL_STRAINED` | COOL: breath vapour only (a band cue, not a rung) | FULL |
| STRAINED 1 | `band_t` in {FROST, FREEZING, DEEP_COLD, HOT, SCORCH}, or `abs(thermal_load) ≥ TL_STRAINED` | cold side: rime level 1 and shiver at rest; **heat side: no body cue** — the reading's zone band, the room's scorch trail and the audio carry it | FULL |
| CRITICAL 2 | `abs(thermal_load) ≥ TL_CRITICAL` (only reachable by cold past the shifted onset, heat past `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, SCORCH or DEEP_COLD); descends below `TL_CRITICAL − TL_HYST` | rime level 2; harder shiver; **warning mark** beside the warmth glyph; stagger | WALK_ONLY |
| FAILED 3 | `abs(thermal_load) ≥ TL_MAX`; descends below `TL_MAX − TL_HYST` | collapse (DOWNED); rime level 2 persists on the downed sprite | NONE |

**Integrity axis** (counter axis; the thresholds are Suit's own symbols).

| Rung | Condition | Cue | Cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| OK 0 | `integrity_bp ≥ SUIT_HP_OK` | none | FULL |
| STRAINED 1 | `SUIT_LEAK_HP ≤ integrity_bp < SUIT_HP_OK` | no body overlay in the MVP (the art-director ruled no scuff overlay: it would collide with the scar and persistent-patch vocabulary and the +25-image budget is spent); the integrity reading's zone band carries it alone; **inert by design** — no cap, insulation (SE-21), drain or onset effect, so nothing lethal needs a witness here (OQ-19 ruled, CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26) | FULL |
| CRITICAL 2 | `SUIT_HP_FAIL ≤ integrity_bp < SUIT_LEAK_HP` — held `HOLD_12`; **the suit leaks from the run `integrity_bp` falls below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`**, before the hold completes | seam vapour jet; **warning mark** beside the integrity glyph; stagger | WALK_ONLY |
| FAILED 3 | `t`: `integrity_bp < SUIT_HP_FAIL` — held `HOLD_23(integrity)` after CRITICAL (exogenous: SE-23) | suit rupture, collapse | NONE |

The seam jet is drawn from the run the leak starts, even before the CRITICAL hold completes: the pipe leaks before the rung changes.

**Rung transition rules** (SE-17; D.3 restates with variables). `HOLD_12` and `HOLD_23(axis)` are in runs.

| From | To | Guard | Hold reset |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 1 | `d ≥ 1` | hold = 0 |
| 1 | 2 | `d ≥ 2` for `HOLD_12` consecutive runs | 0 on promotion; 0 on any run with `d < 2` |
| 2 | 3 | `t` true for `HOLD_23(axis)` consecutive runs | 0 on promotion; 0 on any run with `t` false |
| any `r` greater than the demand | the demand | instantly, the run the demand falls; a FAILED seat whose terminal condition still holds stays FAILED and drops only when `t` clears (to `min(d, 2)`) | 0 |
| warmth, any | by `abs(thermal_load)` and the band | one rung per run upward, except 1 to 2 within `HOLD_12` runs of a fall from 2 or more (refractory, D.4); instant downward past the hysteresis | none (the accumulator is the hold) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| integrity, after an EXOGENOUS apply | the threshold band of `integrity_bp` (0..3) | on the run after an EXOGENOUS apply, while `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` is 1 (the default) (SE-23) | clear `exo_pending` |

`vitals_state` is the worst rung; the cap follows it (OK and STRAINED FULL, CRITICAL WALK_ONLY, FAILED NONE). **Cap effects, for orientation (the Controller's):** a falling edge FULL → WALK_ONLY plays one stagger of `STAGGER_TICKS` 30 and then walk-only (`run` becomes a no-op); a cap of NONE makes the seat DOWNED (the carried module dropped, any channel cleared); a DOWNED seat becomes DEAD after `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` 1800 unless the cap rises first.

**Cell ladder (SE-8).**

| State | Condition | Behaviour | Cause of transitions |
|---|---|---|---|
| OK | `cell_ticks` above `CELL_WARN_S × TICK_RATE` | drains while the beam is lit (as of T-1) | the player's `toggle_beam` (Controller); `credit_cell` (Interaction V7) |
| WARN | `0 < cell_ticks ≤ CELL_WARN_S × TICK_RATE` | cell-whine audio and the **warning mark** beside the charge glyph; beam output untouched | draining |
| EMPTY | `cell_ticks = 0` | `force_off(beam_id)` fires once on the run it reaches 0; `toggle_beam` ON is rejected `CHARGE_EMPTY` | draining; refilled only by `credit_cell` |

**Holder states.**

| Holder | States | Transitions and who causes them |
|---|---|---|
| Tool slot (1-4) | HELD-EQUIPPED, HELD-STOWED-ON-RAIL, EMPTY-CHARGES (suppressant at 0, greyed) | `equip_tool` (player) moves the equipped mark; `consume_charge` (Interaction / the effect owner) decrements; `replace_tool` (Interaction `pickup`) swaps an empty suppressant |
| Stowed stock (per `def_id`) | ABSENT, COUNTED (1..`stack_max`), RESERVED (units held out of the count by a FULL repair job, SE-41) | `add_stowed` (Interaction `pickup`) merges; `spend_stowed` (Interaction throw) decrements; `reserve_stowed` / `release_stowed` / `consume_reserved` (Interaction repair jobs) move units between COUNTED and RESERVED or destroy them; refused merges leave the surplus in the world |
| Carried | NONE, CARRYING | `set_carried` (Interaction `pickup`); `release_carried` (Interaction `drop`, the NONE-cap conversion, or Assembly's `install_module` apply, MSA-27); Suit never self-releases |

**The cue table (cue, channel class, driving true value, never-source)** *(a restatement of D.10's normative cue table, which wins wherever they differ; Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Levels are derived on read from the last committed block; presentation steps on the next frame after a level changes and never fades; cadence derives from vitals only and never speeds up or slows with sanity. **Never-source for every row: Sanity, Attention, any `PERCEPTION_OUT` view, any filtered value.**

| Cue | Channel class (SE-26) | Driving true value | Level definition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Breath vapour puff (exhale) | closed list: breath | `band_t` in {COOL, FROST, FREEZING, DEEP_COLD} | `cue_cold` 1 |
| Shiver (sprite, idle only) | closed list: shiver | warmth rung ≥ STRAINED, cold side | `cue_cold` ≥ 2 |
| Rime on shell plates (never at a fitting) | on-sprite anchor (routed list amendment) | warmth rung, cold side | `cue_cold` 2 (level 1, STRAINED) and 3 (level 2, CRITICAL and above) |
| Visor fog | closed list: visor fog | breath rung | `cue_fog` 1 (STRAINED), 2 (CRITICAL and above) |
| Breath cadence — thin | closed list: breath | `band_o2` THIN, `band_p` LOW, source, breath rung | rate steps 0-2 by rung |
| Breath cadence — load | closed list: breath (separate layers: creak plus footfall exhale) | `cue_load` (SE-28) | `cue_load` 0 / 1 / 2; **distinct signature from thin** |
| Reserve valve click | breath family; routed list ask | change of `breath_src` | an edge, not a level |
| Seam vapour jet | on-sprite anchor | `integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` | on/off |
| HEAVY back-frame | on-sprite anchor | stowed class HEAVY | on/off |
| Stagger, walk-only gait, slump | Controller animation via the cap | `locomotion_cap` | the three cap states |
| Cell whine and the charge warning mark | reading (lie-capable by architecture, uncorrupted in the MVP) | `cell_ticks` at or below `CELL_WARN_S` | `charge_band` WARN |
| The beam going dark | Emitter State's physical result | `force_off` at zero cell | an edge |

**The loadout and slot table (noise is Interaction's, not Suit's).**

| Slot | Tool | Verbs it enables (Interaction's / Assembly's / Sanity's) | Uniqueness | Charge | Noise class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `tool_cutter` | V8 `begin_cut` (salvage), `begin_cap`; `begin_seam_cut` (Assembly, the section cut; TOOL with one LOUD at DONE) | one per player | none | Interaction's declaration (TOOL; LOUD at cut completion) |
| 2 | `tool_welder` | V1 `begin_patch`, V9 `begin_weld`; self-patch if hosted | one per player | none | TOOL |
| 3 | `tool_scanner` | `field_check` (the falsification command is Interaction's; Sanity owns the result) | one per player | none (`charges_max` 0) | Interaction's declaration |
| 4 | `tool_suppressant` | none at MVP, full or empty (AT-19; HE-39); the Vertical Slice effect is Attention's and Horror's | one per player | `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES` 3 of `charges_max` 3; no refill | Interaction's declaration |
| 5, 6 | unbound in the MVP | — | — | — | — |
| passive rail entries | the glowstick count (the `throw` action, Q / R3) and the carried-module glyph | — | — | — | — |

The chest lamp is **not a tool def** and not `mod_lamp`: it is Suit equipment, available in every actionable state.

### Interactions with Other Systems

*Data flow is from Suit's perspective ("in" = to Suit, "out" = from Suit). Where a counterpart GDD exists, its Interactions or Dependencies row is cited by section id with one clause, so the two documents can be checked against each other (bidirectional consistency); section ids are used because line numbers move. Rows about a system whose GDD is absent are **provisional** and end with what that GDD must repeat; Interaction, Ship Section & Layout and 2D Lighting & Occlusion are designed (2026-10-26, Interaction 19:34; re-verified at the final advisor review, 2026-10-27; 18:38 and 18:48) and their rows quote current text. Status as of the round-1 revision, 2026-10-26; **consistency pass 2026-10-27:** the rows for Ship Body, Attention, Sanity, Boarding & Salvage, the HUD & Suit Layer, the Perception Filter, Adaptive Audio & Tells, Horror Entities, Web Map & Transit, the new-game flow (Save/Load SL-18 and the Chart Table) and Modular Ship Assembly now cite designed text. This GDD never edits another document: every refresh owed is routed in Cross-References.*

## Formulas

*Conventions.* Every formula is per seat, per `suit.vitals` run (one run = `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` ticks = one Graph step = 0.1 s; runs per second = `TICK_RATE / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` = 10). Everything is integer arithmetic; every quotient is taken on non-negative operands (floor unless `ceil` is written), because GDScript integer division truncates toward zero and the engine reference has no scripting file to confirm a convention for negative operands (→ ADR candidate 4; NOT SOURCEABLE). Nothing here is hashed or replicated except the block, whose fields are all ints (kernel D.10 and D.12 apply). The four Graph band constants appear only as names. "Step n" in a worked example means the n-th Graph step (= Suit run) after the fixture's t = 0, counted exactly; every fixture time is `[derived; the Graph harness confirms to ±15 %]`. D.1 to D.7 are the seven formulas registered at Phase 5 (R32); D.8 to D.12 are unregistered helpers that restate derived values and build assertions. **Number provenance:** where the game-designer and systems-designer proposals diverged, the stage rulings and the already-written rules decided; the divergences are listed in Tuning Knobs and Open Questions. Every constant below is `[PH]` unless it is a registry name cited by name.

### D.1 `suit_breath_source`

The `suit_breath_source` formula is defined as:

```
AIR_OK   = (band_o2 in {NORMAL, THIN}) AND (band_p in {NORMAL, LOW})
AIR_POOR = (band_o2 == HYPOXIC)        AND (band_p in {NORMAL, LOW})
AIR_NONE = (band_o2 == ANOXIC)         OR  (band_p in {DECOMP, VACUUM})
source   = ROOM    if AIR_OK
         = RESERVE if (AIR_POOR or AIR_NONE) and reserve_mgu > 0
         = GASP    if AIR_POOR and reserve_mgu == 0
         = NONE    if AIR_NONE and reserve_mgu == 0
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `band_o2` | enum | NORMAL, THIN, HYPOXIC, ANOXIC | Graph `compartment(cid)`; ANOXIC by rule for an EXPOSED seat (SE-3) | the occupant's O2 band; hysteresis inherited; while IN_POD D.1 is not evaluated (HOLD_ROOM) |
| `band_p` | enum | NORMAL, LOW, DECOMP, VACUUM | Graph `compartment(cid)`; VACUUM by rule for an EXPOSED seat (SE-3) | the occupant's pressure band |
| `reserve_mgu` | int mGU | 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | `suit.state`, after this run's shortfall debit (D.2) | the seat's reserve |
| `source` | enum | ROOM, RESERVE, GASP, NONE | result | drives D.2 and the breath-axis demand (SE-19) |

**Output Range:** exactly one of four values; total over the domain (AIR_OK takes 4 of the 16 band pairs, AIR_POOR 2, AIR_NONE 10). The HYPOXIC and DECOMP boundaries *are* `HYPOXIA_PP` and `DECOMP_P`, so no literal exists in Suit. The source never depends on a `draw_air` grant (SE-4). **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Example (DOOR-BREACH, suited seat, reserve full, `lsg` D.15):** steps 1 to 63 `band_o2` NORMAL or THIN (THIN from step 25), `band_p` NORMAL then LOW (from step 56): AIR_OK, ROOM. Step 64: HYPOXIC with LOW: AIR_POOR and `reserve_mgu` > 0: RESERVE (valve click). Step 116: ANOXIC: AIR_NONE, RESERVE. Step 964: reserve 0, AIR_NONE: NONE. **Example (GASP, O2-ROOM, `lsg` D.15):** at step 3063 the reserve is 0, `band_o2` is HYPOXIC (`p_o2` about 128 dkPa) and `band_p` NORMAL: AIR_POOR with reserve 0: GASP; the seat draws from the room again until the room reaches ANOXIC (D.3, third table).

### D.2 `respiration_request`

The `respiration_request` formula is defined as *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (the credit term for the PASSIVE extra and the breath-only shortfall are new):

```
granted_prev   = draw_grant(crew).granted                          # the previous run's M15 grant, published by the Graph until this run (LS-18)
bgrant         = min(granted_prev, pending_draw_mgu)                # the Graph grants one amount; the breath is served first
extra_credit   = granted_prev - bgrant                              # the granted part of last run's PASSIVE extra; 0 in VERB mode
shortfall      = pending_draw_mgu - bgrant                          # R_BREATH-only: a room that grants exactly R_BREATH owes the reserve nothing
debit_sf       = min(shortfall, reserve_mgu)
reserve_1      = min(RESERVE_MAX_MGU, reserve_mgu - debit_sf + extra_credit)       # the cap clamps; nothing is created
breath_starved'= 1 if shortfall > debit_sf else 0
source         = suit_breath_source(band_o2, band_p, reserve_1)     # D.1
request        = R_BREATH if source in {ROOM, GASP} else 0          # draw_air(cid, request + extra), Class B
extra          = D.9 PASSIVE guard (0 unless TOPUP_MODE is PASSIVE and source == ROOM)
spend          = min(R_BREATH, reserve_1) if source == RESERVE else 0
leak           = LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN if integrity_bp < SUIT_LEAK_HP else 0     # integrity as it stands at the start of the run
reserve_2      = reserve_1 - spend
reserve_mgu'   = reserve_2 - min(leak, reserve_2)
pending_draw_mgu' = request ;  pending_extra_mgu' = extra
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `R_BREATH` | int mGU per step per crew | 100-600 (300) | Suit-registered (R32); Graph placeholder today | one crew member's draw |
| `pending_draw_mgu` | int mGU | 0..`R_BREATH` | `suit.state` | the breath request issued last run (ROOM or GASP), else 0; the total asked of the Graph was `pending_draw_mgu` + `pending_extra_mgu`, 0..`R_BREATH` + `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` |
| `pending_extra_mgu` *(new)* | int mGU | 0..`TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` (0 in VERB mode) | `suit.state` | the PASSIVE extra added to last run's ROOM request (D.9) |
| `extra_credit` *(new)* | int mGU | 0..`TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` | derived | the granted part of the extra, credited to the reserve; 0 in VERB mode |
| `granted_prev` | int mGU | 0..`R_BREATH` + `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` | Graph `draw_grant(crew).granted` (M15, LS-18) | what the Graph granted for last run's request (queued to phase 0 of its next run and published until the following run; OQ-12 (a) answered) |
| `reserve_mgu` | int mGU | 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | `suit.state` | reserve at the start of the run |
| `breath_starved` | int | 0..1 | `suit.state` | one-run flag: a shortfall the reserve could not cover; raises the breath demand to 2 for that run (SE-5) |
| `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` | int mGU | 0-150 (150) at `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 (A5) | knob | the extra drain below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` (SE-22), whatever the source |
| `integrity_bp` | int bp | 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL` | `suit.state` | D.7 |

**Output Range:** `reserve_mgu'` in 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU`, never negative (every subtraction is clamped); `request` is `R_BREATH` or 0. Maximum reserve loss in one run: `debit_sf` 300 plus `spend` 300 plus `leak` 150 = 750 mGU at the defaults (a ROOM run that was fully shorted, followed by a RESERVE run on a leaking suit); minimum 0. Seats are processed in ascending PlayerId (SE-5). **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Example 1 (door breach with a full reserve, leak 0):** from step 64 each run spends 300: after 900 runs the reserve reaches 0 at step 963, so the seat is on the reserve for 90 s. **Example 2 (shortfall lag, ascending order):** a room reduced to `n_o2` 450 mGU receives three requests of 300: grants 300, 150, 0 in ascending `(compartment id, crew id)`; the second and third seats record `pending_draw_mgu` 300, and on the next run debit 150 and 300 from their own reserves (a seat with reserve 0 sets `breath_starved`, but the room, now ANOXIC, already selects RESERVE or NONE). **Example 3 (leak in a safe room):** a seat at `integrity_bp` 3500 in NORMAL air requests 300 from the room each run and loses 150 mGU of reserve per run: 270,000 lasts 1800 runs (3 min); the seam jet and the reserve reading are its witnesses. **Example 4 (PASSIVE credit, dormant configuration):** a V = 8 room at 210 dkPa receives a request of 300 + an extra of 30 and grants the full 330: next run `bgrant` 300, `shortfall` 0, `extra_credit` 30, the reserve rises by 30 and the room's O2 fell by the same 30. A room that grants exactly 300 of a 330 request: `bgrant` 300, `shortfall` 0, `extra_credit` 0 — the reserve is untouched (the unamended expression, `pending_draw_mgu − granted_prev` with `pending_draw_mgu` = 330, would have debited it 30, the very amount being topped up). A room that grants 250: `bgrant` 250, `shortfall` 50 debited from the reserve, `extra_credit` 0. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### D.3 `vitals_ladder_step`

The `vitals_ladder_step` formula is defined as:

```
# per counter axis (breath, integrity), once per run. d = demand 0..2, t = terminal condition (SE-19, SE-22)
band_int = 3 if integrity_bp < SUIT_HP_FAIL else 2 if integrity_bp < SUIT_LEAK_HP else 1 if integrity_bp < SUIT_HP_OK else 0
if axis == integrity and exo_pending and SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER == 1:       # CK-2: no hold
    rung' = band_int ; hold' = hold if rung' == rung else 0 ; exo_pending' = 0
elif axis == integrity and exo_pending:                                       # knob 0: the flag clears with no effect; the limited path below runs
    exo_pending' = 0 ; continue with the limited path
elif (rung <= 2 and d < rung) or (rung == 3 and not t):                       # instant descent, any distance
    rung' = min(d, 2) if rung == 3 else d ; hold' = 0
elif rung == 0 and d >= 1:           rung' = 1 ; hold' = 0                    # first cue, no hold
elif rung == 1 and d >= 2:           hold' = hold + 1 ; promote to 2 and hold' = 0 when hold' >= HOLD_12
elif rung == 2 and t:                hold' = hold + 1 ; promote to 3 and hold' = 0 when hold' >= HOLD_23(axis)
else:                                rung' = rung ; hold' = 0                 # any run the qualifying condition does not hold resets the counter
vitals_state = max(rung_breath, rung_warmth, rung_integrity)
locomotion_cap = FULL if vitals_state <= 1 ; WALK_ONLY if vitals_state == 2 ; NONE if vitals_state == 3     # derived on read, never saved
```

The warmth axis uses the same rung semantics with the accumulator as its hold (D.4). **Both bounds** (reflexion pattern 4: the formula, not only its constants, is registered). **Floor:** on every *endogenous* path the earliest FAILED after the first cue (entry to STRAINED) is `HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(axis)` runs; the build assertions of D.8 hold it at or above `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`. **Cap:** a qualifying demand that holds for fewer than `HOLD_12` consecutive runs never raises a rung past STRAINED, and a terminal episode shorter than `HOLD_23(axis)` never reaches FAILED; on the two **counter axes** the FULL to WALK_ONLY edge recurs no more often than once per `HOLD_12` runs (a rung climbs only after a hold of that length and a descent resets it) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The warmth axis has no hold (its rung follows `abs(thermal_load)`, and `TL_HYST` is only 300), so the counter-axis bound is **false for warmth**: a seat moving back and forth through a doorway between a lethal-cold room and a COMFORT room cycled `thermal_load` between 3700 and 4000 (8 runs of gain at 40, 25 runs of relax at 12: a 33-run period), and the Controller's stagger would have fired about every 3.3 s against its documented 10 s floor. The bound for warmth is the **re-entry refractory** of D.4: after the warmth rung leaves 2 or more it may not climb from 1 to 2 again for `HOLD_12` runs. *Both bounds hold for endogenous demand traces; the integrity axis under `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 is excluded from the cap bound and tested by AC-SE-24* (the registry entry carries the same exception in its expression).

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `rung`, `rung'` | int | 0..3 | `suit.state`, per axis (persisted) | OK, STRAINED, CRITICAL, FAILED |
| `hold`, `hold'` | int runs | 0..`HOLD_23(axis)` | `suit.state` (persisted: a restore must not reset the floor) | consecutive qualifying runs |
| `d` | int | 0..2 | the axis demand table | breath: SE-19; integrity: 2 below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, 1 below `SUIT_HP_OK`, else 0 |
| `t` | bool | 0..1 | the axis | breath: source NONE; integrity: `integrity_bp` below `SUIT_HP_FAIL` |
| `HOLD_12` | int runs | 100 | derived (D.8) | `ceil(RUNG_MIN_TICKS / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS)` |
| `HOLD_23(axis)` | int runs | breath 150, integrity 100, warmth 200 | derived (D.8) | the CRITICAL to FAILED hold |
| `exo_pending` | int | 0..1 | `suit.state`, set by an EXOGENOUS `apply_suit_damage` | SE-23 |
| `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` | int | 0..1 (1) | knob | 0 restores the uniform limiter |

**Output Range:** `rung'` in 0..3, rises by at most 1 per run on the limited path; `vitals_state` in 0..3; the cap is a function of it. **Registry candidate: yes (formula, both bounds with the CK-17 exception).**

**Example A (DOOR-BREACH, reserve full; steps as in D.1):**

| Step | Event | Breath rung | Cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| 25 | THIN: `d` 1 | 1 (first cue) | FULL |
| 64 | HYPOXIC: RESERVE, valve click; reserve falls 300 per run | 1 | FULL |
| 663 | reserve at or below `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` (90,000): `d` 2, hold starts | 1 | FULL |
| 762 | hold reaches `HOLD_12` (about 60,000 left, 20 s) | 2 | WALK_ONLY (stagger) |
| 964 | reserve 0, ANOXIC or worse: NONE, `t` | 2 | WALK_ONLY |
| 1113 | `t` held `HOLD_23(breath)` 150 | 3 | NONE (DOWNED) |
| 1413 | the Controller's `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` (300 runs) | DEAD | restart from the autosave |

First cue to FAILED: 1088 runs = 108.8 s. The Player Fantasy timeline rounds these to a tenth of a second.

**Example B (DOOR-BREACH, reserve 0):** step 25 rung 1; step 64 HYPOXIC with LOW and reserve 0: GASP, `d` 2, hold starts; step 163 the 100th qualifying run: rung 2 (WALK_ONLY, stagger); the room is ANOXIC from step 116 so `t` is true; from step 164 the terminal hold counts 150 runs: rung 3 at step 313; DEAD at step 613. First cue to FAILED: 288 runs = 28.8 s, which is at least `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` (20 s). A rescue at any step before 613 descends in one run (the room regains air: `d` falls, the rung follows at once, and the Controller checks the cap first at `death_tick`).

**Example C (O2-ROOM, suited seat: the GASP window, CK-1(c); `lsg` D.15: THIN 870, HYPOXIC 2163, ANOXIC 3646 steps for an unsuited crew):**

| Step | Event | Breath rung | Cap |
|---|---|---|---|
| 870 | THIN: first cue | 1 | FULL |
| 2163 | HYPOXIC: reserve engages (the room stops being drawn from) | 1 | FULL |
| 2762 | reserve at or below the warn threshold: `d` 2 | 1 | FULL |
| 2861 | hold done | 2 | WALK_ONLY |
| 3063 | reserve 0; room `p_o2` about 128 dkPa, still HYPOXIC: GASP (draw resumes), `d` 2, `t` false | 2 | WALK_ONLY |
| about 4123 | the room reaches ANOXIC again (1060 steps of resumed draw): NONE, `t` | 2 | WALK_ONLY |
| about 4272 | `t` held 150 runs | 3 | NONE (DOWNED) |

First cue to FAILED about 3402 runs = 340 s. For the whole GASP window (about 106 s) the seat sits at WALK_ONLY on room air; it is never FAILED while the room still holds air in a HYPOXIC band. Because a RESERVE seat stops drawing for `RESERVE_S`, the room's own clock shifts later (ANOXIC at about 4123 rather than the unsuited fixture's 3646): the Graph owner may add a suited O2-ROOM variant (OQ-12).

**Example D (2 s transient):** a door cycles a seat at reserve 0 through a 2 s DECOMP: `d` 2 for 20 runs, the hold resets when it ends, the axis stays at STRAINED and never loses mobility (the cap bound). **Example E (friendly harm, CK-2, the default, per-step model; `HARM_BP_PER_STEP` 500 at Interaction's designed values `HARM_TOTAL_BP` 10000 over `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS` 120, provisional):** `integrity_bp` after step n is 10000 − 500n: step 7 (6500) rung 1; step 11 (4500) rung 2, the suit leaks, the Controller staggers; step 16 (2000) rung 3 and cap NONE, DOWNED at 1.6 s into the 2 s act; DEAD 30 s later unless a crewmate raises the integrity (VS-B). With the knob at 0: the same 10000 bp reaches rung 1 at once, rung 2 after `HOLD_12` and rung 3 after `HOLD_23(integrity)`: FAILED no sooner than 200 runs = 20 s.

### D.4 `thermal_load_step`

The `thermal_load_step` formula is defined as *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (FREEZING above the shifted onset relaxes; the warmth re-entry refractory `rf` is new):

```
insul_eff_mc = INSUL_MC * min(integrity_bp, SUIT_LEAK_HP) / SUIT_LEAK_HP     # knee at the seam-jet threshold: full above it (CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26)
onset_cold   = COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC - insul_eff_mc            # the shifted cold onset
e_cold       = max(0, onset_cold - T) ;  e_heat = max(0, T - HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC)       # raw T, mC; at most one is non-zero
e            = e_cold + e_heat
gain         = 0 if e == 0 else TL_GAIN_MIN + (TL_GAIN_MAX - TL_GAIN_MIN) * min(e, TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC) / TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC
relax        = TL_RELAX_COMFORT if band_t == COMFORT ; TL_RELAX_COOL if COOL ; TL_RELAX_EDGE if band_t in {FROST, FREEZING, HOT} ; 0 otherwise    # FREEZING relaxes only when e == 0 (above the shifted onset); DEEP_COLD and SCORCH lie past it, so e == 0 does not occur there
L'           = clamp(L - gain, -TL_MAX, TL_MAX) if e_cold > 0 ; clamp(L + gain, -TL_MAX, TL_MAX) if e_heat > 0 ; L - sign(L) * min(abs(L), relax) if e == 0
cand         = 3 if abs(L') >= TL_MAX else 2 if abs(L') >= TL_CRITICAL else 1 if (band_t in {FROST, FREEZING, DEEP_COLD, HOT, SCORCH} or abs(L') >= TL_STRAINED) else 0
floor_r      = 3 if (rung == 3 and abs(L') >= TL_MAX - TL_HYST) else 2 if (rung >= 2 and abs(L') >= TL_CRITICAL - TL_HYST) else 0
target       = max(cand, floor_r)
if rung <= 1 and rf > 0 : target = min(target, 1)                               # re-entry refractory (design review): no 1 -> 2 for HOLD_12 runs after leaving rung >= 2
rung'        = min(target, rung + 1) if target > rung else target
rf'          = HOLD_12 if (rung >= 2 and rung' < 2) else max(0, rf - 1)
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `T` | int mC | any | Graph `compartment(cid).T` (raw) | compared only against the shifted onset and `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, by name |
| `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`, `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` | int mC | registry | Graph | names only; no literal, no heat shift |
| `INSUL_MC` | int mC | 0-10000 (5000) | knob | the cold-onset shift of an intact suit |
| `integrity_bp`, `SUIT_LEAK_HP` | int bp | D.7 | `suit.state`, knob | a leaking suit insulates less (linear below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, full at and above it, so integrity STRAINED has no thermal effect) |
| `TL_GAIN_MIN`, `TL_GAIN_MAX` | int load per run | 8, 40 | knobs | gain just past the onset and at saturation |
| `TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC` | int mC | 20000 | knob | depth past the onset at which gain saturates |
| `TL_RELAX_COMFORT`, `TL_RELAX_COOL`, `TL_RELAX_EDGE` | int load per run | 12, 6, 3 | knobs | recovery by band when there is no gain (`TL_RELAX_EDGE` also in FREEZING above the shifted onset); none in DEEP_COLD, SCORCH *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `L` | int | `-TL_MAX`..`TL_MAX` | `suit.state` (persisted) | signed thermal load, cold negative; **never shown as a number** |
| `rf` (`rf_warmth`) *(new)* | int runs | 0..`HOLD_12` | `suit.state` (persisted) | the warmth re-entry refractory: runs left in which the rung may not climb from 1 to 2 after it fell from 2 or more; first entries are never delayed |
| `TL_STRAINED`, `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_MAX`, `TL_HYST` | int | 800, 4000, 12000, 300 | knobs | rung thresholds on `abs(L)` and the descent hysteresis |

**Output Range:** `gain` is 0 or 8..40; `L'` in −12000..12000, changes by at most 40 per run (gain) or 12 (relax). Minimum latencies: from the first gain run to `abs(L) >= TL_CRITICAL` 100 runs (10 s, equal to `HOLD_12`); to `TL_MAX` 300 runs (30 s). Maximum (slowest gain) 500 and 1500 runs. `HOLD_23(warmth)` = `(TL_MAX − TL_CRITICAL) / TL_GAIN_MAX` = 200 runs; `THERMAL_FAIL_S` = 20 s is that in seconds, a derived value. Recovery from `L` = −4000 in COMFORT: 334 runs (33 s). **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Example 1 (FREEZE-BREACH, `lsg` D.15: V 30, `T_amb` −30 °C, `τ` 4615; the shifted onset is reached at step 7428, 12.4 min).** Past the onset, `e(D) = 10000 × (1 − exp(−D / 4615))` mC, so `gain(D) = 24 − 16 exp(−D / 4615)` and `L(D) = 24 D − 73,840 (1 − exp(−D / 4615))`: `abs(L)` reaches 4000 at D about 456 runs (CRITICAL, +46 s) and 12000 at D about 1210 (FAILED, +121 s).
