The cue ladder precedes it: FROST at 3.9 min (shiver and rime from the band alone), FREEZING at 9.3 min (gain 0 and `TL_RELAX_EDGE` relax between that boundary and the shifted onset, so the load, still 0 here, only falls), CRITICAL at about 13.2 min, FAILED at about 14.4 min; first cue to FAILED about 10.5 min, far above the floor. **Example 2 (deep cold):** `e` at or above `TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC` gives gain 40: CRITICAL at run 100, FAILED at run 300 exactly. **Example 3 (insulation):** a room 3000 mC below `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` with an intact suit has `onset_cold` 5000 mC lower, so `e_cold` is 0 and the seat is only STRAINED; the same room with `integrity_bp` 2500 (`SUIT_HP_FAIL`, still CRITICAL) has `insul_eff_mc` 2500, `e_cold` 500 and gain 8 per run, lethal in 1500 runs; at `integrity_bp` 6000 (STRAINED, patched) or 3500 the same room stays harmless (`insul_eff_mc` 5000 and 3500, `e_cold` 0), because insulation only falls once the suit leaks. **Example 4 (heat):** a HOT room 5000 mC past `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`: gain 16, CRITICAL at 250 runs, FAILED 500 runs later; SCORCH saturates the gain (40): CRITICAL at 10 s, FAILED at 30 s. **Example 5 (FROST soak):** a FROST room never gains and relaxes 3 per run, so a twenty-minute soak ends at STRAINED, cap FULL.

### D.5 `cell_drain`

The `cell_drain` formula is defined as:

```
lit_prev    = emitter.state.lit on the seat's beam as of T-1
cell'       = max(0, cell_ticks - GRAPH_STEP_TICKS) if (lit_prev and CELL_ENABLED) else cell_ticks
force_off(beam_id) when lit_prev and CELL_ENABLED and cell' == 0                       # E5, Class B; lands before the same tick's emitter.place
charge(player_id) = cell'                                                               # read by toggle_beam.validate; 0 rejects a turn-ON CHARGE_EMPTY
charge_band = 0 EMPTY if cell' == 0 ; 1 WARN if cell' <= CELL_WARN_S * TICK_RATE ; 2 OK
charge_advise = (cell' <= CELL_ADVISE_S * TICK_RATE) and (cell' > CELL_WARN_S * TICK_RATE)     # silent step on the reading only: no audio, no mark, not in SHARED_SUMMARY
units_req   = ceil(cell_deficit_ticks * CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000)                     # M16 amount on a BATTERY port (D.9)
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cell_ticks` | int ticks | 0..`CELL_MAX_TICKS` (72,000) | `suit.state` | lit-beam time remaining |
| `lit_prev` | bool | 0..1 | Emitter State read view, as of T-1 | whether the beam was lit |
| `CELL_ENABLED` | int | 0..1 (1) | knob | 0 makes the formula the identity (the fallback) |
| `CELL_WARN_S` | int s | 10-30 (20) | knob | the low-charge tell, inside P2's window |
| `CELL_ADVISE_S` *(new)* | int s | 60-300 (120), above `CELL_WARN_S` (A14) | knob | a long-threshold, silent step on the charge reading: a 20 s warning on a 20-minute resource is too short to plan around, so the reading's zone band steps 120 s early with no sound and no mark (the mark discipline of SE-30 is untouched) |
| `CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK` | int dJ per 1000 lit ticks | 500-8000 (2000) | knob | the BATTERY stock unit is dJ and M16 is legal on a BATTERY `stock` (Graph LS-18, confirmed 2026-10-27) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |

**Output Range:** `cell'` in 0..72,000; one run changes it by 0, −6 or the credit. A full cell is 12,000 runs = 1200 s of lit beam; the warn window is 1,200 ticks = 200 runs. A full refill costs `72 × CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK` = 144,000 stock units at the default, which is 4.0 % of one `BATT_CELL` (3,600,000 dJ, `lsg` charge model) (the unit is dJ, confirmed); the game-designer's unitless 40 would have made the refill free, which is why the default is derived from a 12 W lamp. Whether a refill that costs about 4 % of one cell is felt as the Pillar 3 cost is OQ-44 (12 dJ per step × 1000 / 6 = 2000 dJ per 1000 ticks). **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Example:** full cell, beam lit continuously: the warn edge is crossed at run 11,800 (cell 1,200), `force_off` fires once at run 12,000, and a channel lit only by the beam aborts within 5 ticks. **Sampling note (accepted, SE-8):** the hook reads the end state of tick T−1 once per 6 ticks; any lit span of 6 or more consecutive ticks contains exactly one sample point, a span of 4 or fewer can avoid every sample, so a toggling player under- or over-drains by up to 5 ticks per ON/OFF pair; the Controller adapter holds `BEAM_TOGGLE_MIN_INTERVAL_TICKS` (10 ticks, adopted by the Controller, PC-5), so every input-driven lit span contains a sample point (AC-SE-47, spike).

### D.6 `held_mass_cue`

The `held_mass_cue` formula is defined as:

```
carried_cls = weight_class(instance_mass_g(carried instance)) if a module or curio is carried else LIGHT
stowed_cls  = weight_class(stowed_mass_g)                      # stowed_mass_g = sum over stowed stacks of mass_g(def_id) * count; reserved parts excluded (SE-41)
stowed_term = stowed_cls if stowed_cls >= STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS else LIGHT
cue_load    = max(carried_cls, stowed_term)                    # 0 LIGHT, 1 MEDIUM, 2 HEAVY; in-hand tools excluded
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `weight_class` | registry formula | LIGHT, MEDIUM, HEAVY | Item DB (`WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G`, `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` by name) | never restated here |
| `instance_mass_g`, `mass_g(def_id)` | int g | 1..500000 | Item DB (static) | per the registry |
| `stowed_mass_g` | int g | 0..45,200 | derived from the stowed counts | the maximum with the plate; 15,200 g without it |
| `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` | enum | MEDIUM or HEAVY (MEDIUM) | knob (CK-8) | the lowest stowed class that sounds |

**Output Range:** {0, 1, 2}, derived on read, never saved. Only the 30,000 g `part_hull_plate` (or a heavy carried module) reaches HEAVY from stowed stock (the maximum without the plate is below `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G`), which is the Item DB's binding trigger. **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Masking table (the cost of `max`, stated):**

| carried \ stowed | LIGHT | MEDIUM | HEAVY |
|---|---|---|---|
| none or LIGHT | 0 | 1 | 2 |
| MEDIUM | 1 | 1 | 2 |
| HEAVY | 2 | 2 | 2 |

**Example:** the start kit stows two glowsticks (0.4 kg): 0. Add four `part_duct_coupling` and four `part_cable_splice` (5,600 g, at or above `WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G`): 1. Add the plate (35,600 g): 2. Carry `mod_door_motor` (9,000 g) with only the glowsticks stowed: 1; carry `mod_scrubber` (40,000 g): 2; carry the door motor while the plate is stowed: 2, so the plate masks the carried class and the VS-A field check is blind to a carried item while it is on the back (OQ-8). With `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` HEAVY the MEDIUM column reads 0, 1, 2 on the stowed side only.

### D.7 `suit_integrity_damage`

The `suit_integrity_damage` formula is defined as:

```
burn        = SCORCH_BURN_BP_PER_RUN if band_t == SCORCH else 0                          # endogenous
arc         = ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN if (mode 2 SHORT descriptor at top severity in cid and dist(seat, anchor) <= ARC_RADIUS_PX) else 0   # endogenous
hits        = sum of apply_suit_damage amounts landed since the last run                   # clamp, never reject; EXOGENOUS sets exo_pending (SE-23)
integrity_1 = clamp(integrity_bp - burn - arc - hits, 0, SUIT_HP_FULL)
integrity'  = max(integrity_1, min(integrity_1 + patch_bp, SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP)) if patch_bp > 0 else integrity_1     # patch_integrity: never lowers, never past the cap
leak        = LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN if integrity_bp < SUIT_LEAK_HP else 0                       # consumed by D.2
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `integrity_bp` | int bp | 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL` (10000) | `suit.state` | on the Graph's basis-point scale, with Suit's own thresholds |
| `SUIT_HP_OK`, `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, `SUIT_HP_FAIL`, `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` | int bp | 7000, 5000, 2500, 6000 | knobs | `SUIT_HP_FAIL < SUIT_LEAK_HP < SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP < SUIT_HP_OK` |
| `SCORCH_BURN_BP_PER_RUN` | int bp | 0-10 (4) | knob | 0 makes the burn dormant |
| `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN`, `ARC_RADIUS_PX` | int bp, int px | 10-25 (20), 24-96 (48) | knobs | the arc landing (LS-14 anchor) |
| `hits`, `patch_bp` | int bp | 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL` | mutators | `apply_suit_damage` (Interaction `begin_harm`, Horror); `patch_integrity` (Interaction self-patch) |

**Output Range:** `integrity'` in 0..10000; `leak` 0 or `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN`. A patch can never reach `SUIT_HP_OK`: a patched suit is permanently STRAINED (a scar, not a cure); only the death restore returns it to OK. **Registry candidate: yes (formula).**

**Example 1 (SCORCH burn, 4 bp per run):** below `SUIT_HP_OK` at run 751 (STRAINED), below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` at run 1251 (the suit leaks, `d` 2), CRITICAL after `HOLD_12` at run 1350, below `SUIT_HP_FAIL` at run 1876, FAILED `HOLD_23(integrity)` later at run 1975; in practice the warmth axis fails first (30 s in SCORCH), so integrity is the slow secondary axis. **Example 2 (arc, 20 bp per run):** 5000 to below 2500 takes 125 runs, at least `HOLD_12`, so the CRITICAL rung is reached while `integrity_bp` is still at or above `SUIT_HP_FAIL` (D.8 assertion A7). **Example 3 (repair):** `integrity_bp` 2500 and a requested patch of +5000: `min(7500, SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP)` = 6000, no leak, STRAINED for the rest of the run.

### D.8 `suit_capacities`

The `suit_capacities` formula is defined as:

```
RUNS_PER_S        = TICK_RATE / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS                                  # 10
RESERVE_MAX_MGU   = RESERVE_S * RUNS_PER_S * R_BREATH                              # 270,000
RESERVE_WARN_MGU  = RESERVE_WARN_S * RUNS_PER_S * R_BREATH                         # 90,000
CELL_MAX_TICKS    = CELL_S * TICK_RATE                                             # 72,000
HOLD_12           = ceil(RUNG_MIN_TICKS / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS)                        # 100
HOLD_23(breath)   = max(HOLD_12, HYPOX_FAIL_S * RUNS_PER_S)                        # 150
HOLD_23(integrity)= max(HOLD_12, INTEG_FAIL_S * RUNS_PER_S)                        # 100
HOLD_23(warmth)   = (TL_MAX - TL_CRITICAL) / TL_GAIN_MAX                           # 200
STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS = floor(STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS)               # 10 (D.12)
```

Build assertions, checked at boot (a failure fails boot): **A1** `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S >= W_MIN_WARNING_S`. **A2** `RUNG_MIN_TICKS >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S * TICK_RATE / 2`. **A3** `HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(axis) >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S * RUNS_PER_S` for each axis. **A4** `TL_CRITICAL / TL_GAIN_MAX >= HOLD_12` and `HOLD_23(warmth) >= HOLD_12`. **A5** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the leak is now in the bound)* `RESERVE_WARN_S * R_BREATH >= (HOLD_12 * GRAPH_STEP_TICKS / TICK_RATE + W_MIN_WARNING_S) * (R_BREATH + LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN)` and `RESERVE_WARN_S <= RESERVE_S / 2` (a leaking suit on the engaged reserve loses `R_BREATH + LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` per run, so the warn threshold must still leave `W_MIN_WARNING_S` after the hold: 9,000 >= 9,000 at the defaults, which is exactly tight). **A6** `SUIT_HP_FAIL < SUIT_LEAK_HP < SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP < SUIT_HP_OK`. **A7** `(SUIT_LEAK_HP - SUIT_HP_FAIL) / ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN >= HOLD_12`. **A8** `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT <= GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX` and `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES <= charges_max`. **A9** `CELL_WARN_S >= W_MIN_WARNING_S`. **A10** `TL_STRAINED < TL_CRITICAL - TL_HYST` and `TL_CRITICAL < TL_MAX - TL_HYST`. **A11 (I-SUPPLY, evaluated at the bound seat count)** `seats * GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX <= MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE`: 4 passes at one seat; at two or more seats a violation is a boot **warning** naming OQ-14 (the recorded CONFLICT: 16 at four seats), never a failure, so the evict-wane edge case is reachable and multi-seat fixtures boot; a multi-seat test fixture may instead set `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16 (inside its registry range 4-16). At one seat a violation fails boot. **A13** *(new, 2026-10-27 — design review)* `HOLD_23(warmth) * GRAPH_STEP_TICKS >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S * TICK_RATE`: the heat side has no body overlay (VA-C4), so its first cue the body can act on is the CRITICAL entry (the stagger and the mark), and the time from there to FAILED must itself meet the floor; at the defaults it is 200 runs = 20 s = `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` exactly, which A3 alone (measured from STRAINED, 300 runs) did not guard. **A14** *(new)* `CELL_ADVISE_S > CELL_WARN_S`. **A12** `offset(suit.vitals) == offset(graph.propagate.atmo)` — the kernel itself only *warns* about a never-co-running `after` pair (CR-3), so Suit adds its own failing check: offset 0 exists to give the body the Graph's same-tick atmosphere (P1) and a warning is easy to ignore. 

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `TICK_RATE` | int ticks/s | 60 | registry, kernel | simulation ticks per second |
| `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` | int ticks | 6 | registry, Graph | ticks per Graph step and per `suit.vitals` run |
| `RESERVE_S` | int s | 45-240 (90) | knob (Tuning Knobs) | seconds of engaged reserve breath |
| `RESERVE_WARN_S` | int s | 20-60 (30) | knob | reserve seconds left at which the breath axis demands CRITICAL; at most `RESERVE_S` / 2 |
| `R_BREATH` | int mGU per step per crew | 100-600 (300) | knob | breath draw per run |
| `CELL_S` | int s | 600-2400 (1200) | knob | seconds of lit beam in a full cell |
| `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` | int ticks | 300-900 (600) | knob | ticks a demand must hold before one rung advance |
| `HYPOX_FAIL_S` | int s | 10-30 (15) | knob | terminal hold of the breath axis for source NONE |
| `INTEG_FAIL_S` | int s | 10-60 (10) | knob | terminal hold of the integrity axis |
| `TL_MAX` | int load | 8000-24000 (12000) | knob | thermal-load ceiling |
| `TL_CRITICAL` | int load | 4000-8000 (4000) | knob | thermal CRITICAL threshold |
| `TL_GAIN_MAX` | int load per run | 20-40 (40) | knob | deepest-cold gain per run |
| `RUNS_PER_S` | int runs/s | 10 | derived | `TICK_RATE` / `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` |
| `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | int mGU | 45,000..1,440,000 (270,000) | derived | reserve capacity |
| `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` | int mGU | 20,000..360,000 (90,000) | derived | reserve level at the warn edge |
| `CELL_MAX_TICKS` | int ticks | 36,000..144,000 (72,000) | derived | cell capacity in lit ticks |
| `HOLD_12` | int runs | 50..150 (100) | derived | runs a demand must hold for the first rung step (rung 1 to 2) |
| `HOLD_23(breath)` | int runs | 100..300 (150) | derived | terminal hold of the breath axis |
| `HOLD_23(integrity)` | int runs | 100..600 (100) | derived | terminal hold of the integrity axis |
| `HOLD_23(warmth)` | int runs | at least `HOLD_12` by A4 (200) | derived | terminal hold of the warmth axis |

The build assertions A1 to A13 additionally read the other Tuning Knobs, each named in the assertion. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Feasible region of the coupled knobs** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; the review found the assertion set tight at the defaults, with two published ranges false and no sweep proving the Pillar 2 floor)*. The slack column is the margin at the defaults; 0 means the default sits exactly on the boundary.

| Assertion | Inequality at the defaults | Slack | Knobs it couples |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` 20 >= `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 | +10 s | `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` |
| A2 | `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` 600 >= 20 x 60 / 2 = 600 | 0 | `RUNG_MIN_TICKS`, `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` |
| A3 breath / integrity / warmth | 250 / 200 / 300 runs >= 200 | +50 / **0** / +100 | `HOLD_12`, `HYPOX_FAIL_S`, `INTEG_FAIL_S`, `TL_MAX`, `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_GAIN_MAX` |
| A4 | `TL_CRITICAL` / `TL_GAIN_MAX` = 100 >= `HOLD_12` 100; `HOLD_23(warmth)` 200 >= 100 | **0**; +100 | `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_GAIN_MAX`, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` |
| A5 (with the leak) | 30 x 300 = 9,000 >= 20 x 450 = 9,000; 30 <= 45 | **0**; +15 s | `RESERVE_WARN_S`, `RESERVE_S`, `R_BREATH`, `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN`, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` |
| A7 | (5000 - 2500) / 20 = 125 >= 100 | +25 runs | `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, `SUIT_HP_FAIL`, `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN`, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` |
| A9 | `CELL_WARN_S` 20 >= 10 | +10 s | `CELL_WARN_S` |
| A13 | `HOLD_23(warmth)` 200 runs = 20 s >= `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` 20 s | **0** | `TL_MAX`, `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_GAIN_MAX`, `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` |

**What the table forces.** (1) `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` is **pinned at 600 at the default values of the other knobs**: A2 sets its floor at `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` x 30 and A4 its ceiling at 6 x `TL_CRITICAL` / `TL_GAIN_MAX`, so the published range 300-900 is the knob's range, not its feasible region; it widens to [300, 600] with `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` 10, and to [600, 900] with `TL_CRITICAL` 6000. (2) `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` may not exceed `R_BREATH` x (`RESERVE_WARN_S` / (`HOLD_12` in seconds + `W_MIN_WARNING_S`) - 1) = 150 at `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 and 300 only at `RESERVE_WARN_S` 45 (its published range was 0-300, which failed the Pillar 2 floor above 150; narrowed to 0-150). (3) `INTEG_FAIL_S` cannot go below 10 s and `TL_MAX` cannot fall without raising `TL_CRITICAL` or lowering `TL_GAIN_MAX` by A13 and A4. (4) Any retune that leaves the table is a boot failure naming the assertion, which is the intended behaviour. The sweep is AC-SE-44b.

**Output Range:** each derived value is a positive int; `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` is 9 % of the home `O2_TANK` (3,000,000 mGU, 11.1 fills). Because the time knobs are the stored quantities, changing `R_BREATH` keeps the felt duration (AC-SE-8). **Registry candidate: no (derived helpers).** **Example:** `R_BREATH` 600 doubles `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` to 540,000 and leaves the reserve at 90 s; a saved reserve above the new maximum fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`, never clamps.

### D.9 `topup_credit`

The `topup_credit` formula is defined as:

```
reserve_deficit_mgu = RESERVE_MAX_MGU - reserve_mgu ;  cell_deficit_ticks = CELL_MAX_TICKS - cell_ticks      # the validate reads
O2_TANK port: granted = withdraw_store(node_id, reserve_deficit_mgu) ;  credited = min(granted, reserve_deficit_mgu)       # credit_reserve
BATTERY port: units_req = ceil(cell_deficit_ticks * CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000) ;  granted = withdraw_store(node_id, units_req)       # M16 in dJ (LS-18)
              credited_ticks = min(cell_deficit_ticks, granted * 1000 / CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK)                         # floor; credit_cell
              dust_units = granted - ceil(credited_ticks * CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000)        # stock-domain invariant: stock_debit = granted = ceil(credited_ticks * K / 1000) + dust_units; dust_units = 0 on a complete grant, < ceil(K / 1000) on a partial one
PASSIVE fallback (TOPUP_MODE PASSIVE, ROOM source only; dormant unless OQ-2 is ruled PASSIVE): extra = min(TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN, reserve_deficit_mgu) if band_o2 == NORMAL and (n_o2 - extra) / (V * GAS_SCALE) >= TOPUP_MIN_PP else 0 ;  draw_air(cid, R_BREATH + extra) ;  the credit is taken one run later by D.2 (extra_credit = granted_prev - min(granted_prev, pending_draw_mgu))
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reserve_mgu` | int mGU | 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | `suit.state` | the seat's current reserve |
| `cell_ticks` | int ticks | 0..`CELL_MAX_TICKS` | `suit.state` | the seat's current lit-beam time |
| `reserve_deficit_mgu` | int mGU | 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` | derived | `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` - `reserve_mgu`; read by Interaction's `validate` (`ALREADY_FULL` at 0) |
| `cell_deficit_ticks` | int ticks | 0..`CELL_MAX_TICKS` | derived | `CELL_MAX_TICKS` - `cell_ticks`; read by Interaction's `validate` (`ALREADY_FULL` at 0) |
| `granted` | int | 0..request | Graph M16 return (`withdraw_store`) | clamped to the node's stock |
| `n_o2` | int mGU | 0.. | Graph `compartment(cid)` | the compartment's O2 amount |
| `V` | int tiles | 1.. | Graph `compartment(cid)` | the compartment's volume |
| `GAS_SCALE` | int mGU per GU | 1000 | registry, Graph | gas amount scale |
| `CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK` | int dJ per 1000 lit ticks | 500-8000 (2000) | knob | the BATTERY stock unit is dJ (Graph LS-18 M16, confirmed 2026-10-27) |
| `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` | int mGU per run | 10-90 (30) | knob | PASSIVE only: the room-air credit per run |
| `TOPUP_MIN_PP` | int dkPa | 185-205 (195) | knob | PASSIVE only: the O2 partial-pressure floor the room must keep |

**Output Range:** a credit never exceeds the deficit; for an O2_TANK the stock falls by exactly the credit, for a BATTERY by `ceil(credited_ticks × CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000)` plus the partial-grant dust (at the default 2000 a partial grant of an odd number of units leaves 1 dust unit; a complete grant leaves none) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; a granted amount of 0 is applied-and-did-nothing — no token, no distinct cue, `credit_*` is not called; the sight-glass not falling and the reserve reading not rising are the answer (Interaction ruling, IA-12: a TAP has no deferred class). In the passive fallback the room's O2 falls by `extra`, so the cost is the room's, honestly. **Registry candidate: no (the V7 apply).** **Example:** a seat at reserve 60,000 has a deficit of 210,000 mGU, 7 % of the home `O2_TANK`; a half-empty cell (deficit 36,000 ticks) asks for 72,000 stock units and credits 36,000 ticks. Passive: a V = 8 room at 210 dkPa holds 1,680,000 mGU; `extra` 30 leaves 209 dkPa, above `TOPUP_MIN_PP`, so the reserve refills in 9000 runs (15 min) while breath plus `extra` (330) stays below the scrubber's per-step output (`R_SCR`).

### D.10 `suit_cue_levels`

The `suit_cue_levels` formula is defined as:

```
cold_side = band_t in {FROST, FREEZING, DEEP_COLD} or (thermal_load < 0 and band_t not in {HOT, SCORCH})     # residual cold load never draws rime or shiver in a hot room (P1)
cue_cold  = 3 if cold_side and rung_warmth >= 2 ; 2 if cold_side and rung_warmth == 1 ; 1 if band_t in {COOL, FROST, FREEZING, DEEP_COLD} ; else 0     # vapour 1, shiver and rime 1 at 2, rime 2 at 3
cue_fog   = min(rung_breath, 2)
breath_step = min(rung_breath, 2)   # the pace step; the air class drives the rate, the load layers never do (SE-28)
cue_breath  = {src: breath_src, air_class: 0 AIR_OK / 1 AIR_POOR / 2 AIR_NONE (D.1), pace_step: breath_step}     # the primary cue's level definition: ROOM open, RESERVE closed, GASP laboured, NONE gasp loop
breath_src_change_seq = (breath_src_change_seq + 1) mod 65536 on each run in which breath_src differs from the previous run     # the edge counter behind the valve click, engage and disengage
cue_load  = D.6
charge_band = D.5
charge_advise = D.5                    # the silent advisory step on the charge reading (no audio, no mark)
carried_class = 0 if no carried instance else 1 + weight_class(carried instance)
mark_axis = (rung_axis >= 2) for breath, warmth, integrity ;  mark_charge = (charge_band == 1)                # rung-based, never at STRAINED (SE-30)
leak_jet = integrity_bp < SUIT_LEAK_HP ;  back_frame = stowed_cls == HEAVY
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `band_t` | enum | COMFORT..SCORCH | Graph `compartment(cid)` | the occupant's thermal band |
| `rung_breath`, `rung_warmth`, `rung_integrity` | int | 0..3 | `suit.state` (D.3, D.4) | the three vitals rungs |
| `thermal_load` | int load | -`TL_MAX`..`TL_MAX` | `suit.state` (D.4) | signed accumulator; negative is the cold side |
| `integrity_bp` | int bp | 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL` | `suit.state` (D.7) | suit integrity |
| `cell_ticks` | int ticks | 0..`CELL_MAX_TICKS` | `suit.state` | lit-beam time remaining; feeds `charge_band` (D.5) |
| `stowed_cls` | int | 0..2 (LIGHT..HEAVY) | derived (D.6) | stowed weight class |
| `cue_load` | int | 0..2 | derived (D.6) | the load layer |
| `cue_cold` | int | 0..3 | output | vapour 1, shiver and rime 1 at 2, rime 2 at 3 |
| `cue_fog` | int | 0..2 | output | visor fog level |
| `breath_step` | int | 0..2 | output | the pace step |
| `cue_breath` *(new)* | tuple `{src 0..3, air_class 0..2, pace_step 0..2}` | — | output (derived on read) | the breath cadence's level definition: the primary cue now has one, and the presenter and audio read it from `view()` |
| `breath_src_change_seq` *(new)* | int | 0..65535 | `suit.state` (persisted, SE-38) | an edge counter, +1 on each source change; the valve click plays when a presenter sees it differ from its last-seen value, and a presenter adopts it silently on the first frame after a restore |
| `charge_advise` *(new)* | bool | 0..1 | output (D.5) | cell at or below `CELL_ADVISE_S` and above `CELL_WARN_S` |
| `charge_band` | int | 0..2 | output (D.5) | EMPTY, WARN, OK |
| `carried_class` | int | 0..3 | output | 0 none, else 1 + `weight_class` of the carried instance |
| `mark_axis`, `mark_charge` | bool | 0..1 | output | the warning marks; rung-based, never at STRAINED (SE-30) |
| `leak_jet`, `back_frame` | bool | 0..1 | output, derived on read | the seam jet and the HEAVY back-frame |

**Output Range:** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `cold_side` is gated on `band_t`, `cue_breath`, `breath_src_change_seq` and `charge_advise` added)* `cue_cold` 0..3, `cue_fog` 0..2, `breath_step` 0..2, `cue_load` 0..2, `charge_band` 0..2, `carried_class` 0..3; all derived on read from the last committed block, never saved, never a function of Sanity, Attention or a filtered value (SE-25). The heat side has no body cue (SE-26). **Registry candidate: no.** **Example:** a seat in a FROST room with `thermal_load` −1200 and the warmth rung at 1: `cold_side` true, `cue_cold` 2 (shiver and rime level 1); the same seat past the shifted onset after 100 runs of gain 40 (`thermal_load` −4000): rung 2, `cue_cold` 3, the warning mark beside the warmth glyph.

**Normative cue table (the single table of record for cue levels, witnesses and captions)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: cue levels had been restated in five or six places and had already drifted; the States and Transitions cue table, the SE-26 classification table and the Visual/Audio event table now restate this one, and where they differ this one wins)*. Columns are the cue's level definition, its audio witness (names only; Adaptive Audio & Tells owns the sounds), its sprite or overlay witness, its HUD witness, its caption id (the id is published here; the text, the localisation and the implementation are the captions policy's, AA-41, Alpha) and its non-audio witness, which is what a deaf or hard-of-hearing player has. Caption ids are `cap.suit.<event>`; a caption fires on the same edge as its cue and on no other, and never for an Attention, tell or Horror event.

| Cue | Level definition | Audio | Sprite / overlay | HUD | Caption id | Non-audio witness |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Breath cadence | `cue_breath` `{src, air_class, pace_step}`: ROOM open, RESERVE closed, GASP laboured, NONE gasp loop; `pace_step` = `min(rung_breath, 2)` | breath steps (open, thin, closed, ragged, gasp) | vapour puff on the exhale (cold air only) | — | `cap.suit.breath.thin` (ROOM, pace 1), `cap.suit.breath.closed` (RESERVE), `cap.suit.breath.ragged` (pace 2), `cap.suit.breath.gasp` (NONE) | visor fog (rung 1+); the reserve reading; nothing in warm NORMAL air by design |
| Valve click | an edge of `breath_src_change_seq`: engage (ROOM to RESERVE), disengage (RESERVE to ROOM) | valve click | the fog level step on the same frame | the reserve reading goes live / falls | `cap.suit.valve.engage`, `cap.suit.valve.disengage` | the fog step and the reserve reading on the same frame |
| Visor fog | `cue_fog` 0..2 | — (the breath cadence carries it) | visor strip, two levels | — | none (visual) | itself |
| Breath vapour | `cue_cold` at or above 1 | exhale | puff | — | none (visual) | itself |
| Shiver | `cue_cold` at or above 2, idle | suit rattle while moving | `idle_shiver` frames | warmth zone band | `cap.suit.shiver` | the idle frames and the rime |
| Rime | `cue_cold` 2 (level 1) and 3 (level 2) | — | rime on shell plates | warmth zone band; mark at CRITICAL | none (visual) | itself |
| Stagger, walk-only, DOWNED | `locomotion_cap` edges (the Controller draws) | stagger gasp | stagger / down frames | mark at CRITICAL | `cap.suit.stagger`, `cap.suit.down` | the animation and the mark (the stagger is audio-first, not audio-only) |
| Seam jet | `leak_jet` (`integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`) | seam hiss | jet particles | mark at integrity CRITICAL | `cap.suit.seam_hiss` | the jet |
| Load | `cue_load` 0 / 1 / 2 (D.6) | breath depth and creak layer | HEAVY back-frame (stowed class 2 only) | none (no weight readout) | `cap.suit.load.medium`, `cap.suit.load.heavy` | HEAVY: the back-frame; MEDIUM: **none** (exception E1, OQ-36) |
| Cell low | `charge_band` WARN (at `CELL_WARN_S`) | cell whine | beam untouched | mark beside the charge glyph | `cap.suit.cell.low` | the mark |
| Cell advisory | `charge_advise` (at `CELL_ADVISE_S`) | none, silent by design | — | the charge reading's zone band steps | none | the reading's band |
| Beam dark | `force_off` at zero cell | the Emitter's switch click | the cone vanishes in one tick | charge glyph empty with the mark | `cap.suit.cell.empty` | the cone vanishing |
| Heat rungs | warmth rung, heat side (no body overlay, VA-C4) | pack fan whirr, rising with the rung | none | warmth zone band; mark at CRITICAL | `cap.suit.heat.fan` | the reading and the mark only (**exception E2**, OQ-20) |

**Accessibility rules recorded with the table** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; items (2) and (3) are USER DECISIONS, OQ-40 and OQ-37)*. (1) The **user-accepted exception list** is, so far, E1 (stowed MEDIUM load has no non-audio witness, OQ-36) and E2 (heat STRAINED to CRITICAL is carried by the reading, the mark and the room, OQ-20); a body-cue event that has neither a non-audio witness nor an exception is a defect (AC-SE-91), and the list needs the user's acceptance before it is final. (2) **Haptics.** If a tactile channel is ever added, it may be driven only by the player's own true body-cue events in this table (valve click, stagger, cell low, the load step, the heat rung) and never by Attention, a tell, a breach or Horror; haptics driven only by the player's own true body state are **not** Attention indicators under the standing user decision, because their only source is the player's own body. This needs the user's confirmation and a matching line in Adaptive Audio's AA-42 (today "none at MVP"). (3) `suit.alarm` (OQ-37) is the creative-director's recommendation for MVP and the user's call.

### D.11 `suit_tell_floor`

The `suit_tell_floor` formula is defined as:

```
floor_runs(axis)  = HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(axis)                                         # earliest FAILED after the first cue, endogenous paths
fair(axis)        = floor_runs(axis) * GRAPH_STEP_TICKS >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S * TICK_RATE >= W_MIN_WARNING_S * TICK_RATE
traverse_s        = tiles * TILE_PX / (V * TICK_RATE) <= RESERVE_S / 2             # authored vacuum traverse; V = V_WALK, or V_CARRY when a module is carried
```

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `HOLD_12` | int runs | 50..150 (100) | D.8 | first-rung hold |
| `HOLD_23(axis)` | int runs | 100..600 | D.8 | terminal hold per axis |
| `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` | int s | 10-30 (20) | knob | Suit's own Pillar 2 floor |
| `W_MIN_WARNING_S` | int s | 10 | registry, Graph | the Graph's fairness floor |
| `TILE_PX` | int px | 48 | registry, Pipeline | pixels per tile |
| `V_WALK` | int px per tick | 2 | registry, Controller | the CRITICAL cap is walk-only, so a traverse is timed at walk speed |
| `V_CARRY` | int px per tick | 1 | registry, Controller | speed while a module is carried |
| `V` | int px per tick | `V_WALK` or `V_CARRY` | derived | `V_CARRY` when a module is carried |
| `tiles` | int tiles | authored path length | Derelict Authoring | the authored vacuum traverse |
| `RESERVE_S` | int s | 45-240 (90) | knob | the traverse must fit in half the reserve |

**Output Range:** breath 250 runs (25 s), integrity 200 (20 s), warmth at least 300 (30 s), each at or above `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` 20 s; an authored traverse of at most 112 tiles walking or 56 carrying. **Registry candidate: no (restates D.3's floor; the authoring bound is exported to Derelict Authoring).** **Example (the death-fairness sweep, AC-SE-20):** DOOR-BREACH reserve full 108.8 s, reserve 0 28.8 s; suited O2-ROOM 340 s; FREEZE-BREACH 10.5 min; SCORCH warmth 30 s minimum from the first cue; every value is at or above 20 s. Exogenous sources are held to AC-SE-41, not to this sweep.

### D.12 `pressure_drop_stagger` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: added; closes the BLOCKING finding that the only live external-stagger trigger had no formula, no persisted state and no rule for compartment changes or restore)*

The `pressure_drop_stagger` formula is defined as:

```
IDX(band_p)   = 0 NORMAL, 1 LOW, 2 DECOMP, 3 VACUUM                       # higher = lower pressure; EXPOSED seat: VACUUM (3) by rule (SE-3)
W             = floor(STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS)             # STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS: 60 / 6 = 10 (range 5..20)
SAT           = W + 1                                                      # "older than the window"
# per bound seat, once per run, after the room is resolved (SE-39 step 2). Not evaluated for a DEAD seat; frozen in HOLD_ROOM (IN_POD), where `prev_cid` is set to NONE on every run so that leaving the pod is a re-anchor.
p             = IDX(band_p) ;  cid = the occupant's compartment id (EXTERIOR for an EXPOSED seat)
if cid != prev_cid :                                                       # a crossing, the first run after a bind, leaving IN_POD (prev_cid is NONE there)
    p_age0 = 0 if p == 0 else SAT ;  p_age1 = 0 if p <= 1 else SAT ;  fire = false     # re-anchor: the new room's history starts now; a crossing never counts
else :
    p_age0 = 0 if p == 0 else min(SAT, p_age0 + 1)                         # runs since the band index was 0
    p_age1 = 0 if p <= 1 else min(SAT, p_age1 + 1)                         # runs since the band index was 0 or 1
    fire   = (p == 2 and p_age0 <= W) or (p == 3 and p_age1 <= W)          # a rise of >= 2 against a sample no older than W runs (inclusive)
    if fire : p_age0 = SAT ; p_age1 = SAT                                  # one stagger per event; the consumed samples cannot fire again
prev_cid      = cid
fire  ->  submit SYSTEM stagger {actor}   (the result, including NOT_STAGGERABLE, is ignored; never retried)
```

**Why two ages and not one reference.** A single stored reference band cannot be exact when the oldest sample leaves the window: a seat that sat at NORMAL until run 105, was LOW from 106 and reaches DECOMP at 112 has fallen two bands inside 7 runs, yet a reference that was refreshed at run 111 reads LOW and sees one band. Four band values need only two ages (the run-distance to the last NORMAL and to the last NORMAL-or-LOW), which makes the window exact at its boundary; the review's proposed `band_p_ref` / `ticks_since_drop` pair is replaced by `p_age0` / `p_age1` for that reason.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `band_p` | enum | NORMAL, LOW, DECOMP, VACUUM | Graph `compartment(cid)`; VACUUM by rule for an EXPOSED seat | the occupant's pressure band, hysteresis inherited (`H_P`); Suit adds none |
| `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` | int ticks | 30-120 (60) | knob | the window, in ticks; `W` is its floor in runs |
| `W` (`STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS`) | int runs | 5..20 (10) | derived (D.8) | "inside the window" is **inclusive**: a low sample exactly `W` runs old still counts, one of `W` + 1 runs does not |
| `p_age0`, `p_age1` | int runs | 0..`SAT` | `suit.state` (persisted, SE-38) | runs since the index was 0, and since it was 0 or 1; `SAT` means "none inside the window" |
| `cid`, `prev_cid` | int | any compartment id; NONE | Graph `compartment_at`; `prev_cid` is a rebuilt `prev_*` copy | a change of `cid` re-anchors and never fires |

**Output Range:** at most one `stagger` per fall; `fire` is false on every run in which `cid` changed. A one-band step (0-1, 1-2, 2-3) never fires. A fall of 0 to 2, 0 to 3 or 1 to 3 inside the window fires once on the run the index reaches the lower pressure, and a continuing fall to the next band does not fire again (the consumed ages are `SAT`); a recovery to a lower index and a new fall inside the window is a new event. Cost: two integer compares and two stores per seat per run. **Registry candidate: no (the registry's `stagger` entry already names the trigger by rule, SE-35).**

**Example 1 (the locked DOOR-BREACH fixture: it does not fire at the default).** `band_p` is NORMAL to step 55, LOW from step 56 and DECOMP from step 143 (D.1). At step 143 the last NORMAL sample is 88 runs old, so `p_age0` has saturated at `SAT` 11 and `fire` is false; VACUUM at step 360 finds the last NORMAL-or-LOW sample 218 runs old: no fire. The breach's stagger comes from the ladder's cap edge (breath CRITICAL, D.3 Example A), not from this trigger. To fire on DOOR-BREACH the window would have to be at least 88 runs = 528 ticks, far outside the knob's range 30-120, so the D.12 trigger is, at the default, a rare-event cue for a decompression that crosses two bands in one second (a hull rupture rather than a door-sized opening); OQ-38 carries the question of whether that is the intended weight. **Example 2 (window boundary, synthetic trace, same compartment).** A sample at index 0 on run `m`, then index 2 on run `m` + 9: `p_age0` 9, fires. On run `m` + 10 (`W`): `p_age0` 10, fires. On run `m` + 11: `p_age0` saturates at 11, no stagger. The same three cases hold for index 1 followed by index 3 with `p_age1`, and at `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` 30 (`W` 5) and 120 (`W` 20) with the boundary at `m` + 5 and `m` + 20. **Example 3 (a crossing).** A seat leaves a NORMAL room (index 0) for a room already at DECOMP (index 2): `cid` changed, the ages re-anchor to `p_age0` `SAT` and `p_age1` `SAT`, no stagger; the breath ladder carries the rest (the reserve engages, SE-4). The same seat standing in that room when its bands fall 0 to 2 inside 10 runs is staggered once. **Example 4 (continuing fall).** Index 0 to 2 on run `n` (fires, ages set to `SAT`), then 3 on run `n` + 3: `p_age1` is `SAT`, no second stagger; had the index recovered to 1 on run `n` + 2 and fallen to 3 on `n` + 3, `p_age1` is 1 and a second event fires.

## Edge Cases

*Each entry is "If [condition]: [exact outcome]. [rationale]" and names the rule or formula it exercises. Step counts are Suit runs (= Graph steps) after a fixture's t = 0.*

### Respiration, the reserve and the room

- **If four seats share one compartment and the room holds 450 mGU of O2 on a run in which all four request `R_BREATH`** (SE-5, D.2): the Graph grants in ascending `(compartment id, crew id)`: 300, 150, 0, 0; seats 3 and 4 set `pending_draw_mgu` 300 and debit it from their own reserves one run late (a seat with reserve 0 sets `breath_starved`, which raises its demand to 2 for that run). Suit submits in ascending PlayerId so the order is deterministic; the room is ANOXIC within a run, which switches every seat to RESERVE or NONE, so the lag never persists.
- **If M15 returns its grant synchronously, or only at the next run's phase 0** (SE-5, OQ-12): the ledger is written and consumed in the same run (no lag) or one run later (0.1 s); source selection is identical either way because SE-4 reads only the enums and the reserve. The rule was written to hold under both readings so the Graph's answer cannot change Suit.
- **If a seat requests from a room whose `n_o2` is already 0 on that run** (D.2): granted 0, the full `R_BREATH` is the shortfall, debited from the reserve next run; the Graph's next evaluation reads ANOXIC, so the source is RESERVE (reserve above 0) or NONE (reserve 0) from then on. A seat can therefore lose at most one run of breath to the lag, never a window.
- **If DOOR-BREACH opens on a suited seat with a full reserve** (D.3 Example A): ROOM until step 63, RESERVE with a valve click from step 64 (HYPOXIC with LOW), STRAINED throughout until the reserve crosses `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` at step 663, CRITICAL (stagger, walk-only, warning mark on the strip's breath dial) at step 762, FAILED at step 1113, DEAD at step 1413 unless the room regains air; first cue to FAILED 108.8 s. The decompression itself (DECOMP at step 143, VACUUM at 360) happens inside the reserve, which is the point of buying it.
- **If the same breach hits a seat with reserve 0** (D.3 Example B): GASP from step 64 (the room still holds breathable-in-extremis air in HYPOXIC with LOW), CRITICAL at step 163, NONE from step 116 when the room goes ANOXIC, FAILED at step 313, DEAD at step 613; first cue to FAILED 28.8 s, above `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`. A reserve of 0 is the worst case the limiter is sized for.
- **If reserve is 0 in a HYPOXIC room at non-decompressing pressure** (GASP, CK-1): the seat draws from the room and its breath axis holds at CRITICAL (walk-only, the Graph's "stamina loss") for as long as the room stays HYPOXIC, however long that is; FAILED requires ANOXIC, DECOMP or VACUUM. Killing a seat in air the Graph itself calls survivable would break P1; the cost is a long, visible walk-only window and the room's own clock (D.3 Example C: 106 s of GASP on the O2-ROOM fixture).
- **If the room recovers while the seat is in GASP or NONE** (hysteresis `H_P` applies to the enum): the source returns to ROOM on the run the enum is AIR_OK, the demand falls, and the rung follows at once (instant descent, SE-17); the Controller's cap rises on the same run. If this lands on the Controller's `death_tick` the cap is read first (SE-24).
- **If `band_o2` flaps between THIN and NORMAL on a hysteresis edge** (SE-19): the demand follows the enum between 1 and 0 with no extra smoothing in Suit; STRAINED has no hold on entry and descent is instant, so the cue tracks the true band and no stagger can result (STRAINED keeps the cap FULL).
- **If a leaking suit (`integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`) stands in NORMAL air and its reserve reaches 0** (SE-22, D.2): no ladder change (the source is ROOM and the demand is by band); the reserve reading shows empty and the seam jet continues. The first unbreathable room it enters is GASP or NONE with no buffer, which is the stated side effect of a leak that costs air whatever the source.
- **If the reserve empties mid-channel** (SE-17, SE-24): at the 1 to 2 advance the Controller staggers on the falling edge and clears the channel (PC-9: stagger clears the channel); at FAILED the cap is NONE, the seat is DOWNED, the carry is dropped. The player sees the warning mark, the stagger and the walk-only gait before the channel can be lost to death.
- **If a top-up is refused** (SE-7, D.9): at a full reserve or cell, `validate` answers `ALREADY_FULL` (an immediate-class reason read from the actor's own state); if the Graph's `withdraw_store` grants 0 the command is applied-and-did-nothing (no token, no distinct cue, `credit_*` is not called; the sight-glass not falling and the reserve reading not rising are the answer, Interaction IA-12); if it grants less than the deficit the credit equals the grant. The O2 stock falls by exactly the credit in every case; a BATTERY stock falls by the credit's conversion plus dust (D.9).
- **If `R_BREATH` is changed by data after a save** (D.8): capacities recompute from the time knobs on read, so the felt duration is unchanged; a saved `reserve_mgu` is then restored through the `data_rev` rescale (SE-34): the felt fraction is kept, nothing is clamped silently and the restore succeeds, so a legitimate retune of `R_BREATH` no longer leaves the player with no loadable save *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the same value under an **unchanged** `data_rev` is out of range and still fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`.

### The cell and the beam

- **If the cell reaches 0 while a channel's only light is the beam** (SE-8, D.5): `force_off(beam_id)` fires once on that run and lands before the same tick's `emitter.place`; `lit` is false at the next placement, the channel aborts within 5 ticks (Emitter State EM-10 and its sampling note), and a turn-ON then answers `CHARGE_EMPTY`. The player has had the cell-whine and the warning mark since `CELL_WARN_S` before the dark.
- **If the player toggles the beam inside one 6-tick window** (D.5): up to 5 ticks of light per ON/OFF pair can fall between two samples, so the over- or under-drain is at most 5 ticks per toggle; the Controller adapter holds `BEAM_TOGGLE_MIN_INTERVAL_TICKS` (10 ticks, PC-5) between two submitted `toggle_beam`, so every input-driven lit span is at least 10 ticks and contains a sample point, and a toggling player cannot light the beam for nothing. The simulation adds no strobe cooldown by design; the debounce is the Controller's accessibility rule.
- **If the cell is empty far from the ship** (SE-8, D.5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the lamp is dark and `toggle_beam` ON answers `CHARGE_EMPTY`. Suit's side of what still works: `toggle_beam` OFF is accepted, `equip_tool` is accepted in any actionable state (no light is needed to change a tool), `throw_glowstick` validates only `stowed_count` (the stick is the plan: it lights a pool where it lands), `locomotion_cap` and movement are unchanged, no Suit mutator reads the cell, and the body cues are unchanged but, being lit-only (VA-R6), visible only where another source lands on the sprite, so the audio witnesses carry them. What does not work is Interaction's: every lit-gated PLAYER verb answers `NOT_LIT` unless some other source (a glowstick pool, a powered fixture, a crewmate's beam) lights the target, so the seat can neither work nor top up in the dark; which declarations are exempt, and whether doors are passable in the dark, are Interaction's and the Controller's (OQ-46). A full home-ship refill costs about 4 % of one `BATT_CELL` at the default `CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK`, which is nearly free; whether the Pillar 3 cost is felt is OQ-44.
- **If a seat with residual cold load (`thermal_load` below 0) walks into a HOT or SCORCH room, or its load crosses zero there** (D.10, D.4) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `cold_side` is gated on `band_t` not in {HOT, SCORCH}, so the rime and the shiver switch off on the run the band changes (the room is hot; a rimed shoulder in it would be a dishonest body channel); the warmth rung, the cap and the stagger are untouched (a seat at CRITICAL stays WALK_ONLY, witnessed by the gait and the mark), and the load relaxes toward 0 at `TL_RELAX_EDGE` in HOT; a load that crosses 0 and turns positive is heat-side and never draws a body cue.
- **If the beam is lit while the seat is DOWNED** (SE-24): the cell keeps draining (1800 ticks is 2.5 % of a full cell) and `force_off` removes it at 0; a DEAD seat is frozen and the Emitter State forces the beam off. A downed player's light is a locator for a crewmate until one of those happens.
- **If `CELL_ENABLED` is 0** (SE-8): `cell_drain` is the identity, `CHARGE_EMPTY` is registered and never fires, the cell reading is omitted by the HUD, and the V7 BATTERY path is dormant. The fallback is data-only because 0 means "empty" everywhere else in this GDD.
- **If a BATTERY top-up grants fewer stock units than requested** (D.9): `credited_ticks` is the granted units converted down (floor) and never exceeds the deficit; at the default 2000 units per 1000 ticks the round trip is exact. The conversion unit is dJ (M16 on a BATTERY `stock` is confirmed legal, OQ-12); a partial grant may leave dust of at most `ceil(CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000)` − 1 units (1 at the default), which is spent and credits no ticks, and the stock-domain invariant is `stock_debit = ceil(credited_ticks × CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000) + dust` (D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

### Ladder, death and recovery

- **If a qualifying demand holds for fewer than `HOLD_12` runs** (D.3 cap bound): a 2 s DECOMP transient at reserve 0 reaches STRAINED on entry, counts 20 runs, resets when the demand falls, and never loses mobility. A door that cycles cannot kill; only a sustained condition can.
- **If two axes are bad at once** (SE-16): each axis climbs on its own counters and `vitals_state` is the worst rung; a SCORCH room raises warmth (CRITICAL at 10 s, FAILED at 30 s) and integrity (4 bp per run) together, and the seat dies of whichever axis completes first. Warmth is the faster in SCORCH, so integrity there is the slow secondary axis.
- **If the cap rises on the Controller's `death_tick`** (SE-24): Suit runs before `controller.step`, so a cap raised on that run is honoured; the Controller checks the cap first, then death. If `death_tick` falls on a tick that is not a multiple of 6 the cap read is the one written at the last multiple (up to 5 ticks old, PC-9), and a rescue computed in those 5 ticks arrives too late; accepted and stated.
- **If an EXOGENOUS hit lands mid-channel at the default knob** (SE-23, D.3 Example E): the integrity rung is set from `integrity_bp` on the run after each per-step apply: STRAINED at step 7 of a 20-step act, CRITICAL with the stagger at step 11 (the channel is cleared by the stagger, so a victim can walk, but not run, out of reach), FAILED and DOWNED at step 16. Whether the remaining steps of the act can still land on a walk-only victim is Interaction's reach arithmetic, not Suit's (CK-3).
- **If a restore lands after an EXOGENOUS apply and before the run that consumes it** (SE-23, SE-38): `exo_pending` is persisted, so the first run after the restore applies the bypass exactly once; a hit that had landed is not dropped by the save, and `post_restore` does not set it.
- **If `apply_suit_damage` exceeds the remaining integrity** (D.7): the amount clamps to 0 and never rejects; at the default knob the rung is set from the band (FAILED), with the knob at 0 the ladder climbs one rung per hold and FAILED comes no sooner than the floor.
- **If a patch targets an integrity at or above `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP`** (D.7): `patch_integrity` accepts 0 and changes nothing; below the cap it raises to `min(integrity + bp, SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP)`. A patched suit is permanently STRAINED, so a hit that lands on a patched suit starts from at most 6000.
- **If a seat walks through a door into a room whose pressure is already two bands lower, or the SE-3 NONE-to-EXPOSED switch happens** (D.12, SE-35) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the compartment changed, so the window re-anchors on the new room, `fire` is false and no `stagger` is submitted; the breath ladder tells the player the rest (the reserve engages, a valve click). A pressure fall inside the compartment the seat already stands in is the only thing D.12 counts. Whether the crossing rule should instead depend on whether the player could see the room they entered is not computable inside Suit and is carried as OQ-39.
- **If a restore lands mid-window** (D.12, SE-38) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: `p_age0` and `p_age1` are persisted and `prev_cid` is rebuilt as the current compartment, so the first run after the restore is not a crossing and the trigger fires on exactly the run an uninterrupted session would have fired; a saved age above `STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS` + 1 fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`.
- **If a DEAD seat is stepped further** (SE-24): the block is frozen: no draw, no drain, no rung evaluation, no cell drain, so its block is unchanged by further runs. A DOWNED seat is not frozen: it keeps breathing and draining.
- **If a breath-axis demand alternates between a vacuum room and a breathable room** (SE-17, RK-3): each return to breathable air drops the rung and resets the counter, so a player who oscillates through a door can stretch reserve-0 time; physically this needs a door and a cycle, and there is no decay branch (the hold resets in full on any non-qualifying run; D.3). The trade-off is stated rather than coded away.
- **If two commands in one tick patch and damage the same seat** (SE-39): they apply in command order inside P2, the hook runs after and reads the final `integrity_bp`; there is no mid-tick intermediate state.

### Warmth

- **If the seat stands in a FROST room, or a HOT room below `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, for twenty minutes** (SE-20, D.4): the load never gains, relaxes `TL_RELAX_EDGE` per run, and the warmth rung holds STRAINED (rime and shiver on the cold side) with cap FULL for as long as the player stays; it can never walk-lock or kill. FREEZING above the shifted onset also gains nothing and relaxes `TL_RELAX_EDGE` (3) per run *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, so a seat that walks out of the lethal strip into it recovers instead of holding its load there (a seat at `thermal_load` −3900, CRITICAL, drops below 3,700 after 67 runs and leaves the walk-only rung).
- **If the suit is leaking (below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`) in a cold room** (SE-21, D.4): `insul_eff_mc` falls linearly with integrity below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, so the shifted onset moves back toward `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` and a room that was safe at full integrity gains load at gain 8 per run (at `integrity_bp` 2500 and a room 3000 mC below the unshifted onset); the seam jet is already visible. Between `SUIT_LEAK_HP` and `SUIT_HP_OK` (integrity STRAINED, including any patched suit) insulation is full and nothing changes. This is the only coupling between the integrity and warmth axes.
- **If an unpowered home-ship cools** (SE-21, `lsg` SHIP-COLD): with the shift the shifted onset is reached after about 40 minutes, past a 30-minute session, so the home-ship is cold and frightening (breath vapour, rime, shiver) but not lethal inside a session; cold damage lands only in a room whose equilibrium is below the shifted onset. Derelict Authoring must place its lethal cold rooms accordingly, or lower `INSUL_MC`.

### Holders, carry and loadout

- **If a pickup targets a full glowstick stack, or a partly full one** (SE-10, SE-12): at 4 of 4 `add_stowed` accepts 0, the item stays in the world and Interaction reports `STACK_FULL`; at 3 of 4 with a pickup of 2 it accepts 1 and leaves 1 in the world. Merge needs equal `def_id`, `condition_bp` and `charge` (IDB-15); a refused merge never destroys an item.
- **If the 30,000 g `part_hull_plate` is stowed** (SE-28, D.6): `cue_load` is HEAVY (the binding Item DB trigger), the HEAVY back-frame shows, movement is unchanged (mass never changes speed or noise), and a carried module can still be lifted but the cue stays HEAVY, so the VS-A field check is blind to the carried item until the plate is dropped (OQ-8). A plate reserved by a repair job is out of the pack from the begin tick in both truth branches (SE-41).
- **If the cap becomes NONE while a module is carried** (SE-13): the Controller clears `carry_ref` on the same tick; for that one tick `carried_id` is set in `suit.state` while `carry_ref` is NONE; Interaction's next-tick conversion calls `release_carried`. The autosave hard gate refuses DOWNED, so no generation can capture the mismatch.
- **If `equip_tool` arrives while carrying, channelling, staggered, downed or for slot 5, 6 or an unheld tool** (SE-11): rejected in the order `NOT_ACTIONABLE` (STAGGERED, DOWNED, DEAD), `BUSY` (carrying or channelling), `EMPTY_SLOT`, as immediate-class reasons; while the kernel is paused it is rejected as not `allowed_when_paused`. Equipping the equipped slot is an accepted no-op.
- **If a fresh `tool_suppressant` is picked up while the held one has charges left, or at 0** (SE-14): refused with charges left (the tool is unique per player); at 0 `replace_tool` discards the empty one and holds the fresh one, so the unique-tool rule can never make a second suppressant an unpickable trap.
- **If `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT` is set above `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`** (D.8 A8): boot fails the start-kit assertion; the start kit is never silently clamped.

### Save, restore and the seams

- **If `post_restore` finds `controller.state.carry_ref` different from `carried_id` and not both NONE** (SE-13, SE-34): the restore fails `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`, live state untouched; an unsupported `schema_version` fails `SUIT_RESTORE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`; an out-of-range field (a rung of 4, a hold above its maximum, a stowed count over `stack_max`) fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE` or `SUIT_RESTORE_STOWED_OVERFLOW`. Never clamped (SL-15).
- **If a restore lands mid-hold or mid-job** (SE-34, SE-41): `rung_*`, `hold_*` and the per-seat `reserved` counts are persisted and restored exactly, so a restore neither resets nor extends the tell floor and a reserved part stays out of the count (no oracle reappears); `prev_*` copies are rebuilt in `post_restore`, so no spurious edge cue (valve click, stagger) fires after a restore.
- **If an autosave is written while a seat is three seconds from FAILED** (SE-33): the hard gate only refuses DOWNED or DEAD, so the generation is legal and the death restore returns to it; the proposed soft gate (`vitals_state` of CRITICAL or worse defers the save, capped at `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS`) closes this, and until it is adopted (a **USER DECISION**, OQ-13 and Save/Load OQ-40) the doomed-autosave loop is a known risk (RK-5).
- **If the seat is IN_POD, or `room_at` answers NONE** (SE-3, SE-35): while `loco_state` is IN_POD (HOLD_ROOM) reserve and cell are held constant for the hop, no `draw_air` is called (the pod's section is dormant, WM-17), and the thermal load and the hold counters do not advance because no band is read; a NONE with `loco_state` not IN_POD (a defect-only state, BDY-29) is EXPOSED to the EXTERIOR (vacuum, cold), never to the last room's bands, and everything keeps running. A seat with no room at spawn is an authoring failure, not a runtime state.
- **If Vertical Slice A staggers the Graph's hooks so `graph.propagate.atmo` moves to offset 2** (SE-1): Suit is re-registered at offset 2 too (a boot-time data change); if only one registration moves, the kernel itself only warns about the never-co-running pair, so Suit's own build assertion A12 (D.8) fails boot and the staggered build cannot ship half-moved.
- **If the kernel is paused** (SE-32): no ticks, no runs, no drift of any counter; `equip_tool` and every mutator are rejected, so a solo player cannot re-arm with time stopped mid-tell.

### Co-op hooks (recorded, not built)

- **If four seats each carry a full stack of glowsticks** (SE-10, A11): 16 is above `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 8; the extra sticks enter the Emitter State's visible evict-wane rather than an instant removal; D.8 A11 raises a boot warning (not a failure) above one seat, so this case is reachable; at the start count of 2 the four-seat total is 8 and fits, and the surplus appears only as sticks are picked up. The CONFLICT is recorded for Vertical Slice B (OQ-14).
- **If a player rejoins on a different peer** (SE-2): the seat's record is keyed by PlayerId, so the reserve, cell, rungs and kit are unchanged; no `peer_id` appears in the block. A new seat's `default_state()` grants the start kit with full reserve and cell and all rungs at 0.
- **If two seats default their beam shape** (SE-9): seat n takes `shape_id` n, so identity is unique among four; a picker on the new-campaign screen (Vertical Slice B; none at MVP, CT-24) may change it before `bind_beam`, and the choice is immutable afterwards.
- **If a crewmate would revive a DOWNED seat** (SE-24): `revive` is declared and dormant in the MVP; its Vertical Slice B contract is the minimal change that clears the integrity terminal condition (`integrity_bp` raised to `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, OQ-18), because breath and warmth are the room's and no revive can fix them.

### Hallucination interplay

- **If a Vertical Slice A Filter wraps `true_value("suit.warmth", player_id)` and shows a warm numeral while the seat is in a FROST room** (SE-25, SE-29): the reading changes and every body cue stays true: breath vapour, rime and the shiver are functions of `band_t` and `suit.state` only; the player can falsify the numeral by looking at their own shoulder plates or listening to their breath at the cost of the lamp and a moment of attention. A Filter that tries to move the chest point or edit overlay visibility violates the cue-purity lint (AC-SE-26).
- **If a phantom item is shown as carried or a phantom part is shown in the pack** (SE-28, SE-41): Suit holds no instance for a phantom, so `cue_load` and the back-frame do not change; the breath is the honest field check, and the reservation API prevents a repair channel from revealing a phantom through a spent or unspent part.
- **If a hallucination makes a crewmate look like a monster and the player harms them** (SE-23): the gate is Sanity's, applied in Interaction's `validate` on the actor; Suit records the per-step exogenous damage as it would for any source, the victim's rung tracks it, and the victim really dies (the user's decision); Suit never reads sanity and never softens the landing.
- **If sanity is lowest** (SE-29): in the MVP no reading is corrupted and no cue changes; the same seat in the same room produces byte-identical cue outputs (AC-SE-26's mutated-sanity replay).

## Dependencies

*Status as of the round-1 revision, 2026-10-26 (Glob of `design/gdd/`): nine GDDs are designed and cited — the Command & Simulation Layer, Life-Support Graph, Item & Module Database, Player Controller & Input, Emitter State & Light-Exposure Query and Save/Load & Versioned Format, plus Interaction (19:34; re-verified at the final advisor review, 2026-10-27), Ship Section & Layout (18:38) and 2D Lighting & Occlusion (18:48), all designed 2026-10-26 with contract current; the Render Pipeline and Failure & Trail are also designed and cited only. **Consistency pass, 2026-10-27:** Attention, Sanity & Hallucination Rules, Boarding & Salvage, HUD & Suit Layer, Ship Body & Interior Physics, Horror Entities, Perception Filter, Adaptive Audio & Tells, Web Map & Transit, the Chart Table, Modular Ship Assembly and the Debug Overlay are now designed; Co-op Session & Replication and Failure Contract & Re-wake have no GDD file. Every contract with an absent system is provisional and says what that GDD must repeat. "Counterpart text names this system?" records whether the other document already names Suit.*

**Index corrections routed to the producer (this GDD never edits the index):** add Suit → Ship Section & Layout (Hard), Suit → Ship Body (Hard), Interaction → Suit (Hard) and Suit → Web Map & Transit (Soft). Layout's and Interaction's designed texts state the Layout and Interaction edges from their side.

**Circularity check.** Suit reads the Graph's atmosphere (ordered after `graph.propagate.atmo`) and calls its Class B mutators; the Graph reads nothing from Suit. Suit reads `emitter.state.lit` as of T-1 and calls `force_off`; the Emitter State reads Suit's `charge` view in `toggle_beam.validate`, a read of a value Suit committed on a previous run — no cycle, because Suit's hook declares only `after: [graph.propagate.atmo]` and `controller.step` declares `after: suit.vitals`. Interaction calls Suit's mutators in its own `apply`; Suit reads nothing from Interaction (it receives the credits and the damage as calls). The Controller reads only `locomotion_cap`; Suit reads the Controller's `loco_state` and `carry_ref`. The index's circular-dependency note does not list Suit.

**Engine reference gaps (Godot 4.7.2, pinned).** `docs/engine-reference/godot/` has **no `scripting.md` or `core.md`** (its `modules/` holds animation, audio, input, navigation, networking, physics, rendering and ui): GDScript integer-math cost, `RefCounted` block performance and the integer-division behaviour for negative operands are NOT SOURCEABLE from the reference, and every cost number here is a model parameter for the spike. Sourced 4.7 changes that bite this module (`breaking-changes.md`): setting one element of a packed array no longer calls the property setter, so dirty keys are marked explicitly in the mutators, never in setters; a typed-return override needs an explicit `return` on every path (avoid virtual dispatch on a hot path); `AudioStreamPlayer2D.area_mask` defaults to 0 (irrelevant to the non-positional breath cue, relevant if a seam hiss is spatialised); `GPUParticles2D.restart(keep_seed)` and the process-time rename bear on the vapour VFX (medium risk, verify in the implementation story). Json integer handling (`JSON.parse_string` float behaviour in 4.7.2) is an open export spike owned by the Item DB; Suit stores integers only and reads them with `int()` at load. No ADR exists yet.

## Tuning Knobs

*Every Suit-owned knob lives in `assets/data/suit/*.json` (integers only, loaded in BOOTING, immutable; the Item DB's IDB-26 convention) and is `[PH]` until the pacing spike and the MVP playtest. **★ marks a registry candidate** — a cross-system fact another GDD reads or must repeat (the Phase 5 registry append, R32); the rest are local to this GDD and are listed so a balance pass has one table. A knob's rationale is the formula or rule in the last column. A retune of any time knob that changes a stored quantity bumps `data_rev` (SE-34), so saves made before it rescale instead of failing. Build assertions A1 to A13 (D.8) constrain several ranges (the feasible-region table in D.8 lists the coupled ones): a value outside the safe range fails boot rather than ship.*

### Breath, reserve and top-up

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `R_BREATH` ★ | 300 | mGU per step per crew | 100-600 | breathing dominates the O2 budget; one crew approaches the scrubber's output; the reserve holds more mGU for the same seconds | rooms rarely thin; the Graph's O2 clocks stretch | D.2, D.8; Graph D.5 and the Item DB F-14 ladder follow it; Suit-registered (it moves from a Graph placeholder) |
| `RESERVE_S` ★ | 90 | s | 45-240 | vacuum becomes trivial; the Graph's clocks stop mattering to a suited crew | authored vacuum traverses become lethal; the top-up ritual dominates play | `RESERVE_WARN_S` (at most half), the Derelict Authoring traverse bound (D.11), A5 |
| `RESERVE_WARN_S` ★ | 30 | s | 20-60 | walk-only arrives early in an authored traverse | the CRITICAL cue arrives with less than `W_MIN_WARNING_S` of air left | `RESERVE_S`, `HOLD_12`; A5 |
| `HYPOX_FAIL_S` | 15 | s | 10-30 | a long agony before FAILED | a thin margin over `HOLD_12`; floor shrinks | `HOLD_23(breath)` (D.8). The terminal hold of the breath axis for source NONE (ANOXIC, DECOMP or VACUUM with an empty reserve); the name predates CK-1 |
| `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` | 150 | mGU per run | 0-150 at `RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 (A5 bounds it by `R_BREATH` x (`RESERVE_WARN_S` / 20 − 1); 300 only at `RESERVE_WARN_S` 45) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | a leak outruns a refill | a leak is unfelt; the seam jet has no cost | D.2, D.7; the reserve reading and the jet are its witnesses |
| `TOPUP_MODE` | VERB | enum | VERB, PASSIVE | — | — | **USER DECISION (OQ-2).** VERB requires the Graph to amend LS-26 to add V7 to the MVP verb list (Interaction has adopted V7, IA-9, and registers `top_up` at boot iff LS-26 lists it); PASSIVE forces `CELL_ENABLED` 0; the Graph's OQ 20 records PASSIVE as the interim until the user rules; both configurations are specified and correct (D.2, D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `TOPUP_CHANNEL_TICKS` | 0 (TAP) | ticks | 0-180 | the ritual drags | none | declined by Interaction (TAP only, IA-9); retained as data only; the amount never changes |
| `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN` | 30 | mGU per run | 10-90 | the room bleeds; breath plus extra exceeds the scrubber's output | the passive refill takes an hour | D.9 (PASSIVE only) |
| `TOPUP_MIN_PP` | 195 | dkPa | 185-205 | the passive refill never runs | a closet is dragged to THIN | D.9 (PASSIVE only); `band_o2` edges |

| `SUIT_HP_FAIL` ★ | 2500 | bp | 1000-4000 | the suit is brittle | the suit is durable | checkpoint CK-12: the systems-designer's value; a 2500-bp CRITICAL band gives the endogenous arc ramp room to satisfy `HOLD_12` before the terminal condition; A6, A7 |
| `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` ★ | 6000 | bp | 5100-6900 (above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, below `SUIT_HP_OK`) | a patch is a cure | a patched suit re-leaks | D.7; mirrors the Graph's patch cap by relation only; A6 |
