| `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` | 60 | ticks | 30-120 | fires on gentle leaks | never fires | live at MVP: the Controller registers the `SYSTEM` `stagger` type (PC-1 b, OQ-11 answered); D.12 reads it as `STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS` = floor(ticks / `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`) = 10 runs (range 5-20). **At the default it fires on none of the locked Graph fixtures** (D.12 Example 1: DOOR-BREACH's LOW-to-DECOMP gap is 87 runs); whether to widen the range is OQ-38 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `SUIT_SCHEMA_VERSION` ★ | 1 | int | not a knob | — | — | SE-1, SE-34 |

**Derived, never tuned:** `RESERVE_MAX_MGU`, `RESERVE_WARN_MGU`, `CELL_MAX_TICKS`, `HOLD_12`, `HOLD_23(axis)`, `THERMAL_FAIL_S` (D.8). **Cited, owned elsewhere (do not duplicate here):** `TICK_RATE`, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, `GAS_SCALE`, `W_MIN_WARNING_S`, `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`, `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, `HYPOXIA_PP`, `DECOMP_P` (Graph registry); `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`, `GLOWSTICK_LIFE_S`, `WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G`, `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` (Item DB); `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE`, `BEAM_BASE_PM`, the `BEAM_*` shapes (Emitter State); `V_WALK`, `V_CARRY`, `STAGGER_TICKS`, `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS`, `TILE_PX` (Controller and Pipeline); `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (Save/Load); `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` (kernel); `THROW_RANGE_TILES`, `THROW_ARC_TICKS`, `HARM_TOTAL_BP`, `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS` (Interaction). Every constant of brief Appendix A is now a registry candidate (R32); the remaining local knobs (`TL_*`, `TOPUP_*`, `CELL_ADVISE_S`, `CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK`, `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN`, `ARC_*`, `SCORCH_BURN_BP_PER_RUN`, `START_EQUIPPED_SLOT`, `BEAM_DEFAULT_SHAPE_SEAT1`) are registered as local `[PLACEHOLDER]` entries at Phase 5 or stay in `assets/data/suit/*.json` by the producer's call.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*Requirements, not outputs.* Suit draws nothing and plays nothing itself (it is a `SIM` block and a divisor-6 hook, H10). It publishes true values (`view(player_id)` cue levels, `true_value`) that the Avatar Presenter (the Controller's), the HUD & Suit Layer, 2D Lighting & Occlusion, Failure & Trail and Adaptive Audio & Tells conform to. This section lists what each of them must be able to show and sound, in which class of truth, colour and motion, and which assets that implies. The art-director signs the look and the audio-director the sound; evidence is a retained screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/` (BLOCKING). Every requirement placed on a system whose GDD is a draft or absent is **provisional** and must be repeated by it (Interactions). Citations `ab:N` are art-bible lines, `ab §x.y` its sections.

### Art stance

The suit is the one place the art bible's two registers touch the same avatar: the **sprite** (an anchor, true on every client, `ab §5.3`) and the **reading** (the suit layer, lie-capable by architecture, `ab §7.0`). The sort into those two registers is the whole visual rule: *what the player's body shows is true and cannot be removed, added or tinted by Sanity or Attention; what the HUD numbers show is a reading that the art bible's grammar already allows to lie and that Suit leaves honest in the MVP (SE-29).* A body cue costs light to see; a reading costs nothing — Principle 1 applied to the avatar: to check the numeral against the body the player must spend light (`ab:36`, `ab:738`). The three tie-breakers of `ab §1`: (1) does it make the player spend something — rime, fog and the vapour puff exist only where light lands on the sprite; (2) is it in the world, not on top of it — a leaking suit shows a jet, not a banner; (3) if the player is lied to here, does the lie leak the sane palette — only the five readings can lie, and only with the violet gleam, glide and sound of HR-5.

| Cite | Rule | What it forces on Suit |
|---|---|---|
| Principle 1 (`ab:36-42`) | True occlusion, never a mask | No screen-space body effect of any kind: no vignette, tunnel, blur, edge frost or hypoxia distortion (SE-27). The beam is never dimmed by low charge (SE-8, EM-8). |
| Principle 2 (`ab:44-55`) | State lives in the sprite or medium; failure is a trail in the loop's own hue | Suit has no loop. Body state is overlays on the sprite and puffs in the beam, never icons over the head. Hue is redundant with shape. |
| Principle 3 (`ab:57-66`) | Emission is exposure | Nothing on the body glows; every overlay is unlit albedo. Only the engine-rendered beam and the landed glowstick pool are light. |
| Corollary (`ab:68-76`) and 4.4 (`ab:422-445`) | Lies leak violet or flesh; anchors never shift | No Suit asset samples violet (270-330 degrees) or flesh (5-30 degrees). |
| 2.9, 2.10 (`ab:228-252`) | Overlay never changes tempo; crew loss goes dark, no red, no text | Cue cadence derives from vitals only. DEAD adds no screen beat (SL-8): the lamp going out is the only Suit-side light event. |
| 3.1-3.2, 4.0-4.2 (`ab:276-408`) | Silhouette test; beam shape is identity; Chemlight is world light only | Overlays keep the circle-on-rectangle read. The hand-rail glowstick glyph is Paper White, **never** Chemlight `#86B896` (`ab:384`, `ab:396`). |
| 4.5, 4.7 (`ab:447-488`) | Luminance ladder; a new semantic hue needs a row | **No new hue is introduced**; no 4.7 row is needed. Rime (Coolant Ice) is separated from the Tungsten beam core by location and shape, not value. |
| 5.0-5.3, 5.6-5.7 (`ab:496-593`) | Sprite is the anchor; rime on plates only, visor fog, stagger; sanity never drawn on the body; overlay budget about 10 images | 5.3's "thresholds are the game-designer's" is discharged by the cue table (States and Transitions); the budget overflows (CONFLICT VA-A3). |
| 7.0-7.3 (`ab:726-851`), HR-1 to HR-15 (`ab:968-986`) | The HUD is a suit; reading register; settle, tremor, glide; red never text; reduced motion; 3 Hz | Readings and marks below. |

### Truth-channel classification

Classes are the five of SE-26: **CL** closed-list cue, **BF** breath-family cue, **AN** on-sprite anchor (list amendment landed, CONFLICT C1 closed 2026-10-27), **RD** reading (lie-capable by architecture, uncorrupted in the MVP), **PR** physical result. **W** marks a world visual Suit only triggers. Every row's never-source: Sanity, Attention, any `PERCEPTION_OUT` view, any filtered value (SE-25).

| # | Visual | Class | Driving true value |
|---|---|---|---|
| VA-1 | Rime on shell plates (two levels) | AN | warmth rung, cold side (`cue_cold` 2 and 3) |
| VA-2 | Visor fog (two levels) | CL | breath rung (`cue_fog` 1 and 2) |
| VA-3 | Breath vapour puff | CL (breath) | `band_t` COOL or colder (`cue_cold` 1 and up) |
| VA-4 | Shiver (idle frames) | CL (shiver) | `cue_cold` at or above 2 and `loco_state` idle |
| VA-5 | Stagger frames, walk-only gait | AN | `locomotion_cap` falling edge FULL to WALK_ONLY (the Controller draws) |
| VA-6 | Down and collapse frames | AN | `locomotion_cap` NONE |
| VA-7 | Seam vapour jet | AN | `integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` |
| VA-8 | HEAVY back-frame | AN | stowed class HEAVY (`stowed_mass_g`) |
| VA-9 | Carry-walk pose | AN | `carried_instance` non-empty |
| VA-10 | The carried module's own sprite | module-body (may lie) | the module def; never Suit's cue (`idb:331`) |
| VA-11 | Chest lamp housing (static, no state frame) | AN (cosmetic) | none |
| VA-12 | The beam (presence, shape, reach, edge, the moment it goes out) | PR (Emitter owns) | `emitter.state.lit`, `shape_id`; `force_off` at zero cell |
| VA-13 | Landed glowstick pool and source sprite | PR (Emitter and Interaction own) | the deployable record |
| VA-14 | Tool in hand (held, in use) | AN | `tool_in_hand` |
| VA-15 | HUD frame, chamfered plates, glyph silhouettes | AN (HR-6) | none |
| VA-16 | O2 dial arc and numeral | RD | `suit.o2` = suit-sensed `p_o2` of the occupant's compartment |
| VA-17 | Reserve reading | RD | `suit.reserve` |
| VA-18 | Integrity reading | RD | `suit.integrity` |
| VA-19 | Warmth reading (suit-sensed T and P, one reading, **no body-temperature number**) | RD | `suit.warmth` |
| VA-20 | Charge reading | RD | `suit.charge` (cell fraction) |
| VA-21 | Rail counts (suppressant 0-3, glowsticks 0-4), carried glyph, equipped highlight | RD for counts (OQ-6); highlight follows VA-14 | `tool_charge`, `stowed_count`, `tool_in_hand` |
| VA-22 | Warning mark beside a reading | RD (inherits the reading it sits beside) | the reading's own value |
| VA-23 | Tank sight-glass drop at the top-up port | W (In-World Instruments) | the Graph `stock` after M16 |
| VA-24 | Draught, dust, hiss, frost on surfaces | W (Failure & Trail) | Graph descriptors |

**Rules that follow.** **VA-R1 Pure function:** VA-1 to VA-9 and VA-11 are redrawn each presentation frame from the last committed cue levels only and carry no state of their own; the first frame after a restore shows exactly the restored levels, with no residue particles and no tween. **VA-R2 One class per visual:** the O2 numeral (RD) never drives fog (CL), and a body cue is never read back from a reading. **VA-R3 No lying variant:** `suit.state` and `view` hold true values only; no Suit asset exists in a "lying" variant — the lie is the HUD's gleam, glide and sound over a true reading's slot (HR-5). **VA-R4 The warning mark is not a truth channel:** it sits beside its reading, so it lies when the reading lies; the honest second opinion is always the body and the room (SE-30). **VA-R5 Chemlight is for light only.** **VA-R6 Unlit means unseen:** body cues are visible to the wearer and to crewmates only while some light reaches the sprite; this is the intended cost ("you look cold" costs only light, `ab:528`) and is why every body cue also has an audio witness.

### Event to feedback table

Owners: Presenter = Avatar Presenter (Controller GDD), HUD = HUD & Suit Layer, Lighting = 2D Lighting & Occlusion, F&T = Failure & Trail, Audio = Adaptive Audio & Tells, I = Interaction, E = Emitter State. Visual changes step on the next presentation frame after the 0.1 s run and never fade or ease. Priority is MVP unless stated. "Mark" is the warning mark (VA-22).

| Event (source) | Visual feedback (owner) | Class |
|---|---|---|
| Breath axis enters STRAINED (reserve engages, or THIN, or LOW pressure) | Visor fog level 1 (Presenter); reserve reading live (HUD); **no screen effect**; no mark | CL + RD |
| Reserve engages | Fog level 1 on the next presentation frame; nothing else visual; the witness is audio: valve click, then the closed breath cadence | CL + BF |
| Breath CRITICAL (reserve at or below `RESERVE_WARN_MGU`, or source GASP or NONE) | Fog level 2; one stagger on the FULL to WALK_ONLY edge, then walk-only (Presenter); mark on the strip's breath dial (HUD) | CL + AN + RD |
| Breath FAILED | Down animation, fog level 2 persists, lamp stays on (Presenter, E); the carried module is dropped by Interaction next tick | AN |
| Top-up at a lit O2_TANK port (I verb V7, if promoted) | Sight-glass falls (In-World Instruments); the reserve numeral ticks up in whole steps (HUD); work loop (Presenter). Fallback PASSIVE: no visual beyond the reading | W + RD |
| Warmth STRAINED, cold side (FROST) | Rime level 1 on helmet crown and pack top edge; shiver at rest; vapour (already from COOL); warmth reading zone band changes | AN + CL + RD |
| Warmth CRITICAL, cold side (past the shifted onset) | Rime level 2 on all four plate groups; stagger on the cap edge; walk-only; mark beside the warmth glyph | AN + RD |
| Warmth FAILED | Down animation; rime level 2 persists on the downed sprite | AN |
| Warmth rungs, heat side (HOT, past `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, SCORCH) | **No body overlay** (VA-C4). Room witnesses (scorch trail, F&T); the reading's zone band and the mark at CRITICAL; audio fan whirr | RD + W |
| Integrity damage lands | The integrity reading steps down; no body flash, no shake, no red (HR-13); crossing `SUIT_LEAK_HP` starts the jet | RD |
| Integrity below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` | Seam vapour jet from the pack seam while below; the reserve reading falls faster | AN |
| `patch_integrity` (self-patch, if Interaction hosts it) | Work loop; the jet stops when `integrity_bp` returns to or above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`; **no persistent patch overlay in the MVP** | AN |
| Beam turned ON or OFF | Cone appears or vanishes on the committed tick, never predicted (E, Lighting); the switch click pre-plays (Audio); no chest-lamp state frame | PR |
| Cell reaches `CELL_WARN_S` | Mark beside the charge glyph; cell whine starts no later than the mark (HR-9); **beam output untouched: no flicker, no dim** | RD + audio |
| Cell reaches zero (`force_off`) | The cone vanishes in one step on that tick (E, Lighting): a hard cut — a fade is a dimming lie, a flicker is the flare's word (`ab:286`) | PR |
| Turn-ON at zero (`CHARGE_EMPTY`) | Lamp stays off; the charge glyph shows empty with the mark; the failed-action cue is Interaction's; no frame shake | RD |
| `equip_tool` accepted | Tool-layer sprite swaps with the hand animation (Presenter); the rail highlight moves one step (Tungsten fixture, HR-11 bracket) | AN + RD |
| `equip_tool` rejected (`BUSY`, `NOT_ACTIONABLE`, `EMPTY_SLOT`) | Nothing in the world; the highlight does not move; Interaction's non-shaking rejection cue | RD |
| Suppressant charge spent / empty / swapped | Count steps down; at 0 the glyph stays in Graphite lit with an 8x8 empty mark (a reading, not a banner); after `replace_tool` it returns to Paper White with count 3 | RD |
| Glowstick thrown (`spend_stowed`) | Count steps down on acceptance; throw animation (Presenter, I); the stick in flight is an **unlit** sprite; the Chemlight pool blooms on landing, not in flight (E) | RD + PR |
| Glowstick pickup refused (`STACK_FULL`) / stowed | Item stays in the world, no Suit visual / count steps up, **no light or glow on the body** (a stowed stick is inert) | W / RD |
| `add_stowed` of a HEAVY def (the 30 kg plate) | HEAVY back-frame appears on the pack; the breath gains the HEAVY load step: deeper, with the creak layer and a footfall-linked exhale, at the air class's own rate (Audio) | AN + BF |
| `add_stowed` of LIGHT or MEDIUM parts | No visual (cue resolves classes, not grams); an audio step only when the stowed class reaches `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` | BF |
| `set_carried` / `release_carried` | Carry-walk set (8 frames), module held below the lamp, carried glyph appears; load layer by carried class / back to the walk set, glyph clears | AN + RD |
| DOWNED / DEAD (every seat) | Down animation, lamp stays on / lamp forced off; sources go dark warmest-first, no red, no violet, **no death screen** (SL-8) | AN |
| Restore from autosave | Cut to the restored world; all cues re-derive on the first frame; vapour and jet pools are cleared; no residue of the dead interval | AN |
| Pause | World freezes undimmed (`ab:876`); particles freeze; `equip_tool` is unavailable (SE-32) | AN |
| Zoom ladder step | Overlays and chest anchors scale with the integer zoom; **no second sprite set** (`ab:579`); the HUD does not scale with world zoom | AN |
| Readings corrupted by Sanity (VS-A, SE-29) | The five readings carry the violet fringe gleam `#B07BE6`, one HR-5 motion signature (glide on a dial needle; continuous tick or stale on a numeral) and a sound signature (HUD, Sanity); body cues VA-1 to VA-9 do not change | RD |
| Crewmate within light, co-op (VS-B) | Their rime, fog, stagger, carry pose, back-frame, seam jet and beam shape drawn from `SHARED_SUMMARY` cue fields (CONFLICT VA-A5); their vitals are not on the crew strip (`ab:902`) | AN |

### Body-cue specifications

All overlays are authored in the 80x80 crewmate cell (`ab:1041`), five drawn facings (S, SE, E, NE, N; three mirrored), flat unshaded albedo with `_nrm`/`_spc` pairing where a surface reads, binary alpha, 2x2 minimum feature, no single-pixel noise (`ab:579`). The 1 px Void Steel contour belongs to the body sprite only; overlays sit inside it and the 2 px violet-fringe clearance outside stays free (`ab:1032`). **Cue-level scheme (D.10, adopted everywhere):** `cue_cold` 0 none, 1 vapour only (COOL), 2 rime level 1 plus shiver (FROST, warmth STRAINED), 3 rime level 2 (warmth CRITICAL and FAILED); `cue_fog` 0 to 2.

- **VA-C1 Rime (warmth axis, cold side).** Sits **only on shell plates** (helmet, pack, forearms, knees) and **never at a fitting** (seam, clamp, visor rim, pack coupling), so it cannot read as a coolant leak (`ab:528`). Coolant Ice `#C8E6F5` only, static, no animation, no glint, no emission. Level 1 (`cue_cold` 2): 2x2 to 3x3 px clusters on the helmet crown and pack top edge only, about 6-10 clusters. Level 2 (`cue_cold` 3): clusters on all four plate groups, crown and shoulder line continuous. The levels differ by **plate-group count** (2 versus 4), a shape change that survives 50 % zoom and greyscale. FAILED adds no third level. Coolant Ice (L* about 91) collides with the Tungsten beam core (L* about 92) in greyscale; the backup is location (plates of a figure) and shape (static clusters, not a cone) — the existing 4.7 row "Tungsten vs Coolant Ice", no new row. Rime at a fitting, a drip, or any rime without `cue_cold` at or above 2 is a defect.
- **VA-C2 Visor fog (breath axis).** Inside the visor strip only (at least 4 px high with a 2x2 glint, `ab:1018`); bad air is never cold, and keeping the two on different sprite zones keeps them separable. Three whole-strip values that survive 50 % zoom (strip 2 px, glint below the 2x2 minimum, so **no level may depend on the glint or a small patch**): L0 clear (Void Steel, L* about 5, with glint); level 1 (`cue_fog` 1): the whole strip steps to Graphite mid `#2A303A` (L* about 20), glint halved; level 2 (`cue_fog` 2): the whole strip Graphite lit `#5C6670` (L* about 43), no glint. STRAINED has no mark, so level 1 is its only sprite witness. Needs `_alb` plus `_spc`. Never fed by sanity, Attention, the Filter or the cold.
- **VA-C3 Breath vapour (visual half of the breath cue).** Present only when `band_t` is COOL or colder; the absence of vapour in a warm room is itself information. A puff leaves the helmet front on each exhale, drifts about 6-10 px along the facing and dies in about 0.8 s; 4x4 and one 8x8 texture (the denser FROST and FREEZING puff), nearest-filtered, binary alpha ("pixels, not fog", `ab:1073`), at most 8 particles alive per seat. Graphite lit `#5C6670`, unlit albedo, visible only where engine light lands. **No loop hue:** O2 Cyan would read as a leaking O2 pipe (Principle 2) and Coolant Ice would duplicate rime. One puff per breath event, on the same cadence step as the audio, published by Suit as a cue step and never invented by the Presenter. Pack-breathing amplitude (idle 2-3 px, `ab:520`) stays constant: it is the Controller's idle and is **not** the load cue (SE-28).
- **VA-C4 Heat side: no body cue (ruling).** SE-20 left "sweat, shimmer" to the art-director. Sweat needs skin (`ab §5.1`: no skin anywhere) and a shimmer on the sprite is a distortion, which Principle 1 forbids. HOT and SCORCH are witnessed by the room (scorch trail, ember glow, F&T), the warmth reading's zone band and mark, the audio fan, and SCORCH's integrity damage landing as the seam jet. The Graph's HOT-band text ("sweat, heat shimmer") is not implemented by Suit; a room-level shimmer, if any, is F&T's or Lighting's call and must satisfy Principle 1. **Thin spot** (OQ-20): a deaf or hard-of-hearing player gets only the reading and the room for HOT STRAINED to CRITICAL.
- **VA-C5 Shiver.** `ab §5.2` allows exaggeration only in reaction frames; shiver is added to its whitelist (CONFLICT VA-A2). Two extra idle frames per facing (`idle_shiver`), a **1 px horizontal offset** alternation with the pack rise held; the chest anchor stays inside `|x| <= 2` (the beam origin shifts at most 1 px). Played **only while idle and `cue_cold` is at least 2**; while moving the shiver is audio-only (suit rattle) so the walk and run cycles stay unmodified. 12 fps clock, the pair alternating every 4 frames (a cycle every 8 frames, 1.5 Hz; positional, not luminous). Reduced motion (HR-8): hold one offset frame; rime and vapour carry the cue. Fallback if the budget forbids: audio-only plus rime, recorded as an amendment to the MVP cue set.
- **VA-C6 Stagger, walk-only, down.** Suit decides **when**; the Controller's sheet (stagger 4 frames, down 6, walk, carry-walk) decides **how**. Constraints Suit adds: stagger plays once on the falling edge FULL to WALK_ONLY and nowhere else (no repeat at the CRITICAL hold); it is an input lock plus frames, never a camera shake, displacement, impulse or screen effect (PC-9); the helmet stays a circle and the figure upright-ish; the DOWNED sprite keeps its rime and fog level, the back-frame and the lamp; walk-only is the same walk cycle at base speed with `run` a no-op — no limp animation exists, so it is read off the stagger that preceded it, the missing run and the fog or rime level.
- **VA-C7 Seam vapour jet (suit leak).** The P1 "visibly leaking pipe" on the body. Continuous while `integrity_bp` is below `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, stops the run it returns to or above. A particle jet at a new Aseprite slice `anchor_seam_<facing>` on the pack seam; 8x8 and 4x4 textures, hard pixels, 5-7 particles alive, length about 10-14 px along the seam normal, intermittent on the breath cadence. Hue-less Graphite lit, unlit, lit-only. It is the only **moving** grey emission from the **pack seam** (rime is static on plates, fog static in glass, the puff leaves the helmet front on the exhale and only when cold); location plus motion is the separator. Zero painted frames; requires the closed-list entry (CONFLICT VA-A1) and the anchor slice.
- **VA-C8 Self-patch and scars.** The MVP shows **no persistent patch** on the sprite: a scar is "a Graphite patch with a Hull Grey bead at its edge" (`ab:532`), the same grammar as a real weld patch, so a real repair is the work loop and the integrity reading. If a persistent patch is ever added it goes on the forearm or hip, never the three scar slots (helmet, pack, limb), disjoint by shape (OQ-21). **Integrity STRAINED has no body overlay** (a "scuffed plates" overlay was ruled out for the same vocabulary collision and the spent budget) and none is owed: the rung is inert by design (no cap, insulation or drain effect), so its first witness is the seam jet at `SUIT_LEAK_HP` (OQ-19 ruled, CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26).
- **VA-C9 HEAVY back-frame.** Triggered by the stowed class at or above HEAVY (a comparison over the registry names, never a gram literal); the 30 kg `part_hull_plate` is the only MVP case. A strap-and-plate frame on the pack in Graphite mid with Hull Grey strap beads (thin elements), five facings, static; it **never covers the chest point or the beam's forward arc** (art lint at import, `ab:512`) and never breaks the helmet circle. It is an honest redundant witness for deaf and hard-of-hearing players on the one case where the cue is mandatory (IDB-21); it resolves only HEAVY versus not. It appears only after `add_stowed` succeeds, never from a loose item's sprite or a phantom pickup.
- **VA-C10 Carry pose and chest lamp housing.** Carry-walk holds the module **below** the lamp (or overhead), never across it (`ab:520`, `ab:512`). The housing is a static 4x4 px lens-and-bezel feature on the torso at `anchor_chest_<facing>` with **no on/off sprite state**; the light is the readout, and a beam-off housing in the dark is invisible (Principle 1).

### The visual ladder as a timeline

DOOR-BREACH (V 20, a suited seat with a full reserve; the Graph's LOW at 5.6 s, DECOMP at 14 s, VACUUM at 36 s after the breach), at the default `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` 600 (10 s) per advance; every value `[PLACEHOLDER]`:

| Time | Breath axis | What the eye sees (lit sprite) | What the ear hears (names only) | Reading (HUD) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| reserve engages | STRAINED | fog level 1 | valve click, then the closed breath cadence | reserve reading live; the O2 numeral falls |
| reserve at or below `RESERVE_WARN_S`, plus the 10 s hold | CRITICAL | fog level 2; **stagger** once; walk-only | breath shortens, ragged | mark on the strip's breath dial |
| at least 10 s after CRITICAL (source NONE) | FAILED | down animation; fog level 2 persists | laboured breath, then the gasp loop | reserve empty; mark persists |

The art side needs three **distinct** stages and has them: fog level 1; fog level 2 plus stagger plus mark; down. Descent is never rate-limited, so on re-entering breathable air the next presentation frame steps fog level 2 to level 1 (or off) with no easing. Cold fixture (an unpowered derelict A): COOL puffs begin; FROST adds rime level 1 and the shiver at rest; past the shifted onset, rime level 2, stagger and the mark beside the warmth glyph; then FAILED.

### Beam, tools and glowsticks (placed or read by Suit)

- **The beam.** Four shapes are identity and loadout (`bind_beam`, no change-shape action): WIDE 30 degrees / 288 px; NARROW 12 degrees / 384 px; SPLIT 10 degrees each at the Emitter's `SPLIT_AXIS_UNITS`, dark wedge never closing; FAN 60 degrees / 168 px. Tungsten family only; hard outer edge, soft interior falloff. Suit adds no hue, shape or edge change. **No dim, no flicker, no warm-up, no charge visual in the world**: low charge is shown only by the mark at `CELL_WARN_S` and the cell whine; the beam is constant until `force_off`, a one-tick hard cut. The origin is the per-facing chest point (`|x| <= 2`); the tool layer carries no light; carry, work, stagger and shiver frames keep the point and the forward arc clear. Co-op identity is shape plus a helmet trim-band count (1-4); Suit adds no per-player suit colour (`ab:284`).
- **Tools.** Four tool sprites (cutter, welder, scanner, suppressant), drawn once, never styled per dialect, carrying **no light**, two states each (held, in use). Tool state is a reading beside the glyph, never an icon swap (`ab:813`, `ab:815`): suppressant 0-3 as a count; the scanner has no charge; cutter and welder no state. Slots 5 and 6 are unbound and need no art. The scanner's screen, if it has a lit face, is a world reading (Pillar 4(d), `ab:961`); Suit specifies no scanner readout.
- **Glowsticks.** The pool is Chemlight `#86B896`, radius `GLOWSTICK_RADIUS_TILES` 2, steady for the first 60 % of life then waning in visible integer steps, never flickering, never a cone, non-shadow (Emitter and Lighting own it). Suit's obligations: a stowed stick is inert (no glow, no body light, no HUD glow); the rail count is Paper White (VA-R5); the count steps when the throw is accepted, not when the stick lands. The Chemlight versus O2 Cyan colour-blind pair is carried by shape: a stick is a free-standing omnidirectional pool with no travelling pattern, and the rail glyph is a stick silhouette on the 16 px grid.

### Reading requirements (the HUD & Suit Layer draws; UI Requirements lists the surfaces)

| Reading | Glyph (16x16 art px) | Hue rule (7.2: hue only while the source is alive) | Lie slot |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ambient O2 (`suit.o2`; strip dial unless the source is RESERVE) | thin ring clamp with three close chevrons (`ab:807`) | O2 Cyan while any breathable source exists (room air or reserve above zero); Graphite lit when none | yes (HR-5) |
| Suit O2 / reserve (`suit.reserve`, **new HR-3 reading**; on the strip's dial while the source is RESERVE) | the O2 ring clamp with a tank silhouette inside (7.2 forbids abstract symbols) | O2 Cyan while above zero; Graphite lit at zero | yes |
| Integrity (`suit.integrity`) | rivet line, three rivets and a hairline (`ab:811`); reused for the suit because the HUD carries no ship state | Hull Grey | yes |
| Warmth (`suit.warmth`) | no fitting silhouette exists (T and P are not a loop; CONFLICT VA-A8) | no loop hue: Paper White glyph on Ground with a zone band in the dial; the axis is `warmth_pm` 0..1000, COMFORT at 500, band edges 0, 333, 466, 533, 666, 1000 at the defaults (SE-29) | yes |
| Charge (`suit.charge`) | a cell silhouette (Suit's own store, not a Graph battery) | Paper White outline, fill in Tungsten fixture | yes |
| Rail: equipped tool, glowstick count, suppressant count, carried glyph | tool profile silhouettes on the 16 px grid (`ab:815`) | Paper White; equipped = Tungsten fixture; glowstick **never Chemlight**; suppressant Graphite lit at 0 | no: the rail counts are anchors (HS-34, SN-19; OQ-6 closed) |
| Warning mark | chamfered Alarm Red square with a Paper White stroke (`ab:817`) | Alarm Red on this mark only (HR-4: red is never text) | inherits its reading |

**Mark discipline (cry-wolf protection).** The mark fires only at CRITICAL on an axis (after the hold, so a transient never shows it) and at `CELL_WARN_S` on the charge reading; STRAINED never shows one (`ab:375`, rarity is the signal). At most five marks, all in the same beacon phase (2-3 s period, never faster, never a flicker above 3 Hz, HR-13); under reduced motion they hold solid (HR-8). **Reading motion:** dial arcs settle on change (damped, 0.25-0.4 s, overshoot at most 5 %); numerals tick one step per real change and never tween (`ab:833`, `ab:841`); the reserve ticks upward in whole steps during a top-up. **Tremor is not used for Suit readings** (it means "the loop is loading", `ab:834`). **One HR-5 motion signature** (glide on a dial needle; continuous tick or stale on a numeral) is the lie motion and is not active in the MVP, but each of the five readings must carry the gleam, motion-signature and sound slots so Vertical Slice A switches them on without redrawing (HR-5; test: with hue removed and with audio removed a trained tester still identifies the lying reading). The HUD never shakes, blurs, scales or flashes above 3 Hz in any state, including DOWNED and DECOMP. All reading text sits on opaque Ground plates (`#1C2128`); floors: numerals at least 21 px cap height at 1080p (7 art px), labels at least 15 px (5 art px), stroke at least 2 screen px (HR-12, provisional). The critical strip stays capped at 12 % x 5 % of the frame (`ab:751`); the strip's breath dial swaps between the ambient and suit O2 readings inside the same cap and glyph footprint (SE-30, OQ-41) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

### Palette, motion and legibility rules

1. **No new hue.** Only Section 4 hexes: Graphite mid and lit, Hull Grey (thin elements), Void Steel, Coolant Ice (rime only), Tungsten (beam, rail highlight), O2 Cyan and Hull Grey (reading glyph hue), Paper White (UI only), Alarm Red (mark only), Chemlight (the thrown stick's light only).
2. **Sanity palette is off-limits.** The only violet that ever touches a Suit surface is the stage-1 gleam on a lying reading (VS-A), fringe `#B07BE6`, never the core. Hull Grey back-frame beads are the 4.4 near-case: run the sampling test under every Tungsten source, including another player's beam landing on the pack; if the beads fail they fall back to Graphite lit and are never warmed.
3. **Loop hues keep their meaning.** Suit reuses a loop hue only for a *reading* of that loop's medium on the suit panel; the body never wears one (a body cue in O2 Cyan would read as a pipe leak).
4. **Greyscale ladder.** Rime (L* about 91) versus the beam core (about 92) by location and shape; the Graphite-lit fog strip (about 43) versus the Void Steel strip (about 5) is a large step so level 2 survives greyscale; the Alarm Red mark (about 47) versus the violet core (about 43) by shape (a chamfered square, not an edge gleam); Hull Grey beads (about 61) and Fuel Ember (about 61) are never co-located on the Suit.
5. **Stiff-weighted motion** (`ab §5.2`, PC): the Presenter writes only frame index, `flip_h`, integer position, overlay visibility and the beam light's position and rotation — never scale, rotation, skew or tweened offsets on the body. Overlays are visibility toggles between painted levels: no alpha fades, no easing.
6. **Cue grid and clocks.** Levels change on the 0.1 s grid; the Presenter shows the new level on the next presentation frame and does not interpolate. The body cadence is a fixed 12 fps; particles run on the presentation clock, never the sim, and nothing reads them back (EM-29). On restore the Presenter clears every Suit particle pool and re-derives the levels on the first frame; on pause the world freezes undimmed with the particles frozen.
7. **No uncaused motion.** Shiver (cold), stagger (cap edge), jet (leak) and puff (exhale) each have a stated cause in the true state; a cue that moves without its true cause is a defect, and for a reading it is a *glide*.
8. **Zoom.** Overlays are authored at the default and survive the integer zoom-out step (50 % check, tier-2 marks, `ab:576`); particle sizes, offsets and the chest anchor scale by the same integer.
9. **Reduced motion (HR-8).** The shiver holds one frame; vapour and jet still emit (trail propagation is never suppressed, `ab:979`) at a lower rate; marks hold solid.
10. **No flash.** No Suit event flashes the frame; damage, breach, stagger and DOWNED never use a full-frame or HUD flash (`ab:845`).
11. **Own-avatar visibility in the dark.** The wearer's sprite is lit by the wearer's own lamp from its chest origin, so the torso, pack top and helmet front sit inside the lamp's near field while the beam is on. With the beam **off** the sprite is unlit unless a fixture, glowstick or crewmate's beam lands on it: the cues are then invisible by design and the audio witnesses carry them. **Requirement for the lighting spike (`ab §8.12` step 2):** a lit-sprite check in three conditions (own lamp on; own lamp off with a glowstick adjacent; own lamp off in black) with retained screenshots, showing rime and fog levels 1 and 2 distinguishable in the first two and absent in the third. If the own-lamp near field does not reach the helmet at the narrow cone's chest origin, the fallback is a very low "self-lit" rim (a thin specular highlight on the visor and plate edges only) that the spike may add without changing any sim value; it must not count as illumination for prompts and must not alter the revealed radius (an anchor).
12. **Co-op summary (VS-B).** Rime (`cue_cold`), fog (`cue_fog`), stagger, walk-only and down (`locomotion_cap`, `vitals_state`), carry-walk (`carried_class`) and beam shape draw from the eight-field `SHARED_SUMMARY`. The **seam jet** and the **HEAVY back-frame** cannot be derived from it (`cue_load` is a max over carried and stowed class), so the crew cannot see them until the requested amendment (`cue_leak`, `stowed_heavy`; OQ-5) lands; neither exposes a reserve, a charge or an integrity number.

### Audio requirements (names only; Adaptive Audio & Tells owns the design)

Every body cue has an audio witness that starts **no later than its visual** (HR-9, `ab:980`). Mood: dry, close, claustrophobic, mechanical; no musical sting and no danger stinger. Names: breath cadence steps (normal, closed-reserve, thin-air, load), valve click on reserve engage and disengage, pack-creak load layer with a footfall-linked exhale, suit rattle (shiver while moving), pack cooling-fan whirr and tick (heat side, rising with the rung), seam hiss (leak), cell whine at `CELL_WARN_S`, equip clunk, top-up hiss, stagger gasp, glowstick throw, land and wane (the Emitter's), gasp loop (source NONE). **Load versus thin air** are separable by cadence *direction*: load adds depth, the creak layer and a footfall-linked exhale at the air class's own rate; thin air is shallower and faster with no creak; a blind two-clip test (at least 90 % identification, AC-SE-42) is the precondition for the Vertical Slice A phantom-salvage check. Suit emits **no noise** into Attention in the MVP (SE-31): the cadence of these sounds is presentation of a true state, not a stimulus. Cue sounds belong to the consequence channel: a hallucination may neither remove a real valve click nor add one (the Graph's closed list).

Naming per `ab §8.5`: `<cat>[_<dialect>]_<subject>[_<variant tokens>]_<map>.png`; levels use the `index` token so no new `state` token is added. Counts are drawn images.

| Asset | Stem (proposed) | Cell | Count | Maps | Budget delta |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Rime overlay, levels 1 and 2 | `ovl_vital_rime_01`, `_02` | 80x80, 5 facings | 10 | alb | +10 with fog (the bible budgets about 10 for both) |
| Visor fog overlay, levels 1 and 2 | `ovl_vital_fog_01`, `_02` | 80x80, 5 facings | 10 | alb + spc | counted with rime |
| Shiver idle frames | `char_crew_body_alb` tag `idle_shiver_<facing>` | 80x80 | 10 | alb, nrm, spc | +10 |
| HEAVY back-frame | `ovl_stow_heavy` | 80x80, 5 facings | 5 | alb, nrm, spc | +5 |
| Seam jet and vapour particle textures | `vfx_suit_seamjet`, `vfx_suit_vapour` | 4x4 and 8x8 | 4 | alb | 0 painted frames |

| Tool layer | `tool_cutter`, `tool_welder`, `tool_scanner`, `tool_suppressant` (tags `held_`, `use_`) | 80x80 | 40 (30 under Option A of OQ-34, which drops the suppressant) | alb, nrm, spc | within the budgeted 60 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Glowstick held and throw; world sprites | `tool_glowstick`; `prop_glowstick` (`_msk`) | 80x80; 16x16 and 24x24 | 10; about 4 | alb, nrm, spc | within budget; the prop is Interaction's and the Emitter's |
| HUD glyphs and mark | `ui_glyph_reserve_16` (new), `ui_glyph_charge_16` (new), `ui_glyph_warmth_16` (open, VA-A8), integrity, tool and carried glyphs; the suppressant-at-0 mark (a diagonal cut by default; `ui_mark_empty_08` only if the art-director rules a state-tick exception to 7.2) | 16x16, 8x8 | about 13 | ui | the HUD's budget |

**Net: about +25 painted images** over the `ab §5.7` vital-overlay budget (fog and rime at two levels, +10; shiver, +10; back-frame, +5); particle and UI assets add no sprite-sheet cost.

## Game Feel

*A real note, not a template fill.* Suit has almost no input of its own (one command, `equip_tool`) and no animation; its feel is **the honesty and timing of a body that is running out**. The feel of the walk, the stagger and the work loop belongs to the Controller (PC Game Feel), the feel of how light falls on a rimed shoulder to 2D Lighting, the feel of every sound to Audio; this section owns the numbers underneath them and the one promise Suit makes: **the body is the last instrument that does not lie**, and it speaks slowly enough to be heard and fast enough to be fair.

**Feel references.** Pandorum's helmet light and breath in a dead corridor for *yours, hands-free, costing something*; Subnautica's oxygen for *a clock the player reads in a number and a sound*; Project Zomboid for *death is a bitch, it happens* (the user's decision), with the difference that here every death is preceded by a tell the player could have read; Don't Starve for *a few honest numbers and a lot of world*. The target is **dry, close and mechanical: a suit, not a hero**. The anti-reference is the health bar with a red vignette.

**Input responsiveness.**

| Chain | Steps (ticks at `TICK_RATE` 60) | Budget |
|---|---|---|
| `equip_tool` (press to rail highlight) | the input is sampled in the physics callback before the kernel steps; the Adapter submits the command; validate and apply run at P2 in the same tick; the Presenter plays the hand animation on the **same frame** (the acknowledgement never encodes success) and the rail highlight moves on the committed state at the next presentation frame | at most 1 tick plus 1 render frame (about 17 ms plus one frame) from press to committed `equipped_slot`; no equip time beyond the hand animation (SE-11); no noise |
| `toggle_beam` (press to light) | Suit contributes only the `charge` read in `validate`; the apply is at P2 and the light is committed by `emitter.place` in the same tick | zero Suit latency; at most 1 tick from apply to the lit state the renderer sees (Emitter State Game Feel); the click pre-plays and the light never does |
| Beam off at zero cell | `suit.vitals` drains 6 ticks per run from `emitter.state.lit` as of T-1 and calls `force_off` on the run the cell reaches 0 (P3); `emitter.place` follows in the same tick | the cone is gone the **same tick** as the run that empties the cell; a drain error of at most 5 ticks (0.083 s) per toggle in either direction is accepted and stated |
| A cue after a physical change | the Graph's `graph.propagate.atmo` and `suit.vitals` share offset 0 and divisor 6, so Suit reads the new band enum in the same tick the Graph commits it; the Presenter shows the level at the next presentation frame | at most 6 ticks (0.1 s) of run cadence plus 1 tick plus 1 frame from the physical state change to the on-screen cue; about 117 ms in the worst case |
| Reserve engage (valve click) | the run on which the enums say the air is unbreathable and the reserve is above zero | the click plays on that run's cue edge, not before; the fog and the closed breath cadence follow on the same frame |
| Cap edge to stagger | `locomotion_cap` is committed at P3; `controller.step` follows the same tick | the stagger starts the tick the cap falls (Suit runs before the Controller); `STAGGER_TICKS` 30 (0.5 s) is the Controller's input lock |

**The ladder as felt time (defaults, all `[PH]`).** The player should feel a body failing in three beats, never one: the first cue (fog level 1, the click, the shorter breath, the shiver and rime) arrives the moment the true state allows; the second beat, the stagger into walk-only with the mark, arrives **at least 10 s** later (`RUNG_MIN_TICKS` 600 ticks) and takes the run away; the third, the collapse, arrives **at least 20 s after the first cue** (`SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`, itself at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 s) and starts the Controller's 30 s window (`DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` 1800). Worked on the locked fixtures: DOOR-BREACH with a full reserve is 108.8 s from the first cue to FAILED and with an empty reserve 28.8 s; a suited seat in the O2-ROOM fixture is about 340 s; FREEZE-BREACH is about 10.5 min; a SCORCH room gives 30 s at the minimum. The reserve is 90 s of engaged breath with the warn edge 30 s before empty; the lamp cell is 20 min of lit beam with its warn edge 20 s before dark. **No state is faster than the 20 s floor on an endogenous path**; an exogenous hit (friendly harm, a Horror act) takes its own tell from its own GDD and lands per step, so the victim's rung tracks the harm inside the channel (SE-23).

**Weight without a number.** Mass never changes speed or noise (SE-28): the Controller's carry state (`V_CARRY` 1 against `V_WALK` 2 and `V_RUN` 3) depends on carrying a module, not on its mass class, and stowed parts change nothing about movement. Weight is **heard**: three breath steps (LIGHT baseline, MEDIUM, HEAVY) where each load step adds *depth, a pack-creak layer and a footfall-linked exhale* and never changes the rate, which belongs to the air class alone (SE-28); thin air is *shallower and faster with no creak*. Provisional audio targets for the spike (`[PH]`, Audio owns the final values): baseline period about 4 s; thin air about 2.5 s (the rate goes up); MEDIUM the baseline period with the breath about 20 % deeper and a 0.6 s creak; HEAVY about 40 % deeper with a 0.9 s creak; the closed reserve cadence about 3 s with a dry rasp; CRITICAL ragged and under 2 s. The test that matters is not the numbers but the **direction** (load: deeper and creaking at the air's own rate; thin: faster and shallower), and it is a blind test (AC-SE-42, at least 90 % correct).

**What the lamp costs.** The beam is constant until `force_off`: no dim, no flicker, no warm-up, no charge visual in the world. The cost is felt in two places only — the cell whine and the mark at 20 s remaining, then the hard cut into the dark. The cut is the strongest single beat Suit owns, so it is never softened: a fade is a lamp lying about its power, a flicker is the flare's word (`ab:286`). A turn-ON at zero is refused with `CHARGE_EMPTY` and a failed-action cue that does not shake the frame; the lamp stays dark and the glowstick becomes the plan.

**Impact moments.** (1) *The valve click:* the sound of the reserve taking over, then the sealed, closed cadence and the whole strip of the visor greying — the player learns the click means *the room is no longer breathable* before any tutorial says so. (2) *The first stagger:* a half-second input lock into a walk, no shake, no flash, the run simply gone; the weight of it is the missing speed. (3) *The plate on the back:* picking up the 30 kg hull plate and hearing the breath deepen and the pack creak with the frame appearing on the shoulders — the honest field check. (4) *The cell going out:* a hard cut to black on the chest lamp. (5) *A crewmate's shoulders rimed in your beam,* the one time the player learns the crewmate's temperature without a number. (6) *The DOWNED seat's lamp still burning* while the 30 s window runs. None of these is allowed a screen effect, a stinger or a camera move.

**Anti-targets.** No health bar and no regeneration; no screen vignette, tunnel, blur, frost edge or damage flash (SE-27); no danger stinger or low-health alarm; no speed buff, no adrenaline, no reduced noise at low vitals (the Controller's rhythm independence); no weight-slowed movement and no inventory screen; no auto-refill of the reserve; no readable body-temperature number; no equip animation that locks the feet; no dimming lamp; no camera shake on any Suit event; no cue that speeds up or slows with sanity.

**Onboarding: the player learns the vocabulary by living through it, not by dying through it** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; requirement exported to Derelict Authoring and Boarding & Salvage, OQ-42)*. As specified, nothing guaranteed that a first reserve engagement, a first thin-air breath or a first cold exposure happens where it is survivable, so the cue vocabulary could be learned only by losing up to 20 minutes. Requirement: the authored first sortie must offer, each on a survivable branch and each before any lethal instance of the same hazard, (a) a short unbreathable traverse of at most `RESERVE_S` / 4 (22 s walking) with breathable rooms at both ends, so the first valve click, fog and closed cadence happen where the exit is reversible; (b) a THIN room that is breathable at its worst; (c) a FROST room with an exit that is not through a lethal strip; and (d) the home-ship O2_TANK port in the starting corridor, so the top-up ritual is met before the first sortie. The cell has a silent advisory step 120 s before the warning (`CELL_ADVISE_S`), so the 20 s warning is never the first the player hears of a 20-minute resource. The proof is AC-SE-92 (a naive-player playtest).

**Feel acceptance (AC-SE-84 to AC-SE-89)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the unnumbered AC-FEEL block, now six numbered criteria; all manual, Visual/Feel, with retained screenshots or clips in `production/qa/evidence/` and lead sign-off by the game-designer and art-director, and the audio-director for sound items; the blind audio test is AC-SE-42, the precondition for the Vertical Slice A phantom-salvage check)*:

- **AC-SE-84 (manual) Equip chain (SE-11).** GIVEN a build, WHEN `equip_tool` is pressed, THEN the rail highlight moves within 1 tick plus 1 render frame of the press (a frame-stepped clip), with no equip time beyond the hand animation and no noise.
- **AC-SE-85 (manual) Ladder timeline on DOOR-BREACH (SE-17).** GIVEN a suited seat with a full reserve on DOOR-BREACH, WHEN the clip is captured, THEN three distinct stages are visible (fog level 1; fog level 2 with the stagger and the mark; down), and no stage change comes less than 10 s after the previous one (the `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` hold).
- **AC-SE-86 (manual) Cell cut (SE-8).** GIVEN the cell reaching 0 with the beam lit, WHEN a frame-stepped clip is captured, THEN the cone is gone in a single tick with no preceding dim or flicker.
- **AC-SE-87 (manual) Valve click timing (HR-9).** GIVEN the reserve engaging, WHEN audio and video are captured together, THEN the click starts no later than the frame of fog level 1.
- **AC-SE-88 (manual) Stagger (SE-35, VA-C6).** GIVEN a cap falling edge, WHEN captured, THEN the stagger is an input lock with frames only: no displacement, no camera shake and no screen effect.
- **AC-SE-89 (manual) HEAVY step (SE-28).** GIVEN a stowed `part_hull_plate`, WHEN clips are captured with and without the back-frame, THEN the breath gains the HEAVY step in both and the back-frame appears only after `add_stowed` succeeds.

## UI Requirements

Suit has **no screen of its own**. It supplies true values to three surfaces that the HUD & Suit Layer draws (designed, `hud-and-suit-layer.md`, which repeats this section). The art bible's HUD is a suit worn by the player (`ab §7.0`): a thin reading register on opaque Ground plates, with hue only while the source is alive. Layout, glyph art, numerals and motion are the HUD's and the art-director's; the requirements below say what must be **shown**, **from which value**, **when**, and — as importantly — what must never exist.

### Surfaces

| Surface | Contents (all from Suit) | Source value | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Critical strip** (centre-bottom; capped at 12 % x 5 % of the frame, `ab:751`) | the **breath dial** (arc and numeral, HR-12: the numeral beside the arc) and the breath axis's one warning-mark anchor (the mark sits beside the dial at breath CRITICAL, whichever reading the dial is showing) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | **while the breath source is RESERVE** the dial shows the **suit O2**, `true_value(suit.reserve, player_id)` (the death clock); **in every other source** it shows the **ambient O2**, `true_value(suit.o2, player_id)` = the **suit-sensed `p_o2` (dkPa) of the occupant's compartment** — the suit's reading of compartment atmosphere, not a Graph overlay (`lsg:51`, `lsg:1000`) | The strip stays inside its cap and swaps the content of one dial; the ambient numeral is demoted to the suit panel while the dial shows the suit O2 (the HUD places it; the art-director confirms the swap fits the strip and HR-3, OQ-41). The numeral is the reading that may lie in Vertical Slice A; the body cue beside it never does. |
| **Suit panel** (bottom-left) | **reserve** (own glyph: the O2 ring clamp with a tank silhouette; seconds or permille, the HUD picks the format), **integrity**, **warmth** (a zone band in the dial, **no temperature number**), **charge** (cell fill; absent when `CELL_ENABLED` is 0); the warning mark beside the warmth, integrity or charge reading at its CRITICAL (the breath axis's mark is the strip's) | `true_value` with keys `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge` | **One mark anchor per axis:** the breath mark sits on the strip's breath dial at breath CRITICAL whatever the breath source (it no longer moves between the reserve glyph and the O2 reading), beside the warmth glyph at warmth CRITICAL, the integrity glyph at integrity CRITICAL, and the charge glyph at `CELL_WARN_S`. Never at STRAINED. |
| **Hand rail** (bottom-right) | the four tool glyphs in slot order (cutter, welder, scanner, suppressant); the **equipped** tool in Tungsten fixture colour with the HR-11 bracket; the suppressant charges 0-3; the glowstick count 0-4 as a stick glyph with a number; the carried-module glyph when carrying | `tool_in_hand`, `tool_charge(player_id, def_id)`, `stowed_count(player_id, cons_glowstick)`, `carried_instance` | A suppressant at 0 is greyed (Graphite lit with a diagonal cut by default; an 8x8 empty mark only if the art-director rules a state-tick exception to 7.2), not hidden, not a banner. The glowstick glyph is Paper White, never Chemlight. Key and button glyphs swap with the active device (the Controller's rule). |

### Requirements

- **UI-1 Wrap point.** The five readings reach the HUD **only** through the Filter's `perceived`, which wraps `true_value(key, player_id)`, keys `suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`, so the Perception Filter has a single wrap point (SE-29). The reading fields of `view(player_id)` (`o2_pp_dkpa`, `reserve_pm`, `cell_pm`, `integrity_pm`) are pre-wrap values for cue derivation and diagnostics; a HUD that draws a number from them is a defect (lint AC-SE-77). The rail counts (suppressant, glowsticks) are **anchors** with no key (HUD HS-34, Sanity SN-19; OQ-6 closed): the HUD reads them through `tool_charge` and `stowed_count`, and they are honest at MVP and Vertical Slice A.
- **UI-2 MVP honesty.** Every reading shows the true value in the MVP. Each of the five carries the HR-5 tri-modal lie slots (violet fringe gleam, one HR-5 motion signature — glide on a dial needle, continuous tick or stale on a numeral — and sound) as dormant slots so Vertical Slice A can enable a lie without a redraw.
- **UI-3 The mark is rare and rung-based.** The warning mark fires only at CRITICAL on an axis (after the hold) and at `CELL_WARN_S`; at most five at once, in one beacon phase (2-3 s), never above 3 Hz, solid under reduced motion. It inherits its reading's class and so is **not** an independent honest backup.
- **UI-4 Update model.** The HUD refreshes a surface when the seat's dirty key `S/<PlayerId>` changes or at render rate, whichever the HUD ADR chooses (→ ADR candidate); a value never animates between two committed values except the dial settle (0.25-0.4 s, overshoot at most 5 %); numerals tick one step per real change.
- **UI-5 Pause, DOWNED, DEAD.** While paused the HUD is frozen and undimmed and `equip_tool` is unavailable (SE-32). While DOWNED the readings keep updating (the seat is still breathing and draining) and the lamp stays lit. A DEAD seat's readings freeze; the restore shows no death screen (SL-8).
- **UI-6 Co-op.** The crew strip carries **no vitals** by default (`ab:902`): a teammate's condition is read by lighting their visor, not from a bar (SE-36). The player's own identity glyph (beam shape plus trim-band count) belongs to the suit panel at Vertical Slice B.
- **UI-7 Accessibility.** Every reading has a numeral or a zone band as well as a hue (colour is redundant with shape and position); the mark is a chamfered square with a stroke, readable in greyscale; reading text meets the HR-12 floors (numerals at least 7 art px cap height, labels at least 5); the UI scale and device glyph settings are anchors that no game state may read or alter (HR-6); under reduced motion the marks hold solid and the dial settle becomes a step. Every body cue has an audio and, where cheap, a visual redundancy (the HEAVY back-frame for the mandatory case); the thin spots (the heat side, the MEDIUM load class) are recorded in Open Questions.

### What must never exist (anti-requirements)

No inventory screen, grid or per-part count on any Suit surface (a part count is read by Interaction's repair prompt); **no weight readout** (the mass cue is the breath); **no body-temperature number**; no sanity indicator and no Attention meter on any Suit surface (`ab:741`); no screen-edge tint, vignette or tunnel as a vital indicator (SE-27); no health bar; no banner or toast for a vitals change; no text in Alarm Red (HR-4). Test infrastructure that forces a seat's bands, rungs, cell and reserve for the manual criteria (a debug console owed by the lead-programmer) is **not** a player surface and ships behind the debug flag only.

> **📌 UX Flag — Suit & Equipment**: this system has UI requirements (the critical-strip O2 reading, the suit panel with the reserve, integrity, warmth and charge readings and their warning marks, and the hand rail with the tools, the suppressant and glowstick counts and the carried-module glyph). In Pre-Production, run `/ux-design` for the HUD spec before writing epics; stories should cite `design/ux/hud.md`, not this GDD directly. Noted for the systems index.
