## Acceptance Criteria

The criteria below close the QA review (Stage 7): 92 numbered criteria and 150 one-test-per-THEN sub-criteria *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the earlier count of 83 understated the real number of test cases by about 3x, because about 15 criteria were compound, snapshot-based, disjunctive or statistically unsound and have been split)*, every one GIVEN / WHEN / THEN with its default figures inlined (each names the knob it comes from), and a **Gaps / untestable as written** list at the end. Rules cite their `SE-n`, formulas their `D.n`. Examples use only the ids of SE-40. Fixtures are the Graph's locked D.15 rows (DOOR-BREACH, O2-ROOM, O2-SHIP, FREEZE-BREACH, SHIP-COLD) unless stated; a fixture time is "derived; the Graph harness confirms to +/-15 %", so an absolute step is asserted only against the locked harness output and the relative form always holds. `s_h` below is the run on which the Graph first reports HYPOXIC with LOW pressure (64 in the locked DOOR-BREACH harness). **Fixture traces are pinned by hash** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: every Graph fixture an auto criterion uses is the committed harness output `tests/unit/suit/fixtures/graph_<fixture>.trace`, pinned by its SHA-256 in `tests/unit/suit/fixtures/graph_traces.sha256`; a changed hash fails the test as "Graph fixture changed" before any Suit assertion runs, so a Graph retune surfaces as a fixture update and never as a silent Suit failure. **Split criteria:** a bare id such as AC-SE-1, 7, 8, 9, 12, 19, 20, 28, 34, 36, 38, 42, 43, 44, 45, 47, 49, 53, 79 or 83, cited elsewhere in this document, means all of its lettered sub-criteria.

**Tag legend, story type and gate (testing standard).**

| Tag | Meaning | Story type, location | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| **auto** | a headless test on `Kernel.step()`; `auto/integration` is the Integration sub-tag | Logic, `tests/unit/suit/`; Integration, `tests/integration/suit/` | BLOCKING |
| **lint** | a deterministic source or data scan that runs in CI like a unit test | Logic-class evidence | BLOCKING (AC-SE-1a-b, 26, 32, 38b, 77, 83a are hard guards for P1 and P4) |
| **review** | a reader checks named text in this or a counterpart GDD | design review | ADVISORY per story, but BLOCKING for the story that owns the counterpart GDD (AC-SE-34, 41, 59); interim result **NOT ASSESSED, never PASS** while the counterpart is absent |
| **manual** | a person runs the build; Visual/Feel items need a retained screenshot **and** lead sign-off, UI items a retained screenshot of each screen, in `production/qa/evidence/` | Visual/Feel, UI | BLOCKING |
| **spike** | a bounded experiment whose result may change a knob; records a decision | — | never blocks Done |
| **perf** | `tests/performance/suit/`, machine recorded | — | ADVISORY |

**NOT ASSESSED on a BLOCKING review item** *(rule added 2026-10-27 — design review; the file had none)*. A `review` criterion whose counterpart is absent, unruled or not yet final returns NOT ASSESSED, never PASS and never FAIL. NOT ASSESSED (a) is an open blocker on the **owning story** named in the criterion (the counterpart's story: Save/Load for AC-SE-34a, Derelict Authoring for 75, the HUD and accessibility owner for 91), so that story cannot be marked Done and `/gate-check` reports it as a named CONCERN; (b) does not block a Suit story, whose own automated criteria stand alone; (c) carries an owner, a target (the Open Questions "Target" column) and the date it was first recorded, and is re-run whenever the counterpart changes; (d) never counts toward a coverage pass. A USER DECISION that gates a criterion makes that criterion NOT ASSESSED until it is ruled (AC-SE-16, 34a, 91).

**Acceptance criteria to story type and gate** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; story ids do not exist yet, so the table maps by group and story type, `/create-stories` assigns ids)*:

| Group | Criteria | Story type and location | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| A. Block, hook, keying, room | 1a-1c, 2-5 | Logic, `tests/unit/suit/` | BLOCKING (1c review, ADVISORY per story) |
| B. Breath, reserve, top-up | 6, 7a-c, 8a-c, 9a-e, 46, 49a-c, 51, 52, 63-65 | Logic | BLOCKING (9e only if OQ-2 is ruled PASSIVE) |
| C. Beam, cell, shape, glowsticks | 10, 11, 12a-c, 70, 90 | Logic / Integration, `tests/integration/suit/` | BLOCKING; 47a is a spike |
| D. Tools, holders, carry | 13-15, 17, 40, 67-69, 79a, 80 | Logic | BLOCKING; 16 conditional on OQ-34 Option B |
| E. Ladder, thermal, integrity, death | 18, 19a-f, 20a-c, 21, 22a-e, 23-25, 43a-e, 44a-b, 50, 62, 66, 73, 74, 76, 81, 82 | Logic | BLOCKING; 41 and 75 are review, NOT ASSESSED until the counterpart lands |
| F. Cues, readings, consequence channel | 26, 29, 30, 45a-d, 77, 78 (Logic); 28a-c, 31, 53a-f (Visual/Feel and UI: retained screenshot + sign-off); 27, 91 (review); 42a-b (spike) | as stated | BLOCKING for Logic, Visual/Feel and UI; 27 and 91 per the NOT ASSESSED rule; 42 never blocks Done |
| G. Seams, save, determinism | 32, 33, 34b, 35, 36a-f, 37, 38a-c, 39, 44a, 47b, 48a, 48c-d, 71, 72, 79b-c, 83a-b | Logic / Integration | BLOCKING; 34a is review, NOT ASSESSED pending OQ-13 |
| H. Cross-system | 54-58, 60 (Integration); 59, 61 (review); 84-89 (Visual/Feel manual); 92 (playtest) | Integration, Visual/Feel, playtest | BLOCKING for 54-58, 60, 84-89; 92 is a spike that never blocks Done and returns to OQ-42 on failure |
| I. Performance | 48b | perf, `tests/performance/suit/` | ADVISORY |

Test infrastructure owed by the programmers (without it the manual and auto items cannot run): (1) a debug state-forcing console (bands, rungs, reserve, cell, integrity on a seat; not a shippable verb); (2) a Graph and Emitter stub pair returning the `draw_air` grant synchronously or at the next phase 0; (3) a committed demand-trace corpus and its generator; (4) a multi-seat fixture scene (`MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16 where a criterion says so); (5) a reference performance machine definition.

### A. Block, hook, keying, room (SE-1 to SE-3)

- **AC-SE-1a (lint) Band literals (SE-1, SE-4, SE-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the Suit source (`src/**/suit/**`) and data (`assets/data/suit/**`), WHEN grepped for the registry values of `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`, `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, `HYPOXIA_PP` and `DECOMP_P` used as numeric operands of a comparison against `T`, `p_o2`, `p` or any band variable, THEN zero hits and the four names appear as identifiers.
- **AC-SE-1b (lint) Stale tokens in code and data (SE-40).** GIVEN the same source and data, WHEN grepped for the retired and stale tokens (`vitals.state`, `consume_glowstick`, `stow(`, `unstow`, `has_glowstick`, `equipped_tool`, `apply_harm`, `peer_id`, and an `after:` naming `graph.propagate` with no suffix), THEN zero hits.
- **AC-SE-1c (review) Stale tokens in the GDD (guard rail 2).** GIVEN Formulas, Visual/Audio, UI Requirements and Tuning Knobs (not Core Rules, Interactions or Cross-References, which quote counterpart text), WHEN scanned for the same tokens and the four band values, THEN zero hits; the allowlist is the SE-40 "Retired names" sentence, AC-SE-1a, AC-SE-39 and quoted counterpart text.
- **AC-SE-2 (auto) Hook order (SE-1).** GIVEN a booted kernel with the Graph, Suit, Controller, Body and Emitter registrations, WHEN the hook order is read, THEN it is `graph.propagate.power`, `graph.propagate.atmo`, `graph.propagate.failure`, with `suit.vitals` after `graph.propagate.atmo`, then `controller.step`, `body.integrate`, `emitter.place`; AND Suit declares only `after: [graph.propagate.atmo]` at divisor 6, offset 0, and never `after: emitter.place`.
- **AC-SE-3 (auto) Boot failures (SE-1, D.8 A12).** GIVEN `suit.vitals` registered with an unknown `after` id, or a cycle, WHEN the kernel boots, THEN boot fails with the kernel's registration error; GIVEN `graph.propagate.atmo` at offset 2 and Suit at offset 0, THEN the kernel emits its never-co-running warning for the pair AND boot fails naming A12; GIVEN both at offset 2, THEN boot succeeds with zero warnings from A12.
- **AC-SE-4 (auto) PlayerId keying (SE-2).** GIVEN a four-seat block (fixture `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16), WHEN it is snapshotted, a seat is re-bound to a different peer and the snapshot is restored, THEN no key in the snapshot is shaped like a peer id, every per-seat field is reachable by PlayerId, and the restored seat is byte-equal.
- **AC-SE-5 (auto) Seat room (SE-3).** GIVEN a seat on a door sill, WHEN `suit.vitals` runs, THEN the bands are those of the door's `sill_room`; GIVEN the seat crosses a door, THEN its bands change within one run; GIVEN `room_at` answers NONE with `loco_state` not IN_POD (a fault-injected defect state, BDY-29), THEN the seat is evaluated against the EXTERIOR by rule (`band_p` VACUUM, `band_o2` ANOXIC, raw `T` = `T_AMB_MC`), never the bands of `last_room_ref`, no `draw_air` is issued, and the reserve, the cell and the thermal load keep running.

### B. Breath, reserve, top-up (SE-4 to SE-7; D.1, D.2, D.8, D.9)

- **AC-SE-6 (auto) Breath-source sweep (SE-4, D.1).** GIVEN the 16 `(band_o2, band_p)` pairs and reserve above 0 and equal to 0 (32 cases), WHEN `suit_breath_source` runs, THEN with reserve above 0: ROOM for 4 pairs (`band_o2` NORMAL or THIN with `band_p` NORMAL or LOW), RESERVE for the other 12; with reserve 0: ROOM 4, GASP 2 (HYPOXIC with NORMAL or LOW), NONE 10. GIVEN the DOOR-BREACH, O2-ROOM and O2-SHIP fixtures with a suited seat, THEN the first RESERVE run equals the first run the Graph's enums say unbreathable (DOOR-BREACH: the HYPOXIC-with-LOW entry, step 64 against the locked harness) and no earlier run is RESERVE.
- **AC-SE-7a (auto) Shortfall debit (SE-5, D.2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a test-injected request of 500,000 mGU against an `n_o2` of 300,000, WHEN the Graph grants and publishes `draw_grant(crew)`, THEN granted is 300,000 and the 200,000 shortfall (the breath part only) is debited from the reserve on the next run.
- **AC-SE-7b (auto) Ascending order (SE-5).** GIVEN three seats (fixture `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16) in a room of 450 mGU, THEN grants are 300, 150, 0 in ascending PlayerId order and the shorted seats debit their own reserves.
- **AC-SE-7c (auto) No flap under either timing (SE-4, SE-5).** GIVEN a stub returning the grant at the next run's phase 0 and a stub returning it synchronously, THEN the breath source never flaps in either.
- **AC-SE-8a (auto) Reserve bounds (SE-6, D.8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN any trace, WHEN the reserve is read every run, THEN it is within 0..`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` and never negative.
- **AC-SE-8b (auto) Reserve debits (SE-6, D.2).** GIVEN any trace, WHEN the reserve falls in a run, THEN the fall equals exactly the sum of (i) `min(R_BREATH, reserve)` if the source was RESERVE, (ii) `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` (clamped to what is left) if `integrity_bp` was below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` at the start of the run, and (iii) the SE-5 shortfall debit, `pending_draw_mgu` minus the breath part of last run's `draw_grant` (clamped to the reserve), and nothing else; the largest single-run fall is 750 mGU at the defaults (debit 300, spend 300, leak 150); and in VERB mode the reserve rises only through `credit_reserve`.
- **AC-SE-8c (auto) Felt duration (D.8).** GIVEN `R_BREATH` doubled in data, THEN `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` doubles and the reserve in seconds is unchanged.
- **AC-SE-9a (auto) Already full (SE-7) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a full reserve or a full cell, WHEN `top_up` validate runs, THEN `ALREADY_FULL`.
- **AC-SE-9b (auto) O2 credit (D.9).** GIVEN a reserve deficit d at an O2_TANK port, THEN the credit is `min(granted, d)` and the tank's stock falls by exactly the credit.
- **AC-SE-9c (auto) BATTERY credit in the stock domain (D.9).** GIVEN a cell deficit and a complete grant of `units_req`, THEN `credited_ticks` equals the deficit and `stock_debit` equals `ceil(credited_ticks × CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK / 1000)` with dust 0 (36,000 ticks and 72,000 units at the default); GIVEN a partial grant of 7 units at the default, THEN `credited_ticks` is 3, `stock_debit` is 7, the dust is 1 and the credit never exceeds the deficit; GIVEN a grant of 1 unit, THEN 0 ticks are credited and the dust is 1.
- **AC-SE-9d (auto) Granted 0 (D.9).** GIVEN a granted 0, THEN no `credit_*` call is made, no reason token is returned and the command is Applied.
- **AC-SE-9e (auto) PASSIVE configuration, dormant until OQ-2 is ruled (SE-7, D.2, D.9).** GIVEN `TOPUP_MODE` PASSIVE, THEN `CELL_ENABLED` is 0 and the reserve refills only while `band_o2` is NORMAL and the room stays at or above `TOPUP_MIN_PP`; AND GIVEN a request of `R_BREATH` + 30 granted at exactly `R_BREATH`, THEN the reserve is neither credited nor debited; granted in full, THEN it rises by 30; granted 250 of 330, THEN it is debited 50.
- **AC-SE-49a (auto) Same revision, out of range (D.8, SE-34) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a save with `reserve_mgu` above `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` under the **same** `data_rev`, WHEN restored, THEN `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE` fails the restore and nothing is clamped.
- **AC-SE-49b (auto) Legitimate retune is rescaled (SE-34).** GIVEN a save made at `data_rev` 1 with `R_BREATH` 600 and a full reserve (540,000), WHEN restored under `data_rev` 2 with `R_BREATH` 300 (`RESERVE_MAX_MGU` 270,000) and a history row for revision 1, THEN the restore succeeds, `reserve_mgu` is 270,000 (the same 100 % fraction), `cell_ticks` keeps its fraction, a hold above its new maximum is reduced to it, `suit_restore_rescaled` is emitted once, and a half-full reserve (270,000 of 540,000) restores to 135,000.
- **AC-SE-49c (auto) Unknown revision (SE-34).** GIVEN a save whose `data_rev` is newer than the build or absent from `rev_history.json`, WHEN restored, THEN `SUIT_RESTORE_DATA_REV_UNKNOWN` fails the restore and live state is untouched.
- **AC-SE-51 (auto) GASP (SE-4, CK-1).** GIVEN reserve 0 in HYPOXIC air with pressure NORMAL or LOW, WHEN runs elapse, THEN the breath rung holds at 2 indefinitely and never reaches 3, and the seat draws from the room; GIVEN the room then reaches ANOXIC, DECOMP or VACUUM, THEN source NONE holds and FAILED follows after `HOLD_23(breath)`.
- **AC-SE-52 (auto) M15 timing (SE-5).** GIVEN a stub Graph returning the grant synchronously and another returning it at the next run's phase 0, WHEN DOOR-BREACH is run with each, THEN the breath-rung and cap sequences are identical.
- **AC-SE-63 (auto) `breath_starved` (SE-5, D.2).** GIVEN reserve 0 on source ROOM and a shortfall of 100 mGU the reserve cannot cover, WHEN the next run executes, THEN `breath_starved` is 1 and the breath demand is 2 for that run only; the next run with a full grant has the flag 0 and demand by band; one starved run does not produce rung 2 (the hold stays below `HOLD_12`).
- **AC-SE-64 (auto) Leak (SE-22, D.2).** GIVEN `integrity_bp` 5000 exactly, THEN no leak; GIVEN 4999, THEN the reserve loses `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` (150) per run in addition to any RESERVE spend; GIVEN `integrity_bp` 3500 in NORMAL air with a full reserve (source ROOM), THEN the reserve is 0 after 1,800 runs; the maximum single-run loss is 750 mGU (debit 300, spend 300, leak 150); the reserve never goes negative.
- **AC-SE-65 (auto) Thresholds at the boundary (SE-19, SE-22).** GIVEN `integrity_bp` 7000 / 6999 / 5000 / 4999 / 2500 / 2499, THEN the integrity demand is 0 / 1 / 1 / 2 / 2 / terminal (strictly less than at each threshold); GIVEN a reserve of `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` exactly, THEN the breath demand is 2, and one mGU above it is 1.

### C. Beam, cell, shape, glowsticks (SE-8 to SE-10; D.5)

- **AC-SE-10 (auto) Cell (SE-8, D.5).** GIVEN a full cell and the beam lit continuously, WHEN 72,000 ticks elapse, THEN `cell_ticks` reaches 0 at run 12,000 and `force_off(beam_id)` fires exactly once; the warn edge is crossed at 1,200 ticks remaining; a turn-ON at 0 is rejected `CHARGE_EMPTY`; a turn-OFF is always accepted.
- **AC-SE-11 (auto) `bind_beam` once (SE-9).** GIVEN a new game with N seats, WHEN the session runs for any length, THEN `bind_beam` is called exactly once per seat, in the same boot step as `create_seat_beam`, never at runtime and never by a restore; seat n holds `shape_id` n by default and the shape is immutable afterwards.
- **AC-SE-12a (auto) Stack cap (SE-10) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a stack of 4, WHEN a fifth is picked up, THEN `add_stowed` accepts 0, the item remains in the world and the reason is `STACK_FULL`; GIVEN 3 of 4 and a pickup of 2, THEN 1 is accepted.
- **AC-SE-12b (auto) I-SUPPLY boot behaviour (D.8 A11).** GIVEN four seats and `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 8, THEN boot succeeds with exactly one A11 warning naming OQ-14; GIVEN one seat and `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 3, THEN boot fails naming A11; GIVEN `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16 at four seats, THEN zero A11 warnings.
- **AC-SE-12c (auto) Start count (D.8 A8).** GIVEN `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT` 2 at one seat, THEN A8 passes; GIVEN a count above `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX`, THEN boot fails naming A8.
- **AC-SE-47a (spike) Strobe free-light fraction (SE-8, D.5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a scripted `toggle_beam` at 10 Hz, WHEN it runs with and without `BEAM_TOGGLE_MIN_INTERVAL_TICKS` 10 in the Controller adapter (PC-5), THEN the free-light fraction (lit ticks not drained divided by lit ticks) is measured for both runs and recorded.
- **AC-SE-47b (auto) Every debounced span contains a sample (SE-8, D.5).** GIVEN every phase offset 0..5 of the six-tick sample grid and every lit span of 10 to 24 ticks, WHEN the span is placed on the grid, THEN it contains at least one sample point; GIVEN a span of 4 ticks or fewer, THEN at least one offset avoids every sample (the reason the debounce exists).
- **AC-SE-70 (auto) Cell edge cases (SE-8, D.5).** GIVEN `CELL_ENABLED` 0, THEN `cell_ticks` never drops, `CHARGE_EMPTY` never fires and the charge reading is omitted; GIVEN a DOWNED seat with the beam lit, THEN the cell keeps draining 6 ticks per run and `force_off` fires at 0; GIVEN a DEAD seat, THEN the cell does not drain; a grep of the Suit source finds no call that sets `output_pm`.
- **AC-SE-90 (auto/integration) Verbs with the cell empty (SE-8, Edge Cases; Interaction's half is OQ-46) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN `cell_ticks` 0 and the beam off, WHEN the verbs are submitted, THEN `toggle_beam` ON answers `CHARGE_EMPTY`; `toggle_beam` OFF is accepted; `equip_tool {slot: 2}` is accepted; `throw_glowstick` with `stowed_count` above 0 passes Suit's validation (`OUT_OF_CHARGES` only at 0); `locomotion_cap` is unchanged and no Suit mutator reads the cell; GIVEN a lit-gated verb on an unlit target, THEN Interaction answers `NOT_LIT`, and on a target lit by a glowstick pool the same verb does not.

### D. Tools, holders, carry (SE-11 to SE-15, SE-41)

- **AC-SE-13 (auto) `equip_tool` (SE-11).** GIVEN a carrying seat, WHEN `equip_tool` is submitted, THEN `BUSY`; GIVEN a STAGGERED, DOWNED or DEAD seat, THEN `NOT_ACTIONABLE`; GIVEN slot 5 or 6 or an unheld tool, THEN `EMPTY_SLOT`; GIVEN the kernel is paused, THEN the command is rejected; GIVEN the equipped slot, THEN an accepted no-op; GIVEN `equip_tool {slot: 3}` applied at tick T, THEN `tool_in_hand` answers `tool_scanner` at tick T (sim swap duration 0 ticks; the hand animation gates no command).
- **AC-SE-14 (auto) Holder merge (SE-12).** GIVEN `part_hull_plate` (`stack_max` 1), `part_duct_coupling` (4) and `cons_glowstick` (4), WHEN `add_stowed` adds units one at a time to and past `stack_max` with equal `condition_bp` and `charge`, THEN counts reach 1 / 4 / 4 and the next add returns `accepted_count` 0 with the surplus left where it was; GIVEN two glowsticks with differing `charge` or `condition_bp`, THEN they do not merge; no limit applies other than `stack_max` per def (no pack capacity, no weight cap).
- **AC-SE-15 (auto) Carried instance (SE-13).** GIVEN a carried module and a stagger (cap FULL to WALK_ONLY), THEN `carried_id` is unchanged and the module is not released; GIVEN DOWNED (cap NONE), THEN the Controller clears `carry_ref` and on the next tick `release_carried` returns the identical `(instance_id, instance)`; GIVEN the one tick in which `carry_ref` is NONE and `carried_id` is set, WHEN an autosave is requested, THEN the hard gate refuses it (integration with Save/Load); GIVEN a restore with `carry_ref` different from `carried_id`, THEN `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH` and the live state is untouched.
- **AC-SE-16 (auto; conditional on OQ-34 Option B, deleted under Option A *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*) Suppressant (SE-14).** GIVEN a suppressant at 3 charges, WHEN `consume_charge` is called four times, THEN it clamps at 0 and stays on the rail; GIVEN charges left, THEN `replace_tool` refuses; GIVEN 0, THEN it discards the empty tool and holds the fresh one with 3.
- **AC-SE-17 (auto) Start kit (SE-15).** GIVEN a new game, WHEN `default_state()` runs, THEN the block equals `start_kit.json` (four tools, suppressant 3 of 3 (three tools under Option A of OQ-34), `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT` glowsticks, full reserve and cell, `equipped_slot` 2); a restore of the same save is byte-equal.
- **AC-SE-40 (auto) Reservation conservation (SE-41).** GIVEN `n` parts stowed, WHEN `reserve_stowed(n)` runs, THEN `stowed_count`, `stowed_mass_g` and `cue_load` fall at the begin tick whether or not the subject is damaged; `release_stowed` and `consume_reserved` conserve the total; a stack's stowed plus reserved never exceeds `stack_max`; `reserve_stowed(n)` with the stowed count below n returns false and changes nothing; the `reserved` bucket is in the snapshot, and a restore mid-job leaves `stowed_count` and `cue_load` unchanged (no oracle reappears).
- **AC-SE-67 (auto) Class B clamp table (SE-40, SE-23).** GIVEN a populated four-seat block, WHEN each Class B mutator is called at and beyond its clamp, THEN: `add_stowed` per AC-SE-12 and 14; `spend_stowed` above stock clamps to stock; `set_carried` while carrying refuses and changes nothing; `release_carried` when empty returns NONE; `credit_reserve` and `credit_cell` clamp to the deficit; `apply_suit_damage` above `integrity_bp` clamps to 0 and never rejects; `patch_integrity` per AC-SE-23; an ENDOGENOUS `source_ref` does not set `exo_pending`; an unclassed `source_ref` is rejected as a programmer error (SE-40). Each call changes only `suit.state`.
- **AC-SE-68 (auto) Read-view contract (SE-40).** GIVEN a populated block, WHEN every read view is called twice, THEN the results are equal and `state_hash` is unchanged; `tool_charge` of an unheld def is 0; `tool_in_hand` is NONE when `equipped_slot` is 0; `can_stow` is false when the add would exceed `stack_max`; `view(player_id)` contains exactly the listed field names and no believed or displayed field; the reason codes Suit returns are exactly the five closed codes.
- **AC-SE-69 (auto) `equip_tool` registration and order (SE-11, SE-32, CR-9).** GIVEN the registered descriptor, THEN origin PLAYER, `actor_binding` OWNED_BY_ISSUER, `coalesce` NONE, `max_age_ticks` 120, `allowed_when_paused` false, and it is the only command type Suit registers; GIVEN a seat both STAGGERED and carrying, THEN the reason is `NOT_ACTIONABLE`, not `BUSY`; carrying with slot 5, THEN `BUSY`; slots 1-4 equip cutter, welder, scanner, suppressant; over an enumerated set of every (loco_state, carrying, channelling, slot, held) combination, no command that passes `validate` fails `apply`.
- **AC-SE-79a (auto) Instance invariants (SE-12, SE-34).** GIVEN a restored instance with `condition_bp` 0 or above `cap_bp`, or `patched_at_step` non-zero with `cap_bp` equal to `HP_FULL`, THEN the restore fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE` and live state is untouched.
- **AC-SE-79b (auto) Passive top-up guard (D.9; dormant configuration) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a V = 8 room at 210 dkPa with `extra` 30, THEN it leaves 209 dkPa and the credit is accepted; GIVEN the same room at 195 dkPa, THEN it leaves 194 and `extra` is 0 (`TOPUP_MIN_PP` 195).
- **AC-SE-79c (auto) PASSIVE save and restore (SE-38; dormant configuration).** GIVEN a PASSIVE-mode snapshot with `pending_draw_mgu` 300 and `pending_extra_mgu` 30 awaiting their grant, WHEN restored and the next run executes, THEN the same credit lands as in an uninterrupted session; GIVEN a saved `pending_extra_mgu` above `TOPUP_PASSIVE_MGU_PER_RUN`, THEN the restore fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`.
- **AC-SE-80 (auto/integration) Tool cycle (SE-11).** GIVEN a seat holding all four tools, WHEN `tool_cycle` is pressed 5 times from slot 1, THEN the adapter issues slots 2, 3, 4, 1, 2 (never 5 or 6); a seat with no scanner skips slot 3; one `equip_tool` per press.

### E. The ladder, thermal, integrity and death (SE-16 to SE-24; D.3, D.4, D.7, D.11)

- **AC-SE-18 (auto) Cap mapping (SE-16).** GIVEN every combination of the three rungs, WHEN `locomotion_cap` is read, THEN FULL for a worst rung of 0 or 1, WALK_ONLY for 2, NONE for 3; AND the Controller's source references no Suit field other than `locomotion_cap` (lint).
- **AC-SE-19a (auto) Rise by at most one rung (SE-17, D.3; endogenous demand only) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a committed corpus `tests/unit/suit/fixtures/demand_traces.json` (generated once offline, 1,500 runs per trace, never regenerated in CI; its SHA-256 pinned in the test) plus an exhaustive enumeration of all demand sequences over demand {0,1,2} x terminal {0,1} up to length 6, WHEN each is stepped through `vitals_ladder_step`, THEN on the breath and integrity axes the rung rises by at most 1 per run.
- **AC-SE-19b (auto) Instant descent (SE-17, D.3).** GIVEN the same corpus, THEN a descent to any lower rung happens on the run the demand falls.
- **AC-SE-19c (auto) Floor (SE-17, D.3).** GIVEN the same corpus, THEN rung 3 occurs at least `HOLD_12 + HOLD_23(axis)` runs after the run the rung first became 1.
- **AC-SE-19d (auto) Edge spacing, counter axes only (SE-17, D.3).** GIVEN the same corpus, THEN two consecutive FULL-to-WALK_ONLY edges on one counter axis (breath, integrity) are at least `HOLD_12` runs apart.
- **AC-SE-19e (auto) Transient caps (SE-17, D.3).** GIVEN the same corpus, THEN a qualifying demand lasting `HOLD_12 - 1` runs or fewer never produces rung 2, and a terminal episode of `HOLD_23(axis) - 1` runs or fewer never produces rung 3. The integrity axis under `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 is excluded from 19c-19e (AC-SE-24).
- **AC-SE-19f (auto) Warmth doorway (D.4 refractory) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a seat alternating 8 runs in a lethal-cold room (gain 40) with 25 runs in a COMFORT room (relax 12), a 33-run period, for 600 runs, starting at `thermal_load` −3700, THEN the warmth rung climbs from 1 to 2 at most once per `HOLD_12` + 1 runs (at most 6 FULL-to-WALK_ONLY edges in the 600 runs, against about 18 without the refractory); AND GIVEN a first entry from rung 0 or 1 with no preceding fall from rung 2, THEN the refractory does not delay the climb.
- **AC-SE-20a (auto) Floor on every fixture (SE-18, D.11; endogenous fixtures and Suit-owned damage paths only) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN DOOR-BREACH (reserve full, reserve 0), O2-ROOM (unsuited, suited), O2-SHIP, FREEZE-BREACH, SHIP-COLD and a synthetic SCORCH fixture (constant `band_t` SCORCH with `T` at or above `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC + TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC`), WHEN each is run, THEN `ticks_first_cue_to_FAILED >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE` (heat side measured from the CRITICAL entry, A13).
- **AC-SE-20b (auto) Exact values (D.3).** GIVEN the integer-exact fixtures, THEN DOOR-BREACH with a full reserve is exactly 1,088 runs and with reserve 0 exactly 288 runs; the suited O2-ROOM (about 340 s) and FREEZE-BREACH (about 10.5 min) values are reported, not asserted.
- **AC-SE-20c (auto) Window and vacuous pass (D.11).** GIVEN each fixture run until FAILED or 60 minutes of sim time, THEN a fixture that never reaches FAILED in that window passes vacuously and reports its first-gain run (SHIP-COLD is expected to; AC-SE-82 asserts it).
- **AC-SE-21 (auto) Breath rung table (SE-19, D.3).** GIVEN the locked DOOR-BREACH and O2-ROOM harness traces and a suited seat, WHEN `vitals_ladder_step` is stepped run by run, THEN (defaults in brackets): Example A (DOOR-BREACH, reserve 270,000): rung 1 at the first THIN run (25); source RESERVE at `s_h` (64); reserve at or below `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` 90,000 first at `s_h + 599` (663); rung 2 and WALK_ONLY at `s_h + 698` (762); source NONE at `s_h + 900` (964; reserve 0, ANOXIC); rung 3 and NONE at the NONE run + 149 (1113). Example B (reserve 0): rung 1 at 25; GASP from `s_h`; rung 2 at `s_h + 99` (163); source NONE from the first ANOXIC run (116); rung 3 at the rung-2 run + 150 (313). Example C (O2-ROOM, suited; relations only): the first RESERVE run is the first HYPOXIC run; rung 2 occurs 99 runs after the first run at or below `RESERVE_WARN_MGU`; GASP begins on the run the reserve reaches 0 while HYPOXIC; the rung stays 2 and never reaches 3 while GASP holds; rung 3 occurs 149 runs after the first NONE run. Example D (2 s DECOMP at reserve 0): rung 1, the hold reaches 20 and resets, the rung never exceeds 1, the cap stays FULL.
- **AC-SE-22a (auto) Frost soak (SE-20, D.4).** GIVEN a twenty-minute soak (12,000 runs) in a FROST room from `thermal_load` 0, THEN the load never gains, the warmth rung stays 1 and the cap stays FULL.
- **AC-SE-22b (auto) Gain bounds (D.4).** GIVEN `e` at or above `TL_DEPTH_CAP_MC` every run, THEN `abs(thermal_load)` reaches `TL_CRITICAL` (4000) on run 100 and `TL_MAX` (12000) on run 300, never earlier.
- **AC-SE-22c (auto) Insulation edge (SE-21, D.4).** GIVEN an intact suit, `INSUL_MC` 5000 and a FREEZING room with `T` exactly `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC - 5000`, THEN gain 0, relax 0, load unchanged; with `T` one mC lower, gain exactly `TL_GAIN_MIN` (8); GIVEN `integrity_bp` anywhere in `[SUIT_LEAK_HP, SUIT_HP_OK)` (6000) or at `SUIT_LEAK_HP` itself, THEN `insul_eff_mc` equals `INSUL_MC` and the shifted onset is identical to the intact suit's (integrity STRAINED has no thermal effect); GIVEN `integrity_bp` 2500, THEN `insul_eff_mc` is 2500; the test reads `SUIT_LEAK_HP` by name.
- **AC-SE-22d (auto) FREEZING above the shifted onset relaxes (SE-20, D.4) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN FREEZING above the shifted onset (e = 0) and `thermal_load` −1200, THEN gain is 0, relax is `TL_RELAX_EDGE` (3) per run, the load is 0 after exactly 400 runs and the warmth rung stays 1 (band-driven) throughout; GIVEN `thermal_load` −3900 (rung 2) entering that strip, THEN the load falls below 3,700 (`TL_CRITICAL - TL_HYST`) on run 67, the rung drops to 1 on that run and the load is 0 after 1,300 runs; GIVEN a fresh start (`thermal_load` 0) in that strip, THEN the rung never exceeds 1 (the invariant: from a fresh start FROST, HOT below `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` and FREEZING above the shifted onset cannot reach CRITICAL).
- **AC-SE-22e (auto) Recovery (D.4).** GIVEN `thermal_load` -4000 in COMFORT, THEN relax is 12 per run, the rung leaves CRITICAL when the load first passes below 3,700 (`TL_CRITICAL - TL_HYST`), and the load is 0 after exactly 334 runs.
- **AC-SE-23 (auto) Integrity (SE-22, D.7).** GIVEN a SCORCH burn of 4 bp per run from 10000, THEN `integrity_bp` falls below `SUIT_HP_OK` at run 751 (rung 1), below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` at run 1251 (leak starts), rung 2 at run 1350, below `SUIT_HP_FAIL` at run 1876, rung 3 at run 1975; GIVEN an arc of 20 bp per run from 5000, THEN `integrity_bp` is below `SUIT_HP_FAIL` at run 125 and rung 2 is reached while it is still at or above `SUIT_HP_FAIL` (assertion A7); GIVEN `patch_integrity(bp)`, THEN the result is `max(current, min(current + bp, SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP))` (2500 + 5000 gives 6000; 6500 + 1000 gives 6500; 10000 + 1000 gives 10000), a patch never lowers `integrity_bp`, and a patched suit (at most 6000) never returns to rung 0; GIVEN `integrity_bp` in `[SUIT_LEAK_HP, SUIT_HP_OK)`, THEN the cap is FULL, no leak debit occurs, `insul_eff_mc` equals `INSUL_MC` and every body-cue level is unchanged (the inert STRAINED rung, OQ-19 ruled).
- **AC-SE-24 (auto) Exogenous bypass (SE-23, D.3; knob 1 is the default; driven by a stub, Interaction is designed).** GIVEN `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 and a **stub** applying 500 bp per step (not Interaction's code), WHEN each apply lands, THEN on the next run the integrity rung equals the threshold band of `integrity_bp`: rung 1 at 6500 (step 7), 2 at 4500 (step 11), 3 at 2000 (step 16); boundaries: 7000 after step 6 is still rung 0, 5000 after step 10 is still rung 1, 2500 after step 15 is still rung 2; GIVEN the knob 0, THEN a `HARM_TOTAL_BP` sequence reaches FAILED no sooner than 200 runs and the flag still clears on the next run with no effect; AND `exo_pending` survives a snapshot and restore and is consumed once; an ENDOGENOUS `source_ref` does not set the flag.
- **AC-SE-25 (auto) Death and recovery (SE-24).** GIVEN a DOWNED seat whose cap rises on the `death_tick` run, WHEN the Controller checks, THEN the cap is honoured and the seat is not DEAD; GIVEN a DEAD seat, THEN further runs leave the block unchanged; GIVEN DOWNED, THEN the seat keeps draining.
- **AC-SE-41 (review) Exogenous callers state their tell (SE-18, SE-23).** GIVEN the Interaction GDD (IA-31) and the Horror Entities GDD, WHEN read, THEN each states its own 10-30 s tell for every harm path (the Graph's exported requirement, LS-13) and cites `apply_suit_damage` with a source class. Status: the source-class half is answered — Horror HE-15 calls the 3-argument `apply_suit_damage(player_id, STRIKE_BP, source_ref)` with `source_ref` classed EXOGENOUS once at record creation and persisted across restore, and Interaction IA-31 classes `begin_harm` EXOGENOUS; the tell half is checked at review.
- **AC-SE-43a (auto) Golden run numbers (D.4) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the FREEZE-BREACH room (asymptote 10,000 mC below the shifted onset, tau 4615 runs), WHEN integrated with integer arithmetic, THEN CRITICAL and FAILED occur on exactly the run numbers in the committed golden file `tests/unit/suit/fixtures/freeze_breach.golden` (SHA-256 pinned) produced by the reference integer integrator (456 and 1,210 runs after the shifted onset are orientation figures, not assertions).
- **AC-SE-43b (auto) CRITICAL to FAILED (D.4).** GIVEN the same run, THEN CRITICAL to FAILED is never less than 200 runs.
- **AC-SE-43c (auto) Insulation numbers (D.4, SE-21).** GIVEN a room 3000 mC past `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`, THEN it is harmless at full integrity (`e_cold` 0), gains exactly 8 per run at `integrity_bp` 2500 (`insul_eff_mc` 2500, `e_cold` 500) and 0 at `integrity_bp` 3500 (`insul_eff_mc` 3500, `e_cold` 0).
- **AC-SE-43d (auto) Bounds and descent (D.4).** GIVEN any trace, THEN `thermal_load` never leaves -12000..12000, and a FAILED seat descends only below `TL_MAX - TL_HYST`.
- **AC-SE-43e (auto) SCORCH (D.4).** GIVEN a SCORCH room, THEN CRITICAL is reached at 10 s (run 100) and FAILED at 30 s (run 300).
- **AC-SE-50 (auto) Band flap (SE-19).** GIVEN `band_o2` flapping across the THIN and NORMAL hysteresis edge, WHEN runs elapse, THEN the demand follows the enum, the rung follows within one run, and no stagger edge is produced.
- **AC-SE-62 (auto) Run order, diagnostics and leak timing (SE-39).** GIVEN a stub landing in one tick, in command order, an `apply_suit_damage` of 3000 bp and a `patch_integrity` of 1000 bp on seat 1, WHEN `suit.vitals` runs, THEN it reads the final `integrity_bp` (no intermediate state); GIVEN a SCORCH burn that commits `integrity_bp` below `SUIT_LEAK_HP` in run n, THEN the seam-jet level is on at run n and the first leak debit is in run n+1; GIVEN a hit landed in P2 before run n, THEN jet and first debit are both in run n; GIVEN an unbound or DEAD seat, THEN no field changes; seats are processed in ascending PlayerId (observed by the order of `draw_air` calls); each of `suit_reserve_shortfall`, `suit_rung_limited{axis}`, `suit_cue_state{player}` and `suit_exo_bypass` fires on its stated event, and removing the DIAGNOSTIC subscriber leaves `state_hash` unchanged.
- **AC-SE-66 (auto) Arc (D.7).** GIVEN a mode-2 SHORT descriptor at its top severity in the seat's compartment, WHEN the seat is exactly `ARC_RADIUS_PX` from the anchor, THEN `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN` (20) applies per run; one pixel farther, 0; a lower severity, 0; a different compartment, 0; a mode other than SHORT, 0.
- **AC-SE-73 (auto) Warmth, heat and hysteresis (SE-20, D.4).** GIVEN HOT with `T` 5000 mC past `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, THEN gain 16, CRITICAL at run 250, FAILED 500 runs later; GIVEN a HOT room below `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC`, THEN relax is `TL_RELAX_EDGE` (3) and the rung is 1; SCORCH never relaxes and reaches CRITICAL at run 100 and FAILED at run 300; COOL relaxes 6 per run; the rung descends from 3 only below `TL_MAX - TL_HYST` and from 2 only below `TL_CRITICAL - TL_HYST`; the rung ascends at most one step per run.
- **AC-SE-74 (auto) Two axes and Suit damage paths (SE-16, SE-18).** GIVEN SCORCH (warmth and burn together), THEN the seat reaches FAILED on warmth first and `vitals_state` is the maximum of the rungs; GIVEN the arc alone from 7000 and the burn alone from 10000, THEN first cue to FAILED is at least `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE`, AND the arc starting from `SUIT_LEAK_HP` reaches FAILED at least `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE` ticks after the first seam-jet run (A3 with A7; 225 runs at the defaults).
- **AC-SE-75 (lint/review) Authored vacuum traverse (D.11, SE-6).** GIVEN every authored path crossing an unbreathable compartment, WHEN `tiles x TILE_PX / (V x TICK_RATE)` is computed at walking speed (`V_CARRY` where a module is carried), THEN it is at most `RESERVE_S / 2` (112 tiles walking, 56 carrying at the defaults), and `RESERVE_WARN_S <= RESERVE_S / 2` (A5). The gate sits with Derelict Authoring; Suit supplies the formula. Interim: NOT ASSESSED.
- **AC-SE-76 (auto) Door-cycling exploit (SE-17, RK-3).** GIVEN a seat at reserve 0 alternating 5 s DECOMP and 5 s breathable for 600 s, WHEN runs elapse, THEN the breath rung falls back to 0 or 1 on every breathable interval and never reaches 3 (the hold resets in full; there is no decay branch).
- **AC-SE-81 (auto) `revive` dormant (SE-24).** GIVEN `revive(player_id)` at MVP, WHEN called on a DOWNED seat, THEN no field changes and `integrity_bp` does not rise, until Vertical Slice B enables it.
- **AC-SE-82 (auto) SHIP-COLD and FREEZING hold (SE-21).** GIVEN the Graph's SHIP-COLD fixture with `INSUL_MC` 5000 and an intact suit, WHEN 18,000 runs (30 minutes) elapse, THEN `thermal_load` never gains and the warmth rung stays at or below 1 (the first gain run is reported; the GDD expects about 40 minutes); GIVEN `INSUL_MC` 0, THEN gain starts at the unshifted `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`.

### F. Cues, readings and the consequence channel (SE-25 to SE-30; D.6, D.10)

- **AC-SE-26 (lint/auto) Cue purity (SE-25).** GIVEN Suit's hook, mutators and read views, WHEN scanned, THEN none imports or reads a Sanity, Attention or `PERCEPTION_OUT` symbol and the block has no believed or displayed field; GIVEN a replay with a mutated sanity block, THEN every cue output is identical.
- **AC-SE-27 (review) Classification (SE-26).** GIVEN Visual/Audio Requirements and UI Requirements, WHEN every player-facing value is listed, THEN each is tagged with exactly one of the five SE-26 classes (closed-list cue, breath-family cue, on-sprite anchor, reading, physical result) matching its SE-26 row; the four amendments to the Graph's list (rime, stagger / walk-only / DOWNED, seam jet, HEAVY back-frame) appear in Cross-References as landed. The Graph's consequence-channel list carries the four amendments (2026-10-27; OQ-9 answered), so this criterion no longer blocks on the Graph; it may not be marked passed on the strength of this GDD's own classification.
- **AC-SE-28a (manual) No screen effect, atmosphere states (SE-27; Visual/Feel) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the debug console forcing `band_o2`, `band_p` and rung on a seat, WHEN each of THIN, HYPOXIC, ANOXIC, LOW and DECOMP is captured at every integer zoom `k` in [1, k_auto + 2], THEN no vignette, tunnel, blur or false state appears; files `AC-SE-28a_<state>_k<k>.png` in `production/qa/evidence/`, with a sign-off line from the art-director or creative-director appended to the evidence index.
- **AC-SE-28b (manual) No screen effect, thermal states (SE-27).** GIVEN the same console, WHEN each of FROST, FREEZING, HOT and SCORCH is captured at every integer zoom, THEN no frost overlay or edge tint appears, and HOT and SCORCH show no body cue of any kind; files `AC-SE-28b_<state>_k<k>.png`, with a sign-off line.
- **AC-SE-28c (manual) No screen effect, rung states (SE-27).** GIVEN the same console, WHEN STRAINED, CRITICAL and FAILED are captured at every integer zoom, THEN no screen-space effect appears; files `AC-SE-28c_<rung>_k<k>.png`, with a sign-off line.
- **AC-SE-29 (auto) Mass cue (SE-28, D.6).** GIVEN the start kit, THEN `cue_load` 0; GIVEN `part_duct_coupling` x4 and `part_cable_splice` x4 stowed (5,600 g, at or above `WEIGHT_MEDIUM_G`), 1; GIVEN additionally `part_hull_plate` stowed (at or above `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G`), 2; for carried in {none, MEDIUM `mod_door_motor`, HEAVY `mod_scrubber`} and stowed in {LIGHT, MEDIUM, HEAVY} the nine outputs are 0,1,2 / 1,1,2 / 2,2,2; `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` HEAVY makes stowed MEDIUM contribute 0; in-hand tools and reserved parts never count (`reserve_stowed` lowers `cue_load` on the same run). The test reads the class boundary from the registry and hard-codes no gram figure.
- **AC-SE-42a (spike) Blind audio protocol (SE-28; Vertical Slice A precondition) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN two clips, load breath (deep, creak, at the air class's own rate) and thin-air breath (shallow, fast, no creak), WHEN at least 30 naive listeners (never exposed to the design) each classify each clip blind, in random order, with the clips counterbalanced, THEN the raw classifications are retained as a CSV in `production/qa/evidence/`.
- **AC-SE-42b (spike) Blind audio threshold (SE-28).** GIVEN those results, WHEN each clip's identification rate is computed, THEN the observed rate is at least 90 % **and** the one-sided 95 % Wilson lower bound is at least 75 % (at N = 30 that needs at least 28 of 30; the earlier 18 of 20 had a lower bound near 70 % and could not tell 90 % from 75 %); failure blocks the Vertical Slice A phantom-salvage check, not the MVP.
- **AC-SE-30 (auto) Reading wrap (SE-29).** GIVEN a stub Filter that wraps `true_value` for the five keys, WHEN it returns false values, THEN the HUD value changes and no body cue, `locomotion_cap` or overlay visibility changes; GIVEN the MVP default, THEN every key returns the true value.
- **AC-SE-45a (auto) Cue levels (D.10) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a table of `(band_t, rung, thermal_load)` inputs, WHEN `suit_cue_levels` runs, THEN `cue_cold` is 3 / 2 / 1 / 0 as defined, `cue_fog` is `min(rung_breath, 2)`, `charge_band` is 0 / 1 / 2, `carried_class` is 0 with nothing carried and 1 + the weight class otherwise (0..3), `cue_load` per D.6, and the marks are set at CRITICAL (and the cell warn edge) but never at STRAINED; the heat side yields no body cue.
- **AC-SE-45b (auto) Cold side is gated on the band (D.10).** GIVEN `thermal_load` −2000 (rung 1), WHEN `band_t` is HOT or SCORCH, THEN `cold_side` is false and `cue_cold` is 0 (no rime, no shiver); WHEN `band_t` is COOL, THEN `cold_side` is true and `cue_cold` is 2; GIVEN a load that crosses 0 to positive in a HOT room, THEN `cold_side` stays false; the rung and the cap are unchanged by the gating.
- **AC-SE-45c (auto) Breath cue tuple and edge counter (D.10).** GIVEN `breath_src` ROOM, RESERVE, GASP and NONE, THEN `cue_breath` is `{src, air_class, pace_step}` with `air_class` 0 / 1 / 2 from D.1 and `pace_step` `min(rung_breath, 2)`; GIVEN a source change ROOM to RESERVE, THEN `breath_src_change_seq` increases by exactly 1 on that run and by 0 on every other run; GIVEN a snapshot and restore, THEN the counter is unchanged and no edge is derived.
- **AC-SE-45d (auto) Charge advisory (D.5, D.10).** GIVEN `cell_ticks` at `CELL_ADVISE_S` x `TICK_RATE` (7,200), THEN `charge_advise` is true and `charge_band` is OK; at `CELL_WARN_S` x `TICK_RATE` + 1 (1,201) true; at 1,200, `charge_advise` is false and `charge_band` is WARN; the advisory raises no mark, no audio cue and no `SHARED_SUMMARY` field.
- **AC-SE-31 (manual) Reading and hand-rail screens (SE-30; UI).** GIVEN the build, WHEN the critical strip, the suit panel and the hand rail are captured in OK, STRAINED and CRITICAL, THEN: the O2 numeral equals the seat's compartment `p_o2` in dkPa (a THIN room set to a known value); the mark appears on the strip's breath dial when the breath rung is 2 whatever `breath_src` is (the dial shows the suit O2 while the source is RESERVE and the ambient O2 otherwise), beside the warmth glyph at warmth rung 2, beside the integrity glyph at integrity rung 2, beside the charge glyph at the cell warn edge, and nowhere at rung 1; a suppressant at 0 is greyed, not hidden; with `CELL_ENABLED` 0 the charge reading is absent; no weight readout, inventory or per-part count exists; the HUD is unchanged in size between two world zoom levels; retained screenshots (UI, BLOCKING).
- **AC-SE-53a to 53f (manual) Body-cue evidence (Visual/Feel, lead sign-off) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** *Shared procedure.* GIVEN the build and the debug console, WHEN the cue is captured on each of the five facings at each integer zoom, shown against a paired capture of the same facing with the state forced off, THEN the cue is present only in its true state; one contact sheet per cue (facings in a grid) in `production/qa/evidence/`, and a sign-off line appended to the evidence index. The six criteria differ only in the cue: **53a** rime (two levels), **53b** visor fog (two levels), **53c** shiver (idle only, absent while moving), **53d** the seam jet, **53e** the HEAVY back-frame, **53f** the stagger frame.
- **AC-SE-77 (lint) No reading bypass (SE-29).** GIVEN the HUD and every consumer outside Suit, WHEN scanned, THEN the five readings are read only through `true_value` and never from the `view()` reading fields.
- **AC-SE-78 (auto) Breath cue separability (SE-28, D.10).** GIVEN HEAVY `cue_load` in THIN air, THEN `breath_step` equals the value for the same air with `cue_load` 0 and the creak layer is on; GIVEN `cue_load` HEAVY versus 0 with an identical Controller input, THEN walking speed (`V_WALK`) and the noise class emitted are unchanged.
- **AC-SE-91 (review) Accessibility witness coverage (SE-26, D.10) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the D.10 normative cue table and `design/accessibility-requirements.md`, WHEN read, THEN every body-cue row has a non-audio witness or appears on the user-accepted exception list (E1 stowed MEDIUM load, E2 heat STRAINED to CRITICAL, OQ-40), every caption id in the table exists in the captions policy (AA-41), and any tactile channel is driven only by the table's rows. **Status: NOT ASSESSED** while the accessibility document does not exist and the user has not accepted the exception list (OQ-40); never PASS.

### G. Seams, save, determinism (SE-31 to SE-39)

- **AC-SE-32 (lint) No stimulus push (SE-31).** GIVEN Suit's source, WHEN scanned, THEN no call reaches an Attention intake.
- **AC-SE-33 (auto) Pause (SE-32).** GIVEN a paused kernel, WHEN `equip_tool` or any Suit mutator is submitted, THEN it is rejected and no counter changes.
- **AC-SE-34a (review) Autosave soft gate (SE-33) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the Save/Load GDD, WHEN read, THEN it either adopts the soft gate (a bound seat's `vitals_state` of CRITICAL or worse defers an autosave, capped at `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS`, then RISKY, no glyph) or records the doomed-autosave loop as accepted by the user. **Status: NOT ASSESSED** pending the user's ruling (Suit OQ-13, Save/Load OQ-40); never PASS and not a Suit defect; it blocks the Save/Load stories `autosave_gate` and `generation_select`, not the Suit story.
- **AC-SE-34b (auto) Suit's half of the gate (SE-33).** GIVEN a seat whose worst rung is 2 or 3, WHEN `view(player_id).vitals_state` is read after the run's commit, THEN it is 2 or 3; GIVEN a worst rung of 0 or 1, THEN 0 or 1; the value is derived on read, never saved, and equals the maximum of the three persisted rungs.
- **AC-SE-35 (auto) SL-15 round trip (SE-34, SE-38).** GIVEN a populated four-seat block, WHEN it is snapshotted, restored and snapshotted again, THEN the two snapshots are byte-equal (restore idempotence) including `rung_*`, `hold_*`, `exo_pending` and `reserved`; `post_restore` rebuilds every `prev_*` so no spurious valve click or stagger fires; an unsupported version, an out-of-range field, a stowed count over `stack_max`, an unknown `def_id`, a bad `beam_shape` and an unbound seat each fail with their named `SUIT_RESTORE_*` reason and never clamp.
- **AC-SE-36a (auto) Pod hold (SE-35, SE-3) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a pod hop with `loco_state` IN_POD (the pod's section dormant, WM-17), WHEN a full leg runs, THEN the reserve and the cell are unchanged across it, no `draw_air` is issued, `breath_starved` stays 0, no GASP cue fires, no thermal load, hold or `p_age` advances, and all resume after; AND on the first run after the leg `cid` differs from the NONE `prev_cid`, the window re-anchors and no `stagger` is submitted.
- **AC-SE-36b (auto) Exposed (SE-3, SE-35).** GIVEN a seat in a NORMAL room and `room_at` answering NONE with `loco_state` not IN_POD (fault-injected, BDY-29), WHEN the next runs execute, THEN the seat is EXPOSED to the EXTERIOR (index 3 by rule), the switch is a compartment change, zero `stagger` submissions are made on that run and the following runs, and everything keeps running (AC-SE-5).
- **AC-SE-36c (auto) Pull (SE-35).** GIVEN a DECOMP entry, WHEN the run executes, THEN the DECOMP cue level is set and Suit makes no displacement call of any kind (the seat's position delta caused by Suit is 0).
- **AC-SE-36d (auto) Window boundary (D.12).** GIVEN a synthetic trace in one compartment with index 0 on run `m` and index 2 on run `m` + 9, `m` + 10 and `m` + 11 (three separate traces), WHEN each is stepped at `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` 60, THEN the `stagger` submission counts are 1, 1 and 0; AND the same pattern holds for index 1 then 3, and at `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` 30 and 120 with the boundary at `m` + 5 and `m` + 20.
- **AC-SE-36e (auto) Step sizes and continuing falls (D.12).** GIVEN one-band steps 0-1, 1-2 and 2-3, THEN zero submissions each; GIVEN 0-2, 1-3 and 0-3 inside the window, THEN exactly one each; GIVEN 0-2 then 3 inside the window, THEN one in total; GIVEN 0-2, a recovery to 1, then 3 inside the window, THEN two; AND on the locked DOOR-BREACH harness the trigger submits zero (Example 1), the breach's stagger arriving only through the ladder's cap edge.
- **AC-SE-36f (auto) Crossing and restore (D.12, SE-38).** GIVEN a seat that crosses a door into a room already two bands lower, THEN zero submissions; GIVEN a snapshot taken at `p_age0` 3 with index 0 sampled and restored, WHEN the trace resumes with index 2 five runs later, THEN the `stagger` is submitted on the same run as in an uninterrupted session and not on the restore run itself; GIVEN a saved `p_age0` of `STAGGER_WINDOW_RUNS` + 2, THEN the restore fails `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE` and live state is untouched.
- **AC-SE-37 (auto) `SHARED_SUMMARY` (SE-36).** GIVEN the replication hint, WHEN the summary is built, THEN it carries exactly `{locomotion_cap, vitals_state, cue_cold, cue_fog, cue_load, beam_shape, charge_band, carried_class}` and no reserve value, tool charge or integrity number.
- **AC-SE-38a (auto) Determinism (SE-37) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN two runs of the same seed and command trace, WHEN `state_hash` is compared, THEN they are equal.
- **AC-SE-38b (lint) No nondeterminism or scene tree (SE-37).** GIVEN Suit's hook, mutators and block, WHEN scanned, THEN they contain no RNG, wall clock, `delta` or `Node`.
- **AC-SE-38c (lint) Integer width (SE-37).** GIVEN every block field and every intermediate named in D.1 to D.12, THEN each fits in int32.
- **AC-SE-39 (auto/lint) Guard-rail test (SE-40).** GIVEN every published mutator, WHEN each is called, THEN only `suit.state` changes; AND the only command type Suit registers is `equip_tool`; the retired names (`consume_glowstick`, `stow`, `unstow`, `has_glowstick`, `equipped_tool`, `apply_harm`) appear nowhere.
- **AC-SE-44a (auto) Build assertions (D.8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a data set violating each of A1 to A13 in turn, WHEN the game boots, THEN boot fails naming the assertion; A11 at two or more seats is a warning naming OQ-14 and does not fail boot (AC-SE-12).
- **AC-SE-44b (auto) Knob-corner sweep (D.8 feasible region) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN every corner of the coupled knobs (`SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S`, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS`, `TL_CRITICAL`, `TL_MAX`, `TL_GAIN_MAX`, `RESERVE_S`, `RESERVE_WARN_S`, `R_BREATH`, `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN`, `HYPOX_FAIL_S`, `INTEG_FAIL_S`, `SUIT_HP_FAIL`, `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, `ARC_DMG_BP_PER_RUN`) at its registered range limit, WHEN the build boots and, where it boots, the AC-SE-20 death-fairness sweep runs, THEN either boot fails naming at least one of A1 to A13, or `ticks_first_cue_to_FAILED >= SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE` holds on every lethal fixture (heat side measured from the CRITICAL entry, A13); no corner may boot and break the floor.
- **AC-SE-46 (auto) Credit conversion (D.9).** GIVEN a cell deficit of 36,000 ticks, WHEN a BATTERY top-up grants the full request, THEN 36,000 ticks are credited exactly at the default conversion; a partial grant credits the floor of the granted units and never exceeds the deficit.
- **AC-SE-48a (auto) Size (SE-38) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN a maximally populated four-seat block (every stack at `stack_max`, a carried module, all rungs 3, all holds at maximum), WHEN serialised by Save/Load's serialiser, THEN the size is under 1,536 bytes (raised from 1,024 by the 2026-10-27 additions; measured, 330 bytes per seat is an estimate).
- **AC-SE-48c (auto) One dirty key per seat per burst (SE-38, SE-39).** GIVEN a change burst on one seat, THEN at most one `S/<PlayerId>` key is marked for that seat and the dirty-key count never approaches `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256.
- **AC-SE-48d (auto) Restore notification (SE-38).** GIVEN a restore, THEN exactly one `ALL` notification is emitted, then silence.
- **AC-SE-71 (auto) Derived values not saved (SE-16, SE-34, D.8).** GIVEN a populated block, WHEN snapshotted, THEN the key set contains none of `locomotion_cap`, `vitals_state`, `cue_cold`, `cue_fog`, `cue_load`, `charge_band`, `carried_class`, `held_mass_g`, `stowed_mass_g`, the derived breath source, `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` or any `prev_*`; and at default knobs `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` 270,000, `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` 90,000, `CELL_MAX_TICKS` 72,000, `HOLD_12` 100, `HOLD_23` breath 150, integrity 100, warmth 200.
- **AC-SE-72 (auto) Dirty-key schedule (SE-38).** GIVEN a reserve falling 300 mGU per run, THEN `S/<PlayerId>` is marked on each 5 % bucket crossing (13,500 mGU), on the RESERVE engage edge and on the warn edge, and not otherwise; likewise the cell (5 % bucket, warn edge, empty edge) and integrity (250 bp bucket); `thermal_load` marks only on a rung or cue-level change; `hold_*`, `pending_draw_mgu` and `exo_pending` never mark.
- **AC-SE-83a (lint) Non-negative dividends (SE-37, Formulas preamble) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN the Suit hook, WHEN every division in D.1 to D.12 is scanned, THEN each has a non-negative dividend (an `abs` or clamp precedes it).
- **AC-SE-83b (auto) No overflow at the knob extremes (SE-37).** GIVEN every knob at the extreme of its registered range, WHEN the formulas are evaluated, THEN no intermediate exceeds 2^31 - 1 (the largest is `cell_deficit_ticks x CELL_STOCK_PER_KTICK`: 72,000 x 8000 = 5.76e8 at `CELL_S` 1200, and 144,000 x 8000 = 1.152e9 at `CELL_S` 2400).

### H. Cross-system and integration

- **AC-SE-54 (auto/integration) Emitter State.** GIVEN the cell reaches 0, WHEN the tick completes, THEN `force_off` lands before `emitter.place`, `lit` is false at the next placement, and a channel lit only by the beam aborts within 5 ticks; `toggle_beam.validate` reads Suit's `charge`.
- **AC-SE-55 (auto/integration) Controller.** GIVEN a falling cap edge, WHEN the Controller steps, THEN one stagger of `STAGGER_TICKS` plays and the channel is cleared; NONE downs the seat and drops the carry; the hook order of AC-SE-2 holds and a cap raised on `death_tick` is honoured.
- **AC-SE-56 (auto/integration) Life-Support Graph.** GIVEN a bound seat in a room, WHEN runs elapse, THEN `draw_air` is applied in ascending `(compartment id, crew id)`, the Suit's bands equal the Graph's `compartment(cid)` enums, and `withdraw_store` is never called by `suit.vitals` itself (lint), only inside the V7 apply; a Graph fixture under hallucination leaves Suit's cue outputs unchanged.
- **AC-SE-57 (auto/integration) Save/Load.** GIVEN every bound seat DEAD, WHEN the death stub restores the newest verified generation, THEN `suit.state` rolls back with the world, no PROFILE block exists, and no beat, lights-out or death screen is shown.
- **AC-SE-58 (auto/integration) Interaction (Interaction designed 2026-10-26; driven by stubs until its story lands).** GIVEN Interaction's verbs, WHEN `pickup`, `throw_glowstick`, `begin_harm`, `begin_patch` (self) and `top_up` run, THEN they use only the names of SE-40 and SE-41, a full stack answers `STACK_FULL`, `OUT_OF_CHARGES` reads `stowed_count` or `tool_charge`, and harm lands per step through `apply_suit_damage`.
- **AC-SE-59 (review) Bidirectional dependencies and counterpart text.** GIVEN the dependents' GDDs as they land (Interaction, Layout, Ship Body, Attention, Sanity, Boarding & Salvage, HUD & Suit Layer, Perception Filter, Adaptive Audio, Horror, Web Map, Co-op), WHEN read, THEN each repeats its "must repeat" clause from the Interactions section; the Emitter State's text repeats EM-10 and the once-at-new-game `bind_beam` caller. Interim per GDD: NOT ASSESSED.
- **AC-SE-60 (auto) Four independent seats.** GIVEN four seats in four rooms (fixture `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 16), WHEN runs elapse, THEN each seat's reserve, cell, rungs and cues depend only on its own room and state; swapping two seats' PlayerIds swaps their records and nothing else.
- **AC-SE-61 (review) Registry.** GIVEN `design/registry/entities.yaml` after Phase 5, WHEN grepped, THEN (i) zero `# pending referenced_by` lines still name `design/gdd/suit-and-equipment.md`; (ii) every Tuning Knobs constant marked with the star and each of D.1 to D.7 exist as registry entries with `suit-and-equipment.md` in `referenced_by` or `source`; (iii) a confirming `/consistency-check` run exits clean and prints its patch counts.
- **AC-SE-92 (manual/spike) Naive-player onboarding playtest (Game Feel, OQ-42) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.** GIVEN at least 8 players who have not seen the design, WHEN each plays the authored first sortie with no tutorial text and meets a first reserve engagement, a first THIN room and a first FROST room, THEN for each of those three first exposures at least 7 of 8 players retreat, top up or leave the exposure within `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` (20 s) of the first cue (measured from the session recording), and at most 1 of 8 dies in the first sortie; results are retained as `production/qa/evidence/AC-SE-92_<date>.csv`. A spike: it never blocks Done, and a failure returns to Derelict Authoring and OQ-42.

### I. Performance (ADVISORY)

- **AC-SE-48b (perf) Cost.** GIVEN a Release export on the named reference machine and the tick on which the Graph's three hooks, Attention and Suit all run at (6, 0), WHEN 10,000 such ticks are timed, THEN total P3 time is under `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 at p99 and Suit's own share is recorded; 0.05 ms per run is a proposed target for the lead-programmer and technical-director to ratify once the reference machine exists.

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **AC-SE-34a is NOT ASSESSED, not failed** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Save/Load's OQ-40 now carries the soft-gate input as a recommended, unadopted package and a USER DECISION; until the user rules, the criterion cannot pass. Carried as Suit OQ-13; BLOCKING on the Save/Load stories, not the Suit story (see the NOT ASSESSED rule in the Acceptance Criteria preamble).
- **AC-SE-41, 59 and 75 cannot be evaluated** until the counterpart GDDs (Horror Entities, the dependents, Derelict Authoring) exist or land in final form; interim result NOT ASSESSED, never PASS.
- **AC-SE-24, 58 and the Interaction rows of AC-SE-15 and 40** depend on Interaction's designed values (`HARM_BP_PER_STEP` 500, the reservation API, the names of CK-14); they are driven by stubs until Interaction's story lands.
- **AC-SE-48b** is ADVISORY: 0.05 ms per run is an unratified model parameter and no reference machine exists; the deterministic size half is AC-SE-48a.
- **AC-SE-47's former 5 % threshold** is retired: the Controller adopted `BEAM_TOGGLE_MIN_INTERVAL_TICKS` (PC-5), so the debounce is no longer an ask.
- **AC-SE-42 and 28/31/53** need people and builds (a 20-listener panel, the debug console, the Presenter and HUD); they cannot run until those exist.
- **Fixture times** (suited O2-ROOM, FREEZE-BREACH, SHIP-COLD's first gain) depend on the Graph's harness to +/-15 % and are reported, not asserted (OQ-12).
- **The M15 return timing, M16 on a BATTERY `stock` and its unit** are confirmed Graph facts (LS-18, 2026-10-27; OQ-12 (a)-(c) closed) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; AC-SE-7 and 46 assert them directly and AC-SE-52 keeps the synchronous-return variant as a robustness test only.

## Open Questions

Numbering is fixed: OQ-1 to OQ-18 are cited by number in the rules above (Core Rules, Interactions, Cross-References) and keep their numbers; OQ-19 onward are added by the later sections. Items marked **ruled** were decided at the checkpoint or by an orchestrator ruling and stay listed so the owner can reverse them; the rest are open.

### Binding user decisions this GDD implements (not questions)

| # | Decision (the user's words or ruling) | Where it lands | Reversible by |
|---|---|---|---|
| U-1 | Death is lethal including friendly fire ("This is like project zomboid, death is a bitch. It happens.") | SE-23 (an EXOGENOUS hit sets the rung from `integrity_bp` with no hold; `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1), SE-24; OQ-3 | the user only; the knob is a data flip |
| U-2 | MVP death handling = restart from the last autosave (owned by Save/Load) | SE-24 (Suit decides what drives the cap to NONE and recovery while DOWNED, not the restore), SE-34, AC-SE-57; OQ-13 | the user only |
| U-3 | The lamp is chest-mounted and hands-free like Pandorum; the beam origin is the per-facing chest point | SE-8, SE-9, VA-C10 (the housing and the lamp-origin lint); no tool carries light | the user only |
| U-4 | Throwable glowsticks exist (Chemlight `#86B896`) | SE-10 (counted stack, `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT`), VA-R5, the rail glyph | the user only |
| U-5 | Zoom in and out on an integer ladder (render GDD) | Visual/Audio rule 8: overlays and anchors scale by the same integer, no second sprite set; the HUD does not scale with world zoom | the user only |

| OQ-1 | **GASP (ruled CK-1).** A reserve-0 seat in HYPOXIC air keeps drawing room air and is held at CRITICAL rather than killed, because the Graph's own band reads HYPOXIC as "stamina loss" and ANOXIC as "rapid incapacitation". The single departure from the stage ruling R4 and R14. The owner may reverse it by deleting the GASP rows (data and two table rows). | GDD (creative-director may reverse) | playtest |
| OQ-2 | **USER DECISION: does V7 `top_up` join the MVP verb list, and therefore does the lamp cell exist at MVP?** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* Two GDDs disagree until the user rules. This GDD's `TOPUP_MODE` default is VERB (V7 as Interaction's TAP, IA-9, which registers `top_up` at boot iff LS-26 lists V7); the Graph's OQ 20 (owner: user) says "until decided Suit runs `TOPUP_MODE` PASSIVE with `CELL_ENABLED` 0", and V7 is missing from the Graph's LS-26 MVP verb list. The choice decides whether the lamp cell and the Pillar 3 "go dark" lever exist at MVP: the cell and the top-up ritual are the only designed teaching moment for the reserve. **Creative-director recommendation: VERB** — add V7 to LS-26, close the Graph's OQ 20, and pair it with a diegetic departure check (a forgotten top-up shows as a world-state light and is not first discovered 90 s into vacuum; routed to In-World Instruments and the HUD). **Rows each ruling touches.** *VERB:* Graph LS-26 and OQ 20; Interaction IA-9 and its `top_up` row; Suit SE-7 (as shipped), `TOPUP_MODE` VERB, AC-SE-9a-9d; PASSIVE becomes dormant (AC-SE-9e and 79b-79c stay as a tested dormant configuration, or are deleted at the owner's choice). *PASSIVE:* the Graph keeps OQ 20's interim text; Interaction does not register `top_up`; Suit `TOPUP_MODE` PASSIVE and `CELL_ENABLED` 0, which removes the charge reading, the cell whine and mark, `CHARGE_EMPTY`, the BATTERY path and AC-SE-10, 47, 54 and 70, and leaves Pillar 3's lamp cost to Attention alone. **Until ruled** both configurations are specified and correct: the PASSIVE path was unbuildable as first written (D.2 had no credit term for the granted extra, so a room that granted exactly `R_BREATH` debited the reserve by the very amount being topped up) and is fixed in D.2 and D.9. `use_item` stays removed (Interaction AC-IA-1). | **user** (with the producer, the Graph owner and the creative-director) | next consistency pass |

| OQ-19 | **RULED (CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26, option (a)): integrity STRAINED has no body witness, by design, and nothing lethal hides there.** The States table once named "scuffed plates"; the art-director ruled no scuff overlay (scar vocabulary collision, spent overlay budget). The hidden-stat risk was real while the insulation knee sat at `SUIT_HP_OK` (a STRAINED suit silently moved the lethal cold onset, visible only on a lie-capable reading); the knee now sits at `SUIT_LEAK_HP` (SE-21, D.4), so integrity between `SUIT_LEAK_HP` and `SUIT_HP_OK` changes no cap, no insulation, no drain and no onset, and its first consequence is the leak, witnessed by the seam jet on the same run. Design test 1 and anti-pillar guard rail 5 are reworded to exclude it explicitly. Reversal path: option (b), a breath-family audio witness (a seal tick or creak on movement) classed in SE-26, would let the coupling return to `SUIT_HP_OK`. Playtest watches whether players miss a STRAINED cue. Horror Entities owes no STRAINED witness (C-HE-25): option (b) stays the reversal path only. | GDD (art-director; closed by the creative-director) | playtest |

Options: (a) accept it as that rare cue; (b) widen the range and raise the default (about 540 ticks would stagger on DOOR-BREACH's DECOMP entry, which also changes how often a player is input-locked, a Pillar 2 fairness question); (c) drop the external trigger at MVP, leaving the ladder's cap edge as the only stagger. If playtest shows a pressure fall seen through a door should stagger, the refinement is a Lighting read, not a Suit rule. | agent: game-designer; 2D Lighting & Occlusion | playtest |

## Appendix — Registry additions and published ids

*Siblings cite this section. The constants and formulas below are staged as registry candidates (`registry-candidates.yaml` in the stage directory) and are appended to `design/registry/entities.yaml` at Phase 5; this GDD never edits the registry, the index or another GDD. Values are `[PH]` until the pacing spike (Tuning Knobs).*

### A. Constants (the ★ rows of Tuning Knobs, plus the brief Appendix A constants registered under R32)

| Constant | Default | Range | Registry note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `R_BREATH` | 300 mGU per step per crew | 100-600 | **Ownership move:** was a placeholder "owned by Suit & Equipment" in the Graph (lsg:403, lsg:929); Suit is now the source, and the Graph and the Item DB (F-14 ladder) are appended to `referenced_by`; record a was-comment on the new entry |
| `RESERVE_S` | 90 s | 45-240 | derived `RESERVE_MAX_MGU` = `RESERVE_S` × 10 × `R_BREATH` |
| `RESERVE_WARN_S` | 30 s | 20-60 | at most `RESERVE_S` / 2 |
| `CELL_S` | 1200 s of lit beam | 600-2400 | |
| `CELL_WARN_S` | 20 s | 10-30 | at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` |
| `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` | 20 s | 10-30 | at least `W_MIN_WARNING_S` (Suit's own Pillar 2 floor, endogenous paths only) |
| `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` | 600 ticks | 300-900 as a knob range; feasible only at 600 while the other knobs are at their defaults (A2 against A4, D.8 feasible-region table) | at least `SUIT_MIN_WARNING_S` × `TICK_RATE` / 2 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| `INSUL_MC` | 5000 mC | 0-10000 | shifts `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` colder at full integrity (full at and above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, linear below it); no heat shift; the band constants are read by name only |
| `SUIT_HP_FULL` | 10000 bp | not a knob | **equals `HP_FULL` by relation (the Graph's basis-point scale), never a second value**; cited by Interaction's `HARM_TOTAL_BP ≥ SUIT_HP_FULL` (I-6) |
| `SUIT_LEAK_HP` | 5000 bp | 3000-6000 | below it the suit leaks, the seam jet shows and the insulation scaling begins (D.4, SE-21) |
| `SUIT_HP_FAIL` | 2500 bp | 1000-4000 | distinct from the Graph's `HP_FAIL`, never the same symbol |
| `SUIT_HP_OK` | 7000 bp | 5000-9000 | the integrity STRAINED threshold only (an inert rung; the insulation knee moved to `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, CD-GDD-ALIGN 2026-10-26); by relation to the Graph's `HP_OK`, never the symbol |
| `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` | 6000 bp | 5100-6900 | above `SUIT_LEAK_HP`, below `SUIT_HP_OK` (A6); cited by Interaction IA-17 (`patch_integrity` cap); mirrors the Graph's `PATCH_CAP_BP` by relation only |
| `HYPOX_FAIL_S` | 15 s | 10-30 | the breath axis terminal hold for source NONE; `HOLD_23(breath)` = max(`HOLD_12`, `HYPOX_FAIL_S` × `RUNS_PER_S`) |
| `INTEG_FAIL_S` | 10 s | 10-60 | `HOLD_23(integrity)` |
| `STAGGER_WINDOW_TICKS` | 60 ticks | 30-120 | live at MVP; the Controller registers the `SYSTEM` `stagger` type (PC-1 b, OQ-11 answered) |
| `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` | 1 | 0-1 | user decision (death lethal including friendly fire); Interaction states default 1 (IA-31, D.8, Edge Cases E, OQ 3); the creative-director may reverse by data |
| `GLOWSTICK_START_COUNT` | 2 | 0-4, at most `GLOWSTICK_STACK_MAX` | Suit-owned, not an Item DB field |
| `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES` | 3 | 0-3 (`charges_max`) | no refill source |
| `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS` | MEDIUM | MEDIUM, HEAVY | `cue_load` = max(carried, stowed) (D.6) |
| `SUIT_SCHEMA_VERSION` | 1 | not a knob | `schema_version` of `suit.state` |

### B. Formulas (D.1 to D.7 are registry candidates; D.8 to D.11 are local)

| Registry name | Section | One-line expression |
|---|---|---|
| `suit_breath_source` | D.1 | ROOM / RESERVE / GASP / NONE from the Graph's `band_o2` and `band_p` enums and the reserve; a pure function of enums, never of a grant |
| `respiration_request` | D.2 | `R_BREATH` per run to `draw_air` when the source is ROOM or GASP; the reserve debit, the leak and the shortfall reconcile |
| `vitals_ladder_step` | D.3 | per counter axis: instant descent to the demand, one rung per `HOLD_12` / `HOLD_23(axis)` qualifying hold. **CK-17 exception:** both bounds (the floor and the cap) hold for endogenous demand traces; the integrity axis under `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 sets its rung from the threshold band of `integrity_bp` with no hold after an EXOGENOUS apply and is excluded from the cap bound, tested by AC-SE-24 |
| `thermal_load_step` | D.4 | the accumulator over `insul_eff_mc`, `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` and `HEAT_DMG_ONSET_MC` (by name), gain by depth, relax by band, rungs with `TL_HYST` |
| `cell_drain` | D.5 | `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` per run while `emitter.state.lit` as of T-1; `force_off` on the emptying run |
| `held_mass_cue` | D.6 | `cue_load` = max(carried class, stowed class at or above `STOWED_CUE_FLOOR_CLASS`); derived on read, never saved |
| `suit_integrity_damage` | D.7 | burn, arc and hit sources minus the patch, clamped 0..`SUIT_HP_FULL`; a patch never exceeds `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP` |

D.8 (`suit_capacities`), D.9 (`topup_credit`, the V7 apply), D.10 (`suit_cue_levels`), D.11 (`suit_tell_floor`) and D.12 (`pressure_drop_stagger`, added 2026-10-27) are not registry candidates (derived helpers or restatements).

### C. Ids siblings may cite

| Kind | Ids |
|---|---|
| Block | `suit.state` (`SIM`, `WORLD`, `PER_PLAYER` keyed by PlayerId, `SHARED_SUMMARY`, schema 1; replaces the provisional `vitals.state`) |
| Hook | `suit.vitals` (divisor 6, offset 0, `after: [graph.propagate.atmo]`) |
| Command | `equip_tool {slot: 1..6}` (the only command Suit registers); no `use_item` |
| Reasons | `EMPTY_SLOT`, `NOT_ACTIONABLE`, `BUSY` (equip; Suit-namespaced, distinct from Interaction's `BUSY`); `ALREADY_FULL`, `STACK_FULL` (Suit-namespaced, read by Interaction) |
| Class B mutators (SE-40, SE-41) | `add_stowed`, `spend_stowed`, `reserve_stowed`, `release_stowed`, `consume_reserved`, `set_carried`, `release_carried`, `consume_charge`, `credit_reserve`, `credit_cell`, `apply_suit_damage`, `patch_integrity`, `replace_tool`, `revive` (dormant) |
| Reads (SE-40; `view` gained `cue_breath`, `breath_src_change_seq` and `charge_advise` on 2026-10-27) | `tool_in_hand`, `stowed_count`, `carried_instance`, `locomotion_cap`, `charge`, `tool_charge`, `held_mass_g`, `stowed_mass_g`, `can_stow`, `reserve_deficit_mgu`, `cell_deficit_ticks`, `true_value` (keys `suit.o2`, `suit.reserve`, `suit.integrity`, `suit.warmth`, `suit.charge`), `view` |
| Diagnostics (frozen four; the Debug Overlay reads them) | `suit_reserve_shortfall`, `suit_rung_limited{axis}`, `suit_cue_state{player}`, `suit_exo_bypass`; added 2026-10-27, outside the frozen four: `suit_restore_rescaled{from_rev, to_rev}` |
| Restore reasons | `SUIT_RESTORE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`, `SUIT_RESTORE_FIELD_RANGE`, `SUIT_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_DEF`, `SUIT_RESTORE_STOWED_OVERFLOW`, `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`, `SUIT_RESTORE_BEAM_SHAPE`, `SUIT_RESTORE_SEAT_UNBOUND`, `SUIT_RESTORE_DATA_REV_UNKNOWN` *(added 2026-10-27)* |

### D. Pending `referenced_by` lines to clear at Phase 5

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review.)* **Status: cleared.** A grep of `design/registry/entities.yaml` on 2026-10-27 finds zero `# pending referenced_by` comments naming this GDD (the 36 listed at the first revision were appended at Phase 5 and the consistency pass); the heading is kept so the section ids resolve. Registry changes this review needs are returned as registry requests, not edited here: the `respiration_request` expression (the PASSIVE credit term and the breath-only shortfall), the `vitals_ladder_step` bounds note (the counter-axis restriction), the `thermal_load_step` expression (FREEZING relax and the re-entry refractory), `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` (the range is coupled, not free) and, only if OQ-34 is ruled Option A, the retirement of `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES`.

**Review log, 2026-10-27.** Rule identifiers of the form R-n, CK-n and the "orchestrator ruling" tags in the rules above are the stage-ruling and checkpoint ids of the 2026-10-26 authoring run; their records are not in the repository, every rule that cites one carries its reason inline, and no criterion depends on resolving them. Decisions made by this review, in order: (1) D.12 added as the one normative statement of the external-stagger trigger (two ages, inclusive window, crossings never count, persisted); its default window fires on no locked fixture, carried as OQ-38. (2) The PASSIVE top-up path fixed (credit term, breath-only shortfall, `pending_extra_mgu`, restore case) and kept live and correct; the VERB-versus-PASSIVE ruling is the user's (OQ-2). (3) `tool_suppressant` kept as written, reworded from "declined" to USER DECISION with both edit lists (OQ-34). (4) The warmth axis gained a HOLD_12 re-entry refractory (the counter-axis cap bound was false for warmth); FREEZING above the shifted onset now relaxes. (5) Build assertions A5 (leak included), A13 (heat floor from the CRITICAL entry) and A14 (advisory above warning) added, the feasible-region table published, `LEAK_MGU_PER_RUN` narrowed to 0-150, `RUNG_MIN_TICKS` flagged as pinned at 600. (6) D.10 became the single normative cue table (witness and caption columns); `cold_side` gated on the band; `cue_breath` and `breath_src_change_seq` added. (7) `data_rev` and the rescale path added so a legitimate retune does not leave the player without a loadable save; the soft gate stays a USER DECISION (OQ-13). (8) The strip shows the suit O2 while the source is RESERVE; one mark anchor per axis (OQ-41). (9) Accessibility decisions raised for the user now (OQ-40, OQ-37, OQ-20, OQ-36). (10) Onboarding requirement, the silent cell advisory (`CELL_ADVISE_S`) and the naive-player playtest (AC-SE-92). (11) Acceptance criteria split one-test-per-THEN, numbered through AC-SE-92, mapped to story type and gate, with a NOT ASSESSED rule and hash-pinned fixtures. **Not done, by choice:** relocating decision history out of the normative rules across the whole document (too invasive for one review; the per-rule "(orchestrator ruling ...)" notes remain and are non-normative), and moving any registry value.
